# Steps M21-M23: the Rootdeep, the Remembered Isle, and the Glassway I

Issue-ready specs for S-174 to S-205, with the splits S-181a/b, S-190a/b, S-191a/b, S-201a/b, the added vignette step
S-190c and the added close-out S-205b (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Each `### S-###` block can be pasted into an `agent:improvement` issue as it
stands. The planner maps `#### Why / What / Assets / Acceptance criteria / Verification plan / Out of scope / Risk &
rollback` onto the issue's `## ` sections. Bible links are relative to `docs/roadmap/steps/`, so `../bible/x.md#a` is
`docs/roadmap/bible/x.md#a` in the repo.

Order of authority: `docs/roadmap/CANON-NOW.md` LOCKED > `docs/roadmap/CANON.md` > the reconciliation
rows in `docs/roadmap/steps/*.md` (for the topics they name, until the named close-out edits the losing file) > the
bible (`docs/roadmap/bible/*.md`) > the INDEX row > the issue. Where two bible
files disagree, these steps pick one and say which. Every pick is listed in the [reconciliation table](#reconcile)
directly below, and again under [Index changes](#index-changes) at the end. The step that ships a pick also edits the
losing bible file in the same PR, so the bible stays the single source of truth.

**Conventions every step below assumes** (all built by earlier milestones; names are the bible's, and the implementer
greps for the shipped name before coding):

- Realms: `js/realms/index.js` (`REALMS`, `generateRealm`, S-018), `RealmManager.enter(id, spawn, how)` (S-021),
  `G.debug.realm(id, spawn)`, `?realm=<id>`, `hidden: true` until the unhide step. Recipes are
  `js/realms/recipes/<name>.js`, subclasses of `RealmWorld` (S-018). Variants arrive in S-188.
- Per-realm palettes `W.palette` and region tables `W.regions` (S-027). Lazy bundles `assets/realms/<id>/` with
  `realm: '<id>'` manifest tags (S-029). Music data registered with `G.audio.addTrack / addAmbience` (S-028).
- Prints: `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/<id>.json` via `tests/lib/world-print.js`. The realm suite is
  `js/debug/tests/realm.js`, CI job `realm` (S-022), and reads `REALMS[id].views` (4 views, both cameras).
- Conditions and effects: `js/cond.js` (S-025). Flags registry: `js/content/flags.js`. Content lint:
  `tests/content.test.js`. Text tables: `js/text/en/<realm>.js` through `t(id)` (S-032). Dialogue entries:
  `js/content/dialogue/<realm>.js`. Scenes: `js/content/scenes/<realm>.js`, run by `js/scenes.js` (S-034). Quests:
  `js/content/quests/<realm>.js` and `js/quests.js` (S-036). Items: `js/items.js` (S-035). Abilities, gates and
  the solver: `js/abilities.js`, `js/gates.js`, `js/progress-graph.js` (S-048).
- Dungeons: `js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js` compiled by `js/realms/dungeon-compile.js` with its lock-and-key solver
  (S-049), built by `js/realms/recipes/dungeon.js` (S-050), entities in `js/dungeon-mod.js` (S-052, S-053), kit
  props in `js/props-interior.js` (S-051). Hollows: `js/realms/hollows/<id>.js` (S-073).
- Enemies: `ENEMY[type]` data plus `js/ai/<archetype>.js` (S-040 to S-042). Bosses: `js/boss/defs.js` and the move
  runner (S-043 to S-045). Bestiary: `S.bestiary`, `card_<id>` (S-046).
- Travel: `js/travel.js` (benches, chart, travel menu; S-080), the shared Lamplighter kit `kit_*` (S-082), beacons and
  crown lanterns (S-095), the coverage module `js/realms/coverage.js` with `tests/coverage.test.js` (first shipped by
  Duskwick's finds step, S-086).
- Chapter tests: `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>` with `tests/fixtures/saves/<id>-start.json` and
  `tests/fixtures/chapters/<id>.json` (S-030). In-page suites: `node tests/inpage.mjs <suite>`; a new suite is a file
  `js/debug/tests/<name>.js` registered in `js/debug/tests.js` and added to `.github/workflows/ci.yml`'s matrix.
- NPC looks: `NPC_LOOKS[id] = { rig, h, pal, acc }` in `js/content/looks.js`, read by one `npcDef` case per rig
  family (cast.md "Rig families"). Portraits: `portrait_<id>`, 512² webp ≤ 60 KB.
- Generated images: the implementing agent starts a GPT Sol subagent through the T3 orchestrator's `delegate_task`
  and asks it to generate with Codex's `image_gen.imagegen` (or a Gemini model). Every prompt is the house preamble in
  `../bible/art-audio.md#preamble` plus the kind suffix in `../bible/art-audio.md#kinds` plus the subject given here.
  Raw generations are never committed; the prompt and the processing command go into `docs/art/prompts.md`.
- **The invariants block.** Every step's acceptance criteria include the block in
  `../bible/charter.md#invariants-block`, pasted word for word. A step drops only the lines that cannot apply, and
  names the dropped lines. Below, "Invariants: all lines" means paste every line.

<a id="m21"></a>
## M21 · The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart and Lumbra

**Goal.** Build the layer below home completely but keep it hidden: NPC pathfinding and schedules (ENGINE F27), the
`cavern` recipe, Burrowbottom and its people, the Rootdeep's enemies and finds, the Digging Paws on every shipped
land, root-gate fast travel, the Rootdeep Heart (dungeon d6), Lumbra the Long and the Rootdeep's three Hollows. The
realm is unhidden by chapter 13 in M22 (S-191b).

**Entry.** M20 closed: Act II complete (`act2done`, four crown lanterns, the Sunglass owned), the ch12 small-sneeze
scene sets `ch13`, and Wenna's Act II lines and the Catchers' plaza visits are in.

**Exit criteria.**
- `?realm=rootdeep` loads a 576 m cavern in ≤ 1.2 s desktop with frozen prints, five benches, eight gate rooms, the
  Taproot and all M21 finds; the coverage test passes; the realm suite passes its 4 views in both cameras.
- `G.debug.realm('rootdeep_heart')` loads d6 (14 rooms); its solver proves every chest, the boss door and the exit.
  Lumbra's balance test passes (120-180 s, both escalations and the `holes` arena change).
- The Digging Paws dig soft earth on home and on Duskwick, Pinewhistle, Archwater and Threemills. Root-gates open
  from the land side with the Paws, and the travel menu ferries between opened gates.
- No player-visible change for a save that has not reached `act2done`. After `act2done`, the only new visible thing
  is schedules (villagers walking to evening spots). The Rootdeep stays `hidden: true`.

**What the player can do at the end.** Nothing new in story terms until M22 ships, except see villagers walk to
their evening spots. Testers can play the whole Rootdeep, the Heart and Lumbra through debug.

**Playtime added.** 0 h visible; about 3.5 h built (Rootdeep main path 2.0 h with the Heart, exploration about 1.5 h),
shipped with chapter 13.

**Save-version plan.** None. Everything fits existing containers: `S.flags` (`gate:*`, `lumbraCalm`, `seen:*`),
`S.items` (`mitts`), `S.abilities` (`dig`), `S.travel` (`gate:<land>`, `bench:rootdeep:b<n>`), `S.chests`
(`rootdeep:c<n>`, `rootdeep_heart:*`), `S.secrets` (`rootdeep:k<n>`), `S.collect` (acorns, vistas, logs, petals,
Hollows, Dash), `S.dungeons` (`rootdeep_heart`, Hollow puzzle state), `S.bosses` / `S.plushies` / `S.rematches`
(`rootdeep_heart:lumbra`), `S.bestiary`. The save stays at v9.

---

<a id="m22"></a>
## M22 · The Remembered Isle, chapter 13 and Grumble's Undercap

**Goal.** Variant realms and the Remembered Isle, the young cast, the memory knots and the first three memories; chapter
13 played end to end (Digby leads, Gran Burrows, the Heart, Lumbra, memories 1-3, the roots show the stone sites);
the Rootdeep unhidden; three Rootdeep side stories; the first optional home dungeon (Grumble's Undercap) and ss-11; perf
checkpoint 5; batch 10 of art briefs. Steps S-188 … S-197 (splits S-190a/b, S-191a/b; added S-190c).

**Entry.** M21 closed: the Rootdeep, the Heart, Lumbra, the Paws, root-gates and schedules are built behind `hidden`.

**Exit criteria.**
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch13` passes from `ch13-start` to `ch13-end` (`lumbraCalm`, `mem:1-3`, `stoneSites`,
  `ability:dig`, `mq-ch14` and `mq-remember` active, gates open on home and the four isles); the chapter chain from New
  Game through ch13 passes.
- `rootdeep`, `rootdeep_heart`, `hollow_rootdeep_1..3`, `remembered`, `burrowbottom_homes` and `undercap` are unhidden,
  their prints frozen, the realm suite green in both cameras.
- ss-01 (part 2), ss-11, ss-31, ss-32, ss-33 complete in-page with their world marks within 10 s.
- Checkpoint 5: `full-100` < 80 KB; the 20-cycle soak passes; every view in budget or a bug step filed.

**What the player can do at the end.** Answer Wenna's request: open the root-gate under the Meadow Windmill, follow Digby
by his voice through the dark, meet his Gran, solve the Rootdeep Heart, win the Digging Paws, calm Lumbra the Long and
touch three memory knots to watch the Lamplighters a hundred years ago; see three veins of light point to where the
Stair Stones fell; ride Lumbra between root-gates; relight Burrowbottom, scratch Lumbra's itch, carry Gran's new pouch to
Digby and plant his golden grove; dig into Grumble's Undercap and relight the shroomlets' town; dig up Shellby's old hat
and four other home finds.

**Playtime added.** +6 h visible (cumulative 46.5 h): chapter 13 with the Heart 3.5 h, memories 1-3 0.4 h, Rootdeep
exploration 1.5 h (built in M21), side stories 31-33 and ss-01 part 2 0.9 h, the Undercap and ss-11 1.0 h (the M21
build is counted here because it ships with chapter 13).

**Save-version plan.** None: the save stays **v9**. New state uses `flags` (`stoneSites`, `mem:<n>`, `ch13.*`, `ss31.*`,
`ss32.*`, `ss33.*`, `ss11.*`, `undercapLit`, `gate:home:*`), `items` (`pouch_spring2`, `shellby_hat`), `quests`
(`mq-ch13`, `mq-remember`, `ss-11`, `ss-31`-`ss-33`), `collect` (acorns `remembered:a1..a4`), `chests`, `dungeons`
(`undercap`).

---

<a id="m23"></a>
## M23 · The Glassway I: sailing and Puffin Post

**Goal.** Free sailing on the *Dandelion* (owner decision 4) with the chart-stop fallback built in, the save's boat
place (v10), the `shoals` recipe with its two-state tide, the Glassway's props, art and music, chapter 14 with Puffin
Post's people, its two species and its finds. Steps S-198 … S-205 (splits S-201a/b, the added close-out S-205b).

**Entry.** M22 closed; owner decision 4 at its default (free sailing).

**Exit criteria.**
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch14` and `ch14-chart` pass; the chapter chain through ch14 passes.
- Save v10: migrations from v2-v9 pass; the v6 fixture Continues; a save aboard Continues aboard.
- `glassway` unhidden with frozen prints for both tides; the boat graph reaches every dock at both tides; coverage passes
  at both tides; the realm suite and the `boat` and `glassway` suites green.
- `glassway` bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; core unchanged except `card_sail_reef` and the two Act III portraits recorded.

**What the player can do at the end.** Ask Nell to sail beyond the reef; steer the *Dandelion* freely over glass-clear
water between nine islets (or hop between their piers by chart if the fallback shipped); ring tide stones to walk the
sandbars at low tide; meet Postmistress Pell at her stilt post office and Mr Whelk in his teapot; pick up a mole's
hundred-year-old letter on the beach and three more bottles across the islets; visit Kinako's bazaar; climb Gull Rock to
see the whole Wide Blue; stand on a sleeping whale; find the Glassway's chests, keepsakes, acorns, vistas and Lamp Logs;
open its root-gate; learn that the red buoy leads to Kettle Isle and the white one to Saltglass (both still in fog).

**Playtime added.** +2 h (cumulative 48.5 h): chapter 14 1.0 h, the Glassway's exploration and finds about 1.0 h (the
Bell, the Mabel and the Glassway stories follow in M24-M26).

**Save-version plan.** **v9 → v10 in S-199** (`MIGRATIONS[9]` adds `boat { realm, x, z, heading }`; systems#save-v10).
Everything else uses existing containers: `flags` (`ch14`, `ch14.*`, `tide:glassway`, `boatAboard`), `quests`
(`mq-ch14`), `collect` (bottles `glassway:4/7/8/10`, logs 22-24, acorns, vistas), `chests`, `secrets`, `travel`
(`gate:glassway`, `bench:glassway:b1..b5`), `bestiary`.

<a id="s-174"></a>
### S-174 · feat: NPCs walk paths and keep simple schedules
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-018, S-030 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `?noschedules` / `G.debug.schedules(false)` (off switch) · Bible: [systems#schedules](../bible/systems.md#schedules), [cast#spots-and-schedules](../bible/cast.md#spots-and-schedules), [atlas-home#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)

#### Why
M21 (the Rootdeep) needs Digby to lead Pip through the dark in chapter 13, and Burrowbottom, Puffin Post and later
towns need people who go home at night. This is ENGINE F27: A* pathfinding on the walk grid plus data-driven
schedules. It also delivers the first visible piece of the vision item **The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart and
Lumbra**: after Wenna wakes, the six villagers keep their shipped spots by day and walk to evening spots at dusk, so
the village feels lived in.

#### What
1. **`js/pathfind.js`** (new, pure, no Three.js; `bun test` imports it):
   - `findPath(W, ax, az, bx, bz, opts = {}) → [{x, z}, …] | null`. A* over `W.walk` on 1 m cells with the same cell
     indexing as `World.bfs` / `isReached` in `js/world.js`. Eight neighbours, costs 1 and √2. A diagonal step is
     allowed only when both orthogonal neighbours are walkable (no corner cutting). Octile heuristic. Binary-heap
     open set on a typed array; closed set as a `Uint8Array(W.n * W.n)` reused between queries (one scratch buffer
     per World, freed on `onWorld`).
   - `opts.maxExpand` (default 60,000; return `null` past it), `opts.radius` (default 0.38 m: refuse cells closer
     than this to a solid, using the walk grid only), `opts.goalR` (default 0.6 m: any cell within it ends the search).
   - String-pulling: walk the raw cell path and keep a corner only when the straight segment from the last kept point
     to the next point crosses a non-walkable cell (supercover line over `W.walk`). Output points are cell centres,
     rounded to 0.01 m.
   - Start or goal on a non-walkable cell: snap to the nearest walkable cell within 3 m (ring scan); else `null`.
   - `class PathQueue { request(id, ax, az, bx, bz, cb), step(W) }`: runs **at most one** `findPath` per call. The
     runtime calls `step` once per frame. A request with the same id replaces the queued one.
2. **`js/schedule.js`** (new, pure):
   - `SCHEDULES` table keyed by NPC id: `[[fromHour, spotId, activity], …]` sorted by hour, wrapping at 24.
     Activities: `stand`, `wander:<r>`, `sit`, `work`, `watch`, `doze`, `sleep`, `indoors`, `walk`.
   - `rowAt(npcId, hour) → { spot, activity }`.
   - `targetFor(npcId, S, W, ctx) → { x, z, activity, perch? } | null` applies the rules in this order:
     (1) `ctx.off` (tests, `?noschedules`) → the day spot. (2) **Quest pin:** an active quest step with
     `target.npc === id` → its `pin` spot, or the NPC's current spot. (3) **LOCKED villagers before `wennaAwake`** →
     their shipped spot at every hour. (4) **Protected day:** for `shellby, ribbit, miso, clover, hoot, digby`,
     06:00-19:48 (`S.tod` fractions 0.25-0.825) → the shipped spot, whatever the table says. (5) The table row. A
     spot id that does not resolve in this realm → the day spot (nothing missable).
   - `SPOT_ALIAS`: cast.md's spot ids → `W.npcSpots` keys, so content uses cast.md's names (R-note: atlas-home.md
     coordinates win; cast.md ids are aliases).
3. **Data shipped in this step** (`js/content/schedules.js`), exactly the six LOCKED villagers' rows from cast.md:

   | NPC | Rows (after `wennaAwake`) | Evening spot (new `placeSpots` entry, pass row 6 `new RNG(7151 * 83 + 29)`, appended after the existing rows) |
   |---|---|---|
   | `shellby` | `[[7,'home.shellby_altar','stand'],[18,'home.shellby_bench','sit'],[22,'home.shellby_bench','doze']]` | `home:spot-shellby-bench`: on Shellby's Bench from S-082 (atlas-home.md#benches), seat point 0.4 m in front of the backrest; `home.shellby_altar` = his shipped spot (−3.8, 6.4) |
   | `ribbit` | `[[7,'home.ribbit_plaza','wander:4'],[19,'home.ribbit_pad','sit'],[23,'home.ribbit_pad','sleep']]` | `home:spot-ribbit-pad`: a **perch** at the pond centre (−13, 25): he paths to the nearest walkable bank cell within 4 m, then hops 0.4 s onto the lily pad (y = pond surface + 0.05) |
   | `miso` | `[[7,'home.miso_counter','work'],[20,'home.miso_backroom','indoors'],[23,'home.miso_backroom','sleep']]` | `indoors` before the back room ships (M27) = she stays behind the counter (shipped spot), the night bell rule unchanged |
   | `clover` | `[[8,'home.clover_path','wander:6'],[17,'home.thistledown_door','sit'],[20,'home.thistledown_door','indoors']]` | `home.thistledown_door` = `near(cottage 5 door + 1.5 m out, 0, 2)` (new entry `home:spot-thistle-door`) |
   | `hoot` | `[[8,'home.hoot_woods','work'],[16,'home.hoot_board','stand'],[21,'home.hoot_cairn','watch'],[2,'home.hoot_study','indoors']]` | `home:spot-hoot-board` = 1.5 m in front of Hoot's Board (S-076); `home:spot-hoot-cairn` = `near((94, −44), 0, 4)`; `home.hoot_study` before S-110's door exists = the board spot |
   | `digby` | `[[7,'home.digby_mill','work'],[19,'home.digby_burrow','indoors'],[22,'home.for_spring','work'],[23,'home.digby_burrow','sleep']]` | `home:spot-digby-burrow` = `near(windmill + 5 m on bearing 200°, 0, 3)`; `home.for_spring` = `near((−85, 10), 0, 6)` |

   The protected-day rule keeps every shipped hint, test and `test:playthrough` true: the six villagers stand on their
   shipped spots from 06:00 to 19:48 whatever happens.
4. **Runtime** (`js/npcs.js` + a small module `js/schedule-mod.js` registered in `js/modules.js` `MODULES`):
   - Every 2 s of game time, each NPC with a schedule asks `targetFor`. When the target changes, it requests a path
     through the `PathQueue`. Off screen (more than 60 m from Pip, or the NPC's realm is not loaded) the NPC is
     **placed** at its target with no walking. Indoors: walk to the door spot, fade the model out over 0.3 s, and
     hide it until the next row (the interior realm places its own copy when it exists).
   - `NPC.walkPath(points, speed)`: a new movement mode beside `follow` and `homing`. Speed 1.6 m/s (Grandpa Dormy
     and Granny Mochi 1.0, kits 2.2, from `NPC_LOOKS[id].speed` when present). The NPC turns with
     `dampAngle` at 6 rad/s, moves with `moveCircle` (so dynamic colliders still push), and re-requests a path if it
     has not advanced 0.5 m in 2 s (stuck). Arriving within 0.5 m starts the row's activity; `wander:<r>` becomes
     the shipped wander with `def.wander = r` round the spot.
   - Talking stops the walk (`talkStart`); `talkEnd` resumes it. The shipped follow mode (the parade) takes priority.
   - The look-at and wave behaviour (WP5) is unchanged while walking.
5. **Debug and test switches:** `G.debug.schedules(on)`, the URL flag `?noschedules`, and
   `G.debug.npc.path(id) → points` (the current path, for tests). `tests/chapter.mjs` and `tests/playthrough.mjs` pass
   `?noschedules` (systems.md rule 5).
6. **Docs:** a short "NPC schedules" paragraph in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (the queue, the rules order, off-screen
   placement).

#### Assets
None.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `findPath` returns the shortest 8-neighbour path (within 1 % of the optimal cost) on every fixture in
  `tests/fixtures/paths/*.json`, never cuts a corner, returns `null` when the goal is walled off, and finishes a
  worst-case 360 × 360 home query in under 20 ms in `bun test` (logged).
- `PathQueue.step` runs at most one search per call (asserted with a spy).
- `targetFor` applies the five rules in order; a unit test covers each (quest pin beats protected day; protected day
  beats the table; `?noschedules` beats everything; an unresolved spot gives the day spot).
- With a v9 save after `wennaAwake` at 19:00, Shellby walks from (−3.8, 6.4) to his bench and sits within 40 s of game
  time (in-page); at 07:00 he walks back.
- Before `wennaAwake` no villager moves from its shipped spot at any hour (in-page, with an Act I fixture, at 06:00,
  12:00, 18:00 and 23:00).
- `test:playthrough` and every `wp*` suite are unchanged.
- The new `placeSpots` entries change nothing else on seeds 1-40 (`world.test.js`, the pass-off comparison).
- Invariants: all lines except the precache line.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/pathfind.test.js` (new): 8 fixtures in `tests/fixtures/paths/`: open field, corridor with an S bend,
  a door gap 1 m wide, a diagonal squeeze that must not cut the corner, a walled-off goal (null), start inside a
  solid (snap), the home village from the plaza to Shellby's bench (on the real seed-7151 World), and a timing case.
- `bun test tests/schedule.test.js` (new): the rules table, alias resolution, wrap at 24.
- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (seeds 1-40, new spots).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs npc` (new suite `js/debug/tests/npc.js`, added to the CI matrix): "Shellby reaches his
  evening bench", "Digby goes indoors at 19:00 and is hidden", "no LOCKED villager moves before wennaAwake",
  "talking pauses a walk".
- `bun run test:playthrough`, `node tests/inpage.mjs wp5`.

#### Out of scope
- Schedules for the other home cast (each household step adds its own rows), Burrowbottom people (S-178), Digby's
  ch13 escort (S-191a uses this system), plaza visitors' arrival walks (data only, later steps).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: an NPC walks off its shipped spot during Act I and breaks a playthrough check; the protected-day rule and the
before-`wennaAwake` rule prevent it, and the in-page suite checks it. Rollback: revert the PR; `?noschedules` also
freezes every NPC at its day spot in a live build.

<a id="s-175"></a>
### S-175 · feat: the Rootdeep cavern recipe: walls, low halls and the underlake (hidden)
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-096, S-050 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.rootdeep.hidden = true` · Bible: [atlas-far#rootdeep](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep) (Card, Shape and recipe, Regions, Arrival), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes)

#### Why
The Rootdeep is Act III's first land and the layer under every isle (vision item **The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart
and Lumbra**). It is a new kind of realm, a cavern: carved out of solid rock, with low halls under ceilings and
great halls lit only by glow. This step builds its ground and regions so every later M21 step has a frozen World to
place things in.

#### What
1. **Registry** (`js/realms/index.js`):
   ```js
   rootdeep: { id: 'rootdeep', name: 'The Rootdeep', kind: 'cavern', size: 576, seed: 40241, recipe: 'cavern',
     palette: 'rootdeep', music: 'rootdeep', ambience: 'rootdeep', hidden: true, open: 'flag:act2done',
     arrive: 'gate-home', tod: null, sky: false, bundle: 'rootdeep', enemies: 'rootdeep',
     benches: 5, rootgate: true, dash: true, views: [ /* below */ ] }
   ```
   `sky: false` hides the sky dome, the sun, clouds, horizon cards and every `sky.*` overlay (the Ladder billboard,
   Starstair flights) in this realm. `tod: null` means the global clock keeps running (schedules stay true), but the
   lighting is fixed (palette `skyFixed: 0`).
2. **Recipe `js/realms/recipes/cavern.js`** (`class CavernWorld extends RealmWorld`), stage order:
   `layout → rock → carve → floors → lake → causeways → spurs → ceilings → regions → scatter → trails → reach → splat
   → colliders → anchors`. All stage RNG streams are `new RNG(seed * k + c)` with the home constants (7·3 main, 13·5,
   17·3, 31·17) so later post-passes can use 71·3 and up (atlas-far.md#far-passes).
   - **rock:** every cell `H = 30`, not walkable.
   - **carve:** rasterise these shapes into a carve mask with a floor height per cell (numbers from
     atlas-far.md#rootdeep-shape, copied here):

     | Piece | Shape | Floor |
     |---|---|---|
     | Underlake Hall | disc (0, 0) r 110 | shore ring r 72-110: y 1-4 (value noise amp 1.5, wavelength 40 m) |
     | The lake | disc (0, 0) r 72 | bed y −3, water surface y 0 (deep: not walkable) |
     | Taproot island | disc (0, 0) r 20 | y 1.5 |
     | Causeways (3) | strips 5 m wide from r 20 to r 72 at bearings 0°, 90°, 270° | top y 1.2 |
     | The Drip Gate | the shore ring between bearings 172° and 188° (a 20 m-wide spillway), r 72-110 | water y −0.4 over bed −3 (deep) |
     | Stepping roots | six 1.6 m-long walkable root tops across the Drip Gate on bearing 180° at 2.6 m spacing, tops y 0.6, joined by 1.0 m root "knuckles" at y 0.3 so the walk grid sees one path | |
     | Burrowbottom | ellipse (−165, −5), rx 85, rz 70 | terraces: upper (z < −25) y 9, middle y 6, lower (z > 15) y 3; ramps 4 m wide at x = −150 and x = −190 |
     | Burrow Tunnel | capsule (−110, 0) → (−82, 0), width 10 | interpolated 3 → 2 |
     | Windmill Stair | capsule (−132, −44) → (−118, −58), width 4 | steps rising 9 → 14 (0.3 m per step) |
     | Glowcap Forest | annular sector bearings 295°-65°, r 105-250 | y 2-14 (two octaves, amp 6, wavelengths 60 m and 18 m); three flat glades r 14 at (−60, −160), (60, −170), (0, −228) |
     | Geode Galleries | G1 (147, −85) r 40 y 4 · G2 (140, 0) r 48 y 2 · G3 (220, −10) r 36 y 6 · G4 (147, 85) r 40 y 3 · G5 (205, 125) r 34 y 5 | flat ± 0.4 |
     | Gallery tunnels | width 8: shore (110, 0) → G2; G2 → G1; G2 → G3; G2 → G4; G4 → G5 | interpolated |
     | The G3 crevasse | a 7 m-wide trench at x = 228 across G3 north-south, floor 4 m below G3, ramp out at its north end | |
     | Drip Bay | channel from the Drip Gate south to (0, 240), 28 m wide (water y −0.4, deep), banks 22 m wide either side (y 0.5-3); grottos r 15 at (−40, 170), (40, 190), (−40, 222) | |
     | Spurs (8) | tunnels 8 m wide ending in a gate room (disc r 8) at the spur end; floors rising 0-6 m | branch points and ends below |

     Spurs (end = `gate-<land>` spawn): home (−118, −58) is the Windmill Stair landing itself (no spur); Pinewhistle
     from glade A to (−100, −242); Kettle from G3 to (231, −96); Archwater from G5 to (231, 96); Duskwick from the
     Drip Bay east bank (25, 200) to (96, 231); Glassway from the Drip Bay's south end to (0, 250); Saltglass from the
     west bank (−25, 210) to (−96, 231); Threemills from Burrowbottom's lower terrace (−200, 40) to (−231, 96).
   - **walls:** cells within 4 m outside the carve blend from the floor height up to 30 at 60-80° (not walkable).
   - **ceilings** (`over: true` box colliders, the shipped mechanism for overhead parts): Burrowbottom y 13 over the
     whole ellipse **except** a dome of r 12 m round (−165, −5), where the ceiling is y 20 (R13, so the 11 m Great
     Burrow Lamp fits); the Burrow Tunnel y 8; the Windmill Stair 3 m above each step; gallery tunnels 7 m above
     their floor; spurs 6 m above their floor. Great halls (Underlake, Glowcap, G1-G5) have no ceiling collider; a
     dark roof card at y 34 is drawn over them (S-176 dresses it).
   - **water plane:** `max(1000, 576 + 600)` square, deep `#10202a`, shallow `#2f6f6a`, foam `#9fe8d6`; no tide.
   - **regions** (REGIONS-shaped rows, cards exactly as below):

     | id | Card line 1 / line 2 | Extent | `max` | Spawn table (copied from creatures.md#spawn-tables) |
     |---|---|---|---|---|
     | `burrowbottom` | Burrowbottom / Mind your head | the ellipse, the Burrow Tunnel, the Windmill Stair, the Threemills spur | 0 | none (town) |
     | `glowcap` | Glowcap Forest / Mushrooms the size of houses | the Glowcap sector, the Pinewhistle spur | 6 | `glow_grub 4, drip_bat 3` (rows inert until S-179 defines the species) |
     | `underlake` | The Underlake / Very quiet water | r < 110, Drip Bay, the Duskwick, Glassway and Saltglass spurs | 6 | `drip_bat 3, glow_grub 2, dimling 2` |
     | `geode` | Geode Galleries / Sparkly | G1-G5, their tunnels, the Kettle and Archwater spurs | 6 | `drip_bat 3, glow_grub 2` |

     Until S-179 the spawn tables are `[]` with the final rows in a comment, so nothing spawns. The town rule (no spawn
     within 12 m of Burrowbottom's edge) is asserted by S-179.
   - **reach:** BFS from `gate-home`; `W.unreachable` must be empty (every carved floor cell is reachable on foot).
   - **anchors and spawns:** `gate-home` (−118, −58) facing 225°; `taproot` (0, −22) facing 180°; `heart` (0, 13)
     facing 180°; `gate-<land>` at each spur end facing back down the spur. Named anchors `w:taproot` (0, 0),
     `w:taproot.face` (0, −9) y 6, `w:heartDoor` (0, 9.5), `w:burrowLamp` (−165, −5) y 9, `w:gran` (−180, −32).
3. **Palette `rootdeep`** (`js/realms/palettes.js` or wherever S-027 keeps them), from art-audio.md#palettes:
   swatch `#1c1a24 #ffcf7a #7be0c8 #8a5a3c`; `skyFixed: 0`, all sky rows `#14121b`/`#241f2e`; `hemi: ['#7be0c8',
   '#8a5a3c', 1.1]`; no clouds; fog per region `burrowbottom [12, 90, #3a2f3a, .35]`, `glowcap [10, 80, #2e4a46, .45]`,
   `underlake [14, 90, #1f3340, .40]`, `geode [10, 70, #3a2a48, .35]`, `arena [10, 70]`; landmark fog cap 0.45;
   terrain layers `rootdeep_loam, rootdeep_mycel, rootdeep_glowmoss, rootdeep_lakesand, rootdeep_geode,
   rootdeep_rootfloor, rootdeep_cavestone`, cliff `rootdeep_rootwall` (all fall back to the home layer in the same slot
   until S-177). Splat rules: shore and lake bed → lakesand; terrace floors → rootfloor; Glowcap → mycel with
   glowmoss in glades; galleries → geode; walls → cavestone; tunnels → loam.
4. **Views** (`REALMS.rootdeep.views`, yaw as a compass bearing in degrees converted with the helper S-022 uses):
   densest `{x: -60, z: -150, yaw: 120, pitch: -4}` (glade A toward the Grandmother Cap); landmark `{x: 0, z: -60,
   yaw: 180, pitch: 12}` (north causeway toward the Taproot); town `{x: -150, z: 14, yaw: 270, pitch: 0}`
   (Burrowbottom toward the Great Burrow Lamp); widest `{x: 125, z: -20, yaw: 100, pitch: 2}` (G2 toward the Sun
   Geode).
5. **Performance:** chunks whose cells are all rock and are not within 6 m of a carved cell are not built (no mesh, no
   draw). `W.genMs` is logged and must be ≤ 1.2 s.
6. **Prints:** write `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/rootdeep.json` (placement print, FNV of `H` and `reg`, land m²,
   reachable m², walk count, the expected coverage land-cell count). S-176 to S-180 may refresh it; after S-191b it
   is frozen.

#### Assets
None in this step (terrain textures fall back to the home layers; S-177 generates the real ones).

#### Acceptance criteria
- `generateRealm('rootdeep')` is deterministic (two runs deep-equal) and equals the fixture.
- Land area is 0.14-0.16 km²; `W.unreachable` is empty; every spawn and named anchor is on walkable ground.
- Every `over` ceiling sits at the listed height; Burrowbottom's dome round the lamp is at y 20 (unit test samples
  the ceiling at (−165, −5) and (−200, −5)).
- `W.genMs` ≤ 1.2 s desktop (printed by the test); the realm suite enters and leaves `rootdeep` with no leak and the
  4 views are within budget in both cameras (main ≤ 200 calls, total ≤ 300, ≤ 1.3 M tris).
- The sky dome, horizon cards and the Ladder billboard are not drawn in `rootdeep` (in-page assertion).
- `REALMS.rootdeep.hidden === true`; no portal leads here yet; the chart shows nothing new.
- Invariants: all lines except the precache line (no assets).

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js` (new): determinism, prints, land area, reachability, ceilings, spawn
  walkability, region extents (sample points per region), stepping-root path walkable.
- `bun test` (whole suite, including `tests/world.test.js` home prints unchanged).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the realm suite picks up `rootdeep` from `REALMS`).

#### Out of scope
- Props, the Taproot and glow (S-176), textures, cards and music (S-177), people (S-178), enemies (S-179), finds and
  coverage (S-180).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a 576 m all-rock grid builds more terrain than home; the rock-chunk skip keeps the mesh to the carved area
(about 45 % of the chunks) and the realm suite measures it. Risk: the stepping-root knuckles make the Drip Gate look
like a bridge; S-176's root logs dress them. Rollback: revert; the realm is hidden, so players see nothing.

<a id="s-176"></a>
### S-176 · feat: Rootdeep props: the Taproot, glowcaps, geodes and root walls
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-175 · Size: M (about 480 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [atlas-far#rootdeep-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-kit), [atlas-far#rootdeep-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-states)

#### Why
A cavern with no props is a grey bowl. The Rootdeep's look (the 60 m Taproot, house-sized glowing mushrooms, geodes,
root walls) is what makes Act III feel like a new layer of the world, and in first person its glowing landmarks are
how the player navigates (charter first-person rule 1).

#### What
1. **`js/props-rootdeep.js`** (new, code-built toon models through `js/toon.js`, the same `GB` builder style as
   `js/props.js`). One builder per key, sizes and colours from atlas-far.md#rootdeep-kit:

   | Key | Kind | Build (code) | Tris | Notes |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `rootdeep_taproot` | single mesh | 24-sided tapered cylinder r 6 → r 4 at 60 m, six box buttresses r 9 flare, emissive spiral grain strip `#ffcf7a` on bark `#8a5a3c`, buttress moss `#7be0c8` | 1,800 | grain glow breathes ±10 % over 6 s; **12 knot sockets** at bearing 15° + 30°·(n−1), r 6.6, y 2.2, each an inert `rootdeep_knot` (state `dark`) |
   | `rootdeep_knot` | instanced (12) | flattened sphere 0.9 m with an emissive spiral decal | 80 | three states via a per-instance colour: dark `#4a3a2a`, pulse (group colour), steady (paler, smaller) |
   | `rootdeep_glowcap` | instanced | hemisphere cap `#2f6b66` with emissive freckles and gills `#7be0c8` on a stalk `#e8dcc8`; scales 3 / 7 / 12 m | 260 | cap sway 1° over 5 s; collider on the stalk only (r 0.15 × height) |
   | `rootdeep_grandmother_cap` | single | a scaled glowcap 22 m, cap 18 m wide `#245a57`, a shelf fungus deck at 9 m `#c9a46b` (a walkable bridge deck, `deck` record) | 900 | the landmark at (0, −180) |
   | `rootdeep_sun_geode` | single | upright split geode 14 m, shell `#4a3c48`, emissive gold crystals `#ffcf7a` / `#fff1b8` | 1,100 | landmark at (140, 0); inner glow pulses over 8 s |
   | `rootdeep_burrow_lamp` | single | half-torus root arch 11 m and a 3 m brass cage lantern `#b8894a` with emissive panes `#ffcf7a` | 700 | landmark at (−165, −5); sways 2° over 7 s |
   | `rootdeep_root_arch` | instanced | half torus, tube r 0.8, span 9 m, emissive vein | 220 | at every tunnel mouth (one per tunnel end) |
   | `rootdeep_root_wall` | instanced | three crossed cylinders on a 6 × 8 m box panel, `#6b4a32` / `#8a5a3c`, moss `#7be0c8` | 180 | stamped along carve seams every 10-14 m (own RNG `seed * 71 + 3` is **not** used here: use the recipe's scatter stream so it is part of prints) |
   | `rootdeep_geode` | instanced | half icosphere with inward cones, shell `#5a4a5e`, crystals `#b8a0ff` / `#7be0c8` with a fresnel rim | 300 | 1 / 2.5 / 5 m; Galleries only |
   | `rootdeep_stalactite` | instanced | cone 1-4 m `#5a4a5e` with a glowing tip bead `#7be0c8` | 60 | under every ceiling, density 1 per 60 m² of ceiling |
   | `rootdeep_root_log` | instanced | capsule 1.2 × 4 m `#8a5a3c` with moss | 80 | causeway edging (both sides, every 4 m) and on the stepping roots |
   | `rootdeep_glowmoss` | instanced quad | 4 × 4 m decal, canvas radial teal blob fallback | 2 | floors and walls; breathes ±15 % over 9 s |
   | `rootdeep_vein` | instanced strip | 1 × 6 m additive strip, `#ffcf7a` | 2 | **off** in this step (the `stoneSites` beat turns them on in S-191b) |
   | `rootdeep_stall`, `rootdeep_burrow_door`, `rootdeep_lamp_post` | instanced | built here (briefs in atlas-far.md), **placed** in S-178 | 240 / 160 / 90 | the lamp post has two states (dim `#4a3c48`, lit `#ffcf7a`) |

   Instanced types used by the Rootdeep after this step: 13 of the 24 allowed (the shared kit adds more in S-180).
   New material programs: glow bark, glow cap, geode crystal, moss decal, lamp glass, wet rock, vein strip: 7 of 12.
   Glow uses `glow` / `nightGlow` toon inputs only: **no new lights** (the shipped 4-light pool picks the nearest
   lamps; glowcaps and the Burrow Lamp register as lamps in `W.lamps`).
2. **Scatter** (a `scatterTables` override in `cavern.js`, inside the recipe so it is part of prints): glowcaps in
   Glowcap Forest (1 per 180 m², 60 % small, 30 % medium, 10 % large, none within 6 m of a glade centre or a path);
   small glowcaps along the Underlake shore (1 per 400 m²); geodes in the Galleries (1 per 150 m²); stalactites under
   ceilings; root walls on seams; glowmoss decals everywhere carved (1 per 90 m²).
3. **Roof card:** one large dark quad at y 34 over each great hall (Underlake disc, Glowcap sector, each Gallery),
   colour `#14121b` with glow-root decal dots, `depthWrite` on, no collider. In TP the camera never rises above
   y 32 in these halls (the arm probe caps at the roof).
4. **States** (read at runtime from `S.flags`, no prints change): before `lumbraCalm` every glow-bark material and
   the Taproot grain run at **0.35** of full (atlas-far.md#rootdeep-states, art-audio.md 5.5 "Rootdeep dim");
   `G.debug.flags.set('lumbraCalm', true)` ramps them to 1.0 over 8 s, outward from (0, 9.5). The knots stay dark
   until S-190.
5. **Landmark fog cap** 0.45 for the Taproot, the Grandmother Cap, the Sun Geode and the Burrow Lamp.
6. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/rootdeep.json`.

#### Assets
All code-built (keys in the table, `realm: 'rootdeep'` on every manifest slot, added as commented-out `models`
entries so the owner can drop a GLB in later). One ART BRIEF per new model in the PR body, copied from
atlas-far.md#rootdeep-kit (`rootdeep_taproot`, `rootdeep_knot`, `rootdeep_glowcap`, `rootdeep_grandmother_cap`,
`rootdeep_root_arch`, `rootdeep_root_wall`, `rootdeep_geode`, `rootdeep_sun_geode`, `rootdeep_burrow_lamp`,
`rootdeep_burrow_door`, `rootdeep_stall`, `rootdeep_lamp_post`, `rootdeep_stalactite`, `rootdeep_root_log`,
`rootdeep_glowmoss`, `rootdeep_vein`), each with silhouette, size, palette, materials, tris (code / GLB), pose,
image prompt, 3D prompt and fallback.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every listed key builds with no asset loaded (`?noassets=all`) and renders in the realm suite.
- The four landmarks stand at their anchors (±0.5 m) and are visible through fog from every region entry point listed
  in S-175's region table (in-page ray test against `W.H` and colliders from 1.6 m above each entry point).
- No new point lights; the material-program count added by the realm is ≤ 12 (logged by the realm suite warm-up).
- The 4 views stay within budget in both cameras (record the numbers in the PR).
- Dim state at 0.35 before `lumbraCalm`; full after the ramp (in-page reads the emissive multiplier).
- Prints refreshed; home prints byte-identical.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js` (prints, landmark anchors, 12 knot sockets at the listed bearings).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (views, leak, programs).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs rootdeep` (new suite `js/debug/tests/rootdeep.js`, CI matrix entry): "landmarks visible from
  entries", "dim before lumbraCalm, bright after".
- `bun run test:smoke` with `?noassets=all` and `?realm=rootdeep&debug=1`.

#### Out of scope
- Terrain textures, loading card, map, music (S-177); burrow doors, stalls and lamp posts placed (S-178); knot states
  and memories (S-190); veins lighting (S-191b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the 60 m Taproot and roof cards cost draw calls in the Underlake view; each is one mesh, and the stalactite
density can drop to 1 per 90 m² if the view is over. Rollback: revert; the realm is hidden.

<a id="s-177"></a>
### S-177 · art: Rootdeep look, loading card, map art and music
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-175, S-029b · Size: M (about 260 lines of code and data, plus generated images) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [art-audio#rootdeep](../bible/art-audio.md#rootdeep), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data), [atlas-far#rootdeep-music](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-music)

#### Why
The Rootdeep must read as warm and glowing, never black or grey (charter A6). Painted terrain layers, the loading card,
the map and its own music turn the cavern recipe into a place, and they go into a lazy realm bundle so the core
precache does not grow.

#### What
1. **Generate seven terrain layers** (kind suffix `tile` from art-audio.md#kinds; preamble + suffix + subject),
   1024² seamless, then `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <raw> <out> --blend 64`, exported as `_hd` 1024² and `_lo`
   512² webp q82 under `assets/realms/rootdeep/terrain/`:

   | Key | Slot | Subject (verbatim from art-audio.md#rootdeep) |
   |---|---|---|
   | `rootdeep_loam` | grass | "Dark soft cave loam with tiny glowing amber specks. Palette: #2a2230 #3a2f3a #ffcf7a #8a5a3c." |
   | `rootdeep_mycel` | forest | "A soft white-lilac web of mycelium threads over dark earth. Palette: #d8cce8 #8a7a9a #2a2230 #7be0c8." |
   | `rootdeep_glowmoss` | meadow | "Cushions of teal glow moss with brighter specks. Palette: #2e6a60 #7be0c8 #c8ffe8 #1c1a24." |
   | `rootdeep_lakesand` | sand | "Fine dark-blue lake shore sand with tiny glowing pebbles. Palette: #2a3440 #3a4a58 #7be0c8 #ffcf7a." |
   | `rootdeep_geode` | snow | "Lavender and lilac crystal crust like the inside of a geode, soft facets. Palette: #8a6ab8 #b8a0e0 #e8dcff #3a2a48." |
   | `rootdeep_rootfloor` | dirt | "A floor of flattened smooth roots worn into a path. Palette: #8a5a3c #6f4a32 #c98b4b #ffcf7a." |
   | `rootdeep_cavestone` | rock | "Rounded dark cave stone with warm brown veins. Palette: #3a3040 #4a3a48 #8a5a3c #1c1a24." |

   Cliff slot `rootdeep_rootwall`: generate "Seamless texture of a cavern wall woven from thick tree roots, dark
   plum-brown with teal and gold glowing threads, tileable" (also usable as d6's `tex_rootwall`), same processing.
   Size limits (tests/assets.test.js): hd ≤ 110 KB, lo ≤ 40 KB.
2. **Cards** (alpha webp ≤ 150 KB): `rootdeep_card_ground` (1024 × 512 sheet of 4: glowcap, rootlet, crystal, drip;
   sliced with `python3 -I tools/slice_sheet.py`), `rootdeep_card_spores` (128², particles), `rootdeep_card_lakeglow`
   (512², the Underlake shimmer, used as an additive decal on the lake), `rootdeep_card_memoryknot` (512² 8-frame
   strip through `tools/process_strip.py`; used by S-190). Prompts verbatim from art-audio.md#rootdeep.
3. **Loading card** `load_rootdeep` (1600 × 900 webp ≤ 200 KB, plus `lo` 960 × 540 ≤ 70 KB): "A vast warm underground
   cavern: an enormous glowing tree-root column rising out of sight, mushrooms as big as houses glowing teal, a still
   underground lake, little round burrow doors with lamps, everything lit by soft gold and teal glow. Palette:
   #1c1a24 #ffcf7a #7be0c8 #8a5a3c."
4. **Map art** `map_rootdeep` (1024² webp ≤ 200 KB): "A cave system seen from above as a painted map: a central glowing
   root column, a mushroom forest, an underground lake, crystal galleries, a burrow village; dark warm rock between.
   Palette: #1c1a24 #ffcf7a #7be0c8 #8a5a3c." Registered as the realm's This-land canvas background, aligned so the
   Taproot is at the centre (the canvas fallback draws the carve mask in `#3a2f3a` on `#1c1a24`).
   Also `ui_chart_under` (the chart's **Under** tab, 1024², atlas-far.md#chart): the Taproot at the centre and eight
   spurs at the chart bearings (Pinewhistle 337.5°, Kettle 67.5°, Archwater 112.5°, Duskwick 157.5°, Glassway 180°,
   Saltglass 202.5°, Threemills 247.5°, home straight up as the Windmill Stair), each ending in a round root door
   icon. The tab itself is wired in S-182.
5. **Music** (`js/realms/music/rootdeep.js`, registered with `G.audio.addTrack`): track `rootdeep` exactly as written
   in art-audio.md#track-data (a low cello drone in D, 60 bpm). Layers by region (`G.audio.layer`): `town`
   (Burrowbottom: plucked bass and a mandolin-like pluck at 84 bpm), `glow` (celesta shimmer every 8 bars), `lake`
   (drone alone, single drips at random 1.5-3.5 s), `geode` (glass-harmonica arpeggio in fifths). Ambience beds:
   `drips`, `earthhum` (40 Hz warmth), `market` (Burrowbottom), `glowfizz` (Glowcap). **Dim** (art-audio.md 5.5):
   before `lumbraCalm` the cello `lead2` is muted, the drone runs at 30 % and the cello `tones` are off.
6. **Bundle:** every key above carries `realm: 'rootdeep'` and lives under `assets/realms/rootdeep/`; `node
   tools/build-pages.mjs` puts them in the `rootdeep` bundle only. Record the bundle size in the PR (budget 2.5 MB).
7. Log every prompt and command in `docs/art/prompts.md`.

#### Assets
Seven terrain layers + the cliff wall, four cards, `load_rootdeep` (+ lo), `map_rootdeep`, `ui_chart_under`.
Fallbacks: terrain → the home layer in the same slot; cards → canvas blobs (teal radial dots; a gold ring strip for
the knot); loading card → `F-home:loading_bg`; map → the canvas carve-mask render; chart tab → canvas circles and
lines in the same layout.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every key loads from the `rootdeep` bundle and has a working fallback (`?noassets=all` renders the realm).
- `tests/assets.test.js` passes the size limits; the `rootdeep` bundle is ≤ 2.5 MB; core precache unchanged (± 0).
- In-page: entering `rootdeep` switches the music to `rootdeep` with the region layer; Burrowbottom plays `town`;
  before `lumbraCalm` the `lead2` voice is muted.
- Images are original and in the house style (reviewer checks the prompt log).
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (assets test, PWA partition test `tests/pwa.test.js`).
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs rootdeep` (add "music layers by region" and "dim cello before lumbraCalm").
- `bun run test:smoke` with `?noassets=all`.

#### Out of scope
- Sketch art for the vistas (`sketch_rootdeep_1`-`_2`, S-180), portraits (S-178, S-185), bestiary cards (S-179,
  S-185), the Heart's track (S-183).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a generated tile does not tile cleanly; `make_seamless.py --blend 96` and a regenerate. Risk: the bundle passes
2.5 MB; drop `lo` cards to q75. Rollback: revert; every key falls back.

<a id="s-178"></a>
### S-178 · feat: Burrowbottom's people: Gran Burrows and the burrowers
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-176, S-174, S-068a, S-120a · Size: M (about 440 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [cast#gran-burrows](../bible/cast.md#gran-burrows), [atlas-far#rootdeep-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-people), [atlas-far#rootdeep-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-kit), [cast#spots-and-schedules](../bible/cast.md#spots-and-schedules)

#### Why
Burrowbottom is the Rootdeep's town and the place chapter 13 is about: Digby's Gran, who sent him up top to plant a
spring he could smell. A town needs doors, a market and people who keep their own hours. This step makes the `mole`
rig data-driven so every burrower is a look record, and puts the people of **The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart and
Lumbra** in place before the chapter that meets them (S-191a).

#### What
1. **Mole rig family** (`js/models.js`): turn the shipped `npcDef('digby')` body into a `mole` family builder that
   reads `NPC_LOOKS[id]` (`js/content/looks.js`, S-088): params `h`, `pal {fur, muzzle, nose, coat, coatStrap, hat,
   lamp}`, `eyes: 'squint' | 'open'`, `helmet: bool`, `acc: [...]` (`shawl` torus, `glasses` two tori on a chain tube,
   `lantern` an `extra` with `glow: 0.6`, `jar`, `scarf`, `apron`). `npcDef('digby')` must stay **identical** to today:
   a unit test builds both the old and the new Digby (`tests/models.test.js`: same part names, mesh count, vertex count
   and colour list).
2. **Look records** (every Rootdeep NPC carries a `glow: 0.6` part that reads from 20 m in the dark, cast.md rule):

   | id | Name | h | Palette | Accessories | Speed (F27) |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `gran_burrows` | Gran Burrows | 0.95 | fur `#6e5a4e`, muzzle `#c8a890`, nose `#f0a0a0`, shawl `#b8a6d0`, apron `#8a5a3c`, glasses `#f2c94c`, lantern `#7be0c8` | no helmet, `eyes: 'open'`, shawl, glasses on chain, glowcap lantern on a hook | 1.0 |
   | `teasel` | Teasel | 0.95 | fur `#6f4e3c`, muzzle `#c09878`, overalls `#3f7a6a`, helmet `#e7c45a`, lamp `#fff4b0` | helmet with lamp (the glow part), a lamp-tender's long pole `#8a5a3c` with a brass hook `#b8894a` | 1.6 |
   | `loam` | Loam | 0.9 | fur `#7a5a48`, muzzle `#c8a080`, apron `#c2563f`, jar `#ffcf7a` | apron, a glass jar of glow-grubs held in both paws (the glow part) | 1.4 |
   | `spud` | Spud | 0.8 | fur `#8a6650`, muzzle `#d0b090`, scarf `#7be0c8`, cap `#e7c45a` | knitted scarf, a too-big knitted cap with a pompom, a pocket lamp (glow part) | 2.2 |

3. **Town dressing** (a new recipe stage `town` in `js/realms/recipes/cavern.js`, run after `scatter`, on the recipe's
   scatter stream so it is part of prints; prints refreshed):
   - **14 burrow doors** (`rootdeep_burrow_door`, S-176): on the terrace back walls of the Burrowbottom ellipse, upper
     terrace 5 doors, middle 5, lower 4, every 12 m along each terrace's wall line starting 8 m from its west end,
     facing into the ellipse; tints cycle red `#c2563f`, teal `#3fa79a`, yellow `#e7c45a`. Gran's door is fixed at
     (−183, −36) facing 160° (tint teal) and the regular door nearest to it is skipped.
   - **3 stalls** (`rootdeep_stall`) at `rootdeep.market-1..3` (−158, 22), (−146, 26), (−134, 22), facing 180°.
   - **8 street lamp posts** (`rootdeep_lamp_post`, ids `rootdeep:lamp-1..8`): two either side of each terrace ramp
     (x = −150 and x = −190, 3 m from the ramp's top and bottom), state **dim** (`#4a3c48`) until ss-31 lights them
     (S-192). They are 8 of ss-31's 12 lamps.
   - **The Great Burrow Lamp** (S-176) stays the town landmark at (−165, −5).
4. **Spots** (`W.npcSpots`): `rootdeep.gran` (−180, −32), `rootdeep.gran_door` (−183, −34.5) (cast.md's id, 1.5 m out
   from the door), `rootdeep.market-1..3` as above (+1.5 m south of each stall), `rootdeep.lamp` (−160, 4),
   `rootdeep.stair` (−128, −44), `rootdeep.taproot` (−6, −16).
5. **People** (`js/content/npcs/rootdeep.js`, NPC records with `realm: 'rootdeep'`):

   | NPC | Spot | Schedule (`js/content/schedules.js`) |
   |---|---|---|
   | `gran_burrows` | `rootdeep.gran` | `[[7,'rootdeep.gran','work'],[19,'rootdeep.gran_door','indoors']]` (indoors falls back to the door spot until S-193's interior) |
   | `teasel` (lamp-tender, ss-31's helper, R25) | `rootdeep.lamp` | `[[6,'rootdeep.lamp','wander:6'],[21,'rootdeep.lamp','sit']]` |
   | `loam` (tea and glow-grub stall) | `rootdeep.market-1` | `[[7,'rootdeep.market-1','work'],[20,'rootdeep.market-1','sit']]` |
   | `spud` (the kit who wants to see the sky) | `rootdeep.market-2` | `[[7,'rootdeep.market-2','wander:5'],[19,'rootdeep.gran','stand'],[21,'rootdeep.gran_door','indoors']]` |
   | stall 3 | - | unattended; `[E] Look · Stall sign`: "BACK IN FIVE MINUTES. (Mole minutes.)" |

   Glowcaps in the realm dim to 60 % between 21:00 and 06:00 (`S.tod`; the clock runs here, cast.md).
6. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/rootdeep.js`, text `js/text/en/rootdeep.js`, priority 10 idle, rotating by day
   index; Gran's chapter-13 lines are S-191a's):
   - `rootdeep.npc.gran_burrows.idle.1` "Down here we light our own way. A little help never hurt anybody, mind."
   - `rootdeep.npc.gran_burrows.idle.2` "Digby sends a joke down the roots every spring. Heard it. I've heard all of them."
   - `rootdeep.npc.gran_burrows.idle.3` "Wipe your paws. The whole town's a doormat, but wipe them anyway."
   - `rootdeep.npc.teasel.idle.1` "Tunnels are easy. You keep going until you're somewhere. Then you're there."
   - `rootdeep.npc.teasel.idle.2` "I tend the lamps. Well. I tend them when they'll light. They've been sulky lately."
   - `rootdeep.npc.loam.idle.1` "Tea? It's root tea. Tastes of roots. Very honest tea."
   - `rootdeep.npc.loam.idle.2` "These glow-grubs in my jar are pets. The wild ones out in the mushrooms hop. Mind those."
   - `rootdeep.npc.spud.idle.1` "Is the sky really blue? Gran says so. Teasel says 'mostly'. I want to check."
   - `rootdeep.npc.spud.idle.2` "You came from up top? Does it echo? Is it big? Bigger than the market?"
   - Gran's door before S-193: `[E] Knock · Gran's door` → "Gran's out front, dear. Mind the step." (her own voice
     from the step when she is at `rootdeep.gran`; at night: "It's late. Come back when the glowcaps wake.")
   - Register `seen:rootdeep.*` and `met:gran_burrows`, `met:teasel`, `met:loam`, `met:spud` in `js/content/flags.js`.
7. **Voice markers:** add Gran Burrows's "Heard it." to [charter#reserved-markers](../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers)'s
   table data (`js/content/markers.js` or wherever the content lint keeps the reserved list), owned by `gran_burrows`.
8. **Sound cues:** Gran's thimble-on-needle tap every 6 s within 10 m (`sfx.thimble`, a 2-sample synth tick; visual twin
   her lantern swinging 8°); Loam's teacup clink every 9 s.

#### Assets
- `portrait_gran_burrows`, `portrait_teasel`, `portrait_loam` (realm `rootdeep`, `assets/realms/rootdeep/portraits/`)
  and `portrait_spud` (core, `assets/portraits/spud.webp`: he becomes a plaza visitor in S-191b). Recipe per
  [cast#gran-burrows](../bible/cast.md#gran-burrows) "Portraits": generate 1024², transparent, then
  `python3 -I tools/export_sprites.py <scratch> <scratch>/out part_00=portrait_<id>:480`, pad to 512², webp q82, ≤ 60 KB.
  Prompts = house preamble + portrait suffix + subject:
  - Gran: cast.md's prompt verbatim.
  - Teasel: "A cheerful young brown mole lamp-tender with a yellow mining helmet with a lamp, teal overalls, holding a
    long wooden lamp pole with a brass hook, squinting happily."
  - Loam: "A round, friendly brown mole stall-keeper in a red apron holding a glass jar of softly glowing honey-gold
    grubs like a lantern, warm smile."
  - Spud: "A small mole kit in a teal knitted scarf and a too-big yellow knitted cap with a pompom, a tiny pocket lamp,
    looking up with huge wonder."
- Turnarounds `docs/art/refs/<id>_turnaround.webp` (docs only).
- ART BRIEFs in the PR: `npc_gran_burrows` (cast.md's, ≤ 5,000 tris), `npc_teasel`, `npc_loam`, `npc_spud`
  (mole recolours, 0.8-0.95 m, ≤ 4,000 tris, 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a <look>, <h> m tall."); GLB slots added
  commented out with their heights. Fallback for each: the `mole` rig with its look record.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `npcDef('digby')` is unchanged (unit test), and the four look records build with `?noassets=all`.
- In the realm suite's town view the 14 doors, 3 stalls, 8 dim lamp posts and 4 NPCs are drawn and the view stays
  within budget in both cameras (record numbers).
- Each NPC stands on its spot at 12:00; at 20:00 Gran is at her door spot and Spud beside her (F27 rows; in-page).
- Talking to each NPC shows its idle line with its portrait (or the name only under `?noassets=all`).
- No enemy spawns in the `burrowbottom` region or within 12 m of its edge (the town rule; asserted here with the
  region table empty and re-asserted by S-179 when species exist).
- Every new line passes the content lint (≤ 220 chars, markers, tokens); `!!` and `~` absent.
- Prints refreshed; home prints byte-identical. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/models.test.js` (new: Digby unchanged, the four looks), `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js`
  (prints, door/stall/lamp counts and positions), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs rootdeep` (add "Burrowbottom people on their spots at 12:00 and 20:00", "talk shows portrait").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`, `node tests/inpage.mjs npc` (S-174's suite stays green).
- `bun run test:pwa` after `node tools/build-pages.mjs` (Spud's portrait in core, the others in the `rootdeep` bundle).

#### Out of scope
- Gran's chapter-13 scene and Digby's walk (S-191a); Gran's interior (S-193); the ss-31 relighting and the 4 high
  lamps (S-192); Spud's plaza visits (S-191b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: refactoring Digby's builder changes the shipped villager; the identity test catches it. Risk: 14 doors plus
stalls push the town view's draw calls; doors and lamps are instanced types from S-176. Rollback: revert; the realm is
hidden.

<a id="s-179"></a>
### S-179 · feat: Rootdeep enemies: Glow Grub, Drip Bat and Pebble Mimic
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-042, S-176 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [creatures#enemy-glow-grub](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-glow-grub), [creatures#enemy-drip-bat](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-drip-bat), [creatures#enemy-pebble-mimic](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-pebble-mimic), [creatures#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables), [atlas-far#rootdeep-enemies](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-enemies)

#### Why
The Rootdeep's wild halls need their own gentle threats, harder by pattern (tier 2) than Act II's, so Act III feels
like a step up without bigger hits (CANON §7). Three species, one per archetype the cavern suits: a double-hopping
grub, a swooping bat whose stripe bends, and a pebble pile that is only pretending.

#### What
1. **Species data** (`js/realms/rootdeep/enemies.js`, merged into `ENEMY` by the realm module; the shape of
   [creatures#archetypes](../bible/creatures.md#archetypes)), numbers copied exactly:

   | id | name | archetype | hp | r | emerge | gems (tier) | poof | params |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `glow_grub` | Glow Grub | hopper | 4 | 0.40 | ground | 2 | `0xffcf7a` | `{ crouch: 0.50, harmGate: 0.15, hopSpeed: 4.6, hopT: 0.46, reach: 2.9, rest: 1.0, tier: 2, secondCrouch: 0.55, light: 2 }` |
   | `drip_bat` | Drip Bat | swooper | 4 | 0.38 | sky (drops from the ceiling or the roof card) | 2 | `0xa9e6ff` | `{ rise: 0.60, swoopSpeed: 8.5, swoopT: 0.55, reach: 5.3, alt: 2.1, tier: 2, bend: 35 }` |
   | `pebble_mimic` | Pebble Mimic | mimic | 6 | 0.44 | still | 5 (sure) | `0xb89cf0` | `{ notice: 2.5, spring: 0.8, biteWind: 0.60, bite: 2.2, bites: 3, tired: 1.5, gems: 5, tier: 2 }` |

   Tier-2 patterns used (S-041/S-042 implement the menu; add any missing): hopper "a second hop 0.5 s after landing
   with its own 0.55 s crouch" and brighter glow; swooper "the swoop curves once, the bent part redrawn 0.55 s before
   the bend". `light: 2` makes a Glow Grub count as light for Dimlings within 2 m (add `light` to the ghost module's
   light sources list). Drip Bat's rise plays `sfx.dripRise` (two drips, rising pitch) as its sound-first cue.
2. **Rigs** (`enemyDef` cases in `js/models.js`, ≤ 1,200 tris, ≤ 6 materials each, drawn through `instRig`):
   - `glow_grub`: four spheres in a row (r 0.18, 0.2, 0.18, 0.14), body `#f0e6b0` emissive 0.5 toward `#ffcf7a`, bands
     `#c9a86a` as thin tori, two dot eyes `#2a2440`; inch-squash idle.
   - `drip_bat`: fluff sphere r 0.2 `#4a5a7a`, two wing `rbox` plates with an emissive edge `#7be0c8`, a drop
     (stretched sphere `#a9e6ff`, transmissive look via toon rim) under the nose; flap 8 Hz.
   - `pebble_mimic`: three squashed spheres `#8a7a8a` and three crystal prisms (`cone` 6-sided) `#b89cf0` / `#7be0c8`
     emissive 0.3; awake pose lifts on four `capsule` stubs and shows a mouth line; tremble 0.8 s with a "?" sprite.
3. **Spawn tables** (fill the rows S-175 left as comments; creatures.md is the definition): `glowcap`
   `[['glow_grub', 4], ['drip_bat', 3]]` max 6; `underlake` `[['drip_bat', 3], ['glow_grub', 2], ['dimling', 2]]` max
   6; `geode` `[['drip_bat', 3], ['glow_grub', 2]]` max 6; `burrowbottom` `[]` max 0. Spawn-point filter: no point
   within 12 m of the `burrowbottom` region edge, none on the Taproot island (r < 22 from (0, 0)), none on causeways.
   No night rule (the Rootdeep has no day).
4. **Mimic anchors** (recipe `anchors` stage, part of prints): `rootdeep:mimic-1..5` at (135, −80) G1, (150, 15) G2,
   (212, −2) G3, (140, 95) G4, (198, 118) G5. A mimic never respawns in a visit (turret/mimic rule); a poofed mimic
   leaves its pebble pile as a harmless prop until the realm reloads. The Lantern makes its eyes glint from 6 m.
5. **Bestiary** (S-046 shape): Glow Grub — Hoot "Glow Grubs are tiny lamps that got bored of standing still." /
   Clover "It hops twice. Wait for the second one!"; Drip Bat — "The Drip Bat sleeps upside down so the drips run off
   its nose. Clever." / "Watch the line on the floor. It bends!"; Pebble Mimic — "Pebble Mimics pretend to be treasure
   because they would like to be admired. Admire them, then." / "If the sparkles wiggle, step back!". Land chip
   "The Rootdeep".
6. **Debug:** `G.debug.actors.spawn('glow_grub' | 'drip_bat' | 'pebble_mimic', {x, z})` works in any realm.

#### Assets
- Cards `card_glow_grub`, `card_drip_bat`, `card_pebble_mimic` (384² webp ≤ 45 KB, realm `rootdeep`): prompt
  `[STYLE] A bestiary card illustration on cream paper with a soft torn edge, a single <description>, three-quarter
  view, centred, gentle rim light, no text, no border lines, square.` with the descriptions in creatures.md
  ("a plump soft grub glowing warm honey-gold from inside, sitting on a glowing root in a dark cavern"; "a round
  blue-grey cave bat with glowing teal wing edges and a single water drop on its nose, hanging upside down from a
  glowing root"; "a pile of round pebbles crowned with lilac and teal crystals that has just stood up on stumpy legs,
  looking surprised, in a sparkling geode cave"); processed with `tools/export_sprites.py` to 384². Fallback: the
  canvas blob card.
- ART BRIEFs in the PR copied from creatures.md (silhouette, size, palette, materials, animation, poly 600/2,000,
  700/2,500, 1,000/3,000, card and 3D prompts); GLB slots `models.glow_grub` etc. commented out.

#### Acceptance criteria
- For each species (in-page, `G.debug.actors.spawn`): the telegraph is drawn ≥ 0.55 s before the first damage frame;
  every hit on Pip is exactly 1 HP; at 0 HP it poofs (never "dies") and the bestiary counts it.
- Glow Grub's second hop has its own 0.55 s crouch; Drip Bat's bend is redrawn ≥ 0.55 s ahead; Pebble Mimic shudders
  0.8 s with "?" before its first bite and is tired 1.5 s (gold) after three bites.
- A Glow Grub within 2 m makes a Dimling hittable.
- In 120 s of simulated roaming per region, no spawn lands in Burrowbottom, within 12 m of its edge, on the Taproot
  island or on a causeway; region caps hold; at most 2 tokens.
- Unit test: every move of the three species has wind ≥ 0.55 s and damage exactly 1 (charter decision 22).
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/enemies.test.js` (the wind and damage table check over `ENEMY`), `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js`
  (mimic anchors in prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs species` (add "glow_grub", "drip_bat", "pebble_mimic" tests) and `node tests/inpage.mjs rootdeep`
  ("no spawns in town", "grub lights dimling").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wp4` and `wp5` (archetype modules unchanged for the shipped six).

#### Out of scope
- Dungeon waves in d6 (S-183, S-184 place them from map `e` tiles), Dimling changes (shipped S-058), thornglass (S-233).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the bend pattern overlaps the token rule; the swooper module asks for one token for the whole swoop. Rollback:
revert; the realm is hidden.

### S-180 · feat: Rootdeep finds: chests, keepsakes, acorns, vistas, Lamp Logs, Hollow doors, benches
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-176, S-048, S-074, S-086b · Size: M (about 470 lines, most of it data) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [atlas-far#rootdeep-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-pois), [atlas-far#rootdeep-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-keepsakes), [atlas-far#rootdeep-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-curios), [atlas-far#rootdeep-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-arrival), [atlas-far#rootdeep-coverage](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-coverage), [lore#log-19](../bible/lore.md#log-19), [lore#log-20](../bible/lore.md#log-20), [lore#log-21](../bible/lore.md#log-21), [systems#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids)

#### Why
A land is explored for what it hides. The Rootdeep needs its 116-point-of-interest density (CANON §5.1) and the
coverage test that proves no 40 × 40 m cell is empty, so its 1.5 h of exploration is real.

#### What
1. **Pass `placeFinds`** in `js/realms/recipes/cavern.js` on `new RNG(40241 * 71 + 3)` (atlas-far#far-passes). Each
   find is anchored at the atlas coordinate and nudged to the nearest valid spot within 8 m (ring scan, the shipped
   `placeShells` fallback rule); `ok()` = walkable, reachable (or on its gate's deck), ≥ 2.5 m from other finds and
   NPC spots, ≥ 12 m from every mimic anchor (for chests). Writes `W.finds` (one list, ids below); restores
   `W.walk / W.reach`; adds nothing to other lists. Because the realm is still hidden, prints are refreshed (frozen
   after S-191b).
2. **Ids** (systems#ids; atlas labels map by number, reconciliation R38):

   | Kind | Save ids | Count | Data |
   |---|---|---|---|

   | Sleeping Acorns | `rootdeep:a1..a9` | 9 | atlas list in order (−150, 0) … (140, 75); `collect.acorn` |
   | Vistas | `rootdeep:v1` "The Roots of Everything" (−14, 6) · `v2` "The Sun in the Rock" (125, −20) | 2 | `[E] Sketch · Vista`; `collect.vista` |
   | Lamp Logs | `rootdeep:19` waystation alcove (−140, −42) · `rootdeep:20` Taproot scaffold (16, 4) · `rootdeep:21` G1 survey camp (162, −100) | 3 | texts verbatim from lore#log-19, #log-20, #log-21 (R12); `collect.log` |
   | Hollow doors | `rootdeep:hollow1` (−40, 226) · `hollow2` (98, −226) · `hollow3` (240, 22) | 3 | spiral door props; no portal yet (S-186); Look: "A spiral door, shut tight. Something inside hums." |
   | Benches | `bench:rootdeep:b1` (−165, 14) "Burrowbottom · Market Square" · `b2` (−60, −160) "Glowcap Forest · First Glade" · `b3` (12, −10) "The Underlake · Taproot Isle" · `b4` (128, 14) "Geode Galleries · Sun Geode" · `b5` (22, 150) "The Underlake · Drip Bay" | 5 (R5) | `kit_bench` (S-082); first sit at any Rootdeep bench says once "No sky down here. The roots keep their own time." (`seen:rootdeep.bench`) |
   | Curios | `rootdeep:curio-1..16` | 16 | pill and line verbatim from atlas-far#rootdeep-curios (`[E] Look · <thing>`) |

3. **Gate finds** (gate objects from `js/gates.js`, S-048): c4's `glowbud_door` at the pocket mouth (−146, −146) and the
   pocket as a `dark_mouth` (Lantern); c5's glide: a shelf-fungus deck on the Grandmother Cap's lower shelf at y 9
   (S-176's deck) with the target cap-top deck at (45, −205) y 6, 9 m gap (`gap` gate, glide only; prove unreachable on

   (222, −20) to (234, −20), **inert** until S-183 ships the light-root entity (Look: "A sleepy root lies across the

   deck at y 7; c12's `ivy` lattice on a geode face 6 m tall at (215, 137); the **G1 high ledge**: Lamp Log 21's old
   survey camp sits on a ledge deck at y 9 round (162, −100), 5 m above G1's floor, reached by a hookbloom above it at
   y 12 (a Hook find). Atlas counts 8 gate finds; the soft-earth mound is S-181a's.
4. **Progression solver** (`js/progress-graph.js` data for `rootdeep`): nodes per region; every find's `needs`
   (`lantern`, `glide`, `hook`, `climb`, `sunglass` once S-183 lands the root); the pacing-rule-5 report lists what

   keepsakes 4, acorns 9, vistas 2, Lamp Logs 3, Hollows 3, benches 5 (the generated file shows the counts now; the
   group stays hidden in the tracker until the realm is unhidden).

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c01` Glowcap Night-Lamp, `c10` Moonmoth Lilac trim, `c05` "Grandmother Cap" sticker. Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

  reachable with their tool (solver); c10 is reported "waits for S-183" (not a failure) until then.

<a id="s-181a"></a>
### S-181a · feat: Digging Paws dig soft earth; buried chests on the Rootdeep and the four isles
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-048, S-175, S-180, S-037b · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none (the item is only given in d6, S-184) · Bible: [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [systems#tool-mitts](../bible/systems.md#tool-mitts), [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [systems#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools), [atlas-far#rootdeep-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-pois), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)

Split from INDEX S-181 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): part a is the tool, the gate and the buried chests
on the Rootdeep and the four isles; part b ([S-181b](#s-181b)) is home's five dig spots and the Undercap hole.

#### Why
The Digging Paws are Act III's first tool and the one the Rootdeep is built around: soft earth hides chests on every
land Pip has already walked, so the new tool pays off backwards (pacing rule 5: every land re-opens finds). Digby's
family digs; now Pip can too.

#### What
1. **Item and ability** (`js/items.js`, `js/abilities.js`): `ITEMS.mitts = { name: 'Digging Paws', kind: 'tool', icon:
   'icon_mitts', stack: 1, beat: 'ch13', desc: 'Sturdy digging mitts, mole-sized. Stand by soft earth and press {tool}
   to dig.' }`; `ABILITIES.dig = { name: 'Dig', from: 'mitts' }`. The tool ring gains the Paws in slot 5 (systems
   order: Lantern, Tide Bell, Thistle Hook, Sunglass, Digging Paws, Starsong, Ember Sling); the item-get beat equips
   them. Item-get line (used by S-184's chest, R9): "You got the Digging Paws! Stand by soft earth and press {tool} to
   dig. Digby would be proud."
2. **Gate `soft_earth`** (`js/gates.js`): look = a brown mound r 0.8 m `#8a5a3c` with a sparkle sprite twinkling every
   1.5 s (`#ffe9a8`) and two pebbles; prompt `[T] Dig · Soft earth` within 1.6 m when `ability:dig`; without the Paws
   `[E] Look · Soft earth` says "Soft earth. Something's down there." (systems gate line). Dig: 1.0 s (`player.state =
   'frozen'`, two scoops of the left-hand mitt in FP, dirt-puff particles `#8a5a3c` ×10, sound `sfx.dig` (lowpass noise
   bursts at 0.0 and 0.5 s)); then the mound becomes a hole decal and the record's `reveal` runs: `{ chest: '<id>' }`
   rises the chest out of the hole (0.6 s, shipped chest-rise if present, else a lerp), `{ item }`, `{ flag }`,
   `{ portal }`. Sets `gate:<realm>:<id>` for good. First-person cue: a snuffly earth sound within 4 m (`sfx.snuffle`,
   one per 3 s). Export `digAt(record, ctx)` so the dungeon entity `d` (S-184) uses the same code.
3. **Chime Bell**: if the Chime Bell exists (`grep -rn "chime" js/ | grep -i bell`), buried chests count as chests for
   its chime interval.
4. **Rootdeep `placeBuried`** (`new RNG(40241 * 73 + 5)`, a post-pass in the `cavern` recipe after `placeFinds`):
   `rootdeep:c9` at (30, 215) in the Drip Bay east bank, mound record `rootdeep:mound-c9` with `reveal: { chest:
   'rootdeep:c9' }`. Prints refreshed (realm still hidden).
5. **The four isles' buried chests** (atlas-isles decision 13: two per isle, inside each isle's chest count). First
   `grep -rn "soft_earth\|softEarth" js/realms/` to see what the isle finds steps placed:
   - If the finds steps placed the anchors with a `soft_earth` gate record (atlas-isles marks them "soft-earth mound
     always"), wire those records to the new gate. Nothing moves; isle prints unchanged.
   - If an isle has no record, add a `placeBuried` post-pass on that isle (`new RNG(seed * 73 + 13)`, the isle's seed; never
     `73 + 5`, which is `placeIsleRook`'s stream; see [atlas-isles#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes))
     placing the mound at the atlas coordinate (nearest valid within 8 m), adding nothing to other lists; a realm test
     on that seed and seeds 1-20 of its recipe proves it changes nothing else.

   | Isle | Chest ids | Anchors (atlas-isles) |
   |---|---|---|
   | Duskwick | `duskwick:c8`, `c9` | (150, 70) Coral Steps 6 m terrace; (−104, −66) Ember Sands hot pools |
   | Pinewhistle | `pinewhistle:c12`, `c13` | (−118, −60) beyond Owl Bridge; (−130, 60) logging camp |
   | Archwater | `archwater:c11`, `c12` | (−40, 160) Tidepool Shelf sandy cove; (10, −20) Basalt Stairs top terrace east end |
   | Threemills | `threemills:c12`, `c13` | (−150, −10) sheep downs fallow field; (70, −30) Tumble Hill east foot |

   Each holds 30 gems. Archwater's two must be reachable at both tides (the tide test runs both states).
6. **Solver** (`js/progress-graph.js`): `soft_earth` gates need `dig`; the "what each tool opens" report shows the
   Paws opening 8 isle chests + 1 Rootdeep chest (+ home in S-181b); pacing rule 5 (≥ 3 finds in earlier lands) passes.
7. **Viewmodel** (`js/view/viewmodel.js`): the left-hand tool slot shows `tool_mitts` when equipped (code-built: a
   rounded box mitt `#7a4a2e` with three cone claws `#e8d8c0` and a stitched cuff `#f3e3c3` with a gold spiral decal
   `#ffcf7a`, ≤ 500 tris); in TP the mitts hang on Pip's belt (a small box on the belt bone, never touching the GLB).
8. **Debug:** `G.debug.items.give('mitts')`; `G.debug.gates.dig(id)` (dig a mound without walking to it, for tests).

#### Assets
- `tool_mitts` code-built; ART BRIEF from [systems#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools) (`tool_mitts` row:
  silhouette, palette, "two scoops in 1.0 s with dirt puffs", image prompt "a pair of chunky brown leather digging
  mitts with pale claw tips and a golden spiral stitched on the cuffs, isolated, transparent background", 3D prompt
  "low-poly toon digging mitt with claws and stitched cuff, 500 tris"); slot `models.tool_mitts` commented out.
- `gate_soft_earth` code-built (flattened hemisphere + sparkle sprite, 150 tris); ART BRIEF row from systems.
- `icon_mitts`: one 128² cell in the items icon atlas (S-037's `ui_icons_items`); if the atlas is generated, add the
  cell with prompt "a pair of chunky brown leather digging mitts with a golden spiral on the cuff, icon, centred,
  transparent background" and re-pack with `tools/pack_atlas.py`; fallback the canvas glyph (a brown mitt shape).

#### Acceptance criteria
- With `mitts` given: `[T] Dig · Soft earth` shows within 1.6 m (FP crosshair or TP facing cone), digging takes 1.0 s,
  the chest rises and pays 30 gems, `gate:<realm>:<id>` and `S.chests[id]` are set, and a reload keeps the hole.
- Without `mitts`: the Look line shows and nothing digs.
- All 9 buried chests (Rootdeep 1, isles 8) are reachable and diggable in-page via `G.debug.realm` + walk-to.
- Isle prints byte-identical (or, if a post-pass was needed, the pass-off comparison is equal on that seed and seeds
  1-20); Rootdeep prints refreshed; home prints byte-identical.
- The tool ring shows the Paws in slot 5; `keys.test.js` / `padbinds.test.js` unchanged (no new action).
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/gates.test.js` (soft-earth record, reveal kinds, `gate:` flag), `bun test tests/progress-graph.test.js`,
  `bun test tests/realms/*.test.js` (Rootdeep c9; isle comparisons), `bun test tests/items.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs tools` (add "dig soft earth reveals a chest", "no dig without mitts", "hole persists after
  reload").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope
- Home's dig spots, Digby's rumours, Shellby's hat and the Undercap hole (S-181b); soft earth inside d6 (S-184) and
  o2 (S-194); later lands' mounds (their finds steps).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: an isle's finds step placed the chest on open ground instead of as a mound; then the chest becomes a mound only
by changing a shipped find, which this step must not do: leave it open, record the deviation, and let the solver
report show one fewer Paws find. Rollback: revert; the Paws cannot be obtained before S-184 anyway.
