<a id="s-181b"></a>
### S-181b · feat: home's dig spots: Digby's five rumours, Shellby's old hat and the Undercap hole
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-181a, S-033a · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [atlas-home#dig-spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#dig-spots), [atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes), [cast#digby](../bible/cast.md#digby)

#### Why
Every tool opens at least one home find (pacing rule 5), and Digby is the right person to point the way: one rumour
per talk, each a sight joke only he may make. One mound gives back Shellby's straw hat, the one he wears in memory 4,
so the player meets the hat before they meet the boy.

#### What
1. **Home pass `placeDigSpots`**, pass row 18, `new RNG(7151 * 149 + 79)`, appended after row 17 (`placeOldBeacons`) in
   `js/world-post.js` (or wherever S-004's pass list lives; grep `placeOldBeacons`), guarded by `o.placeDigSpots !==
   false`. Writes `W.digSpots = [{ id, x, y, z, gate: { type: 'soft_earth', reveal } }]` in this order, obeying atlas-home
   §1.1 rules 1-10 (`near()` = rule 5, `ok()` = §1.3):

   | id | Placement | Reveal |
   |---|---|---|
   | `home:dig-windmill` | exactly on acorn a11's position (row 16 `placeAcorns`) | `{ acorn: 'home:a11' }`: the acorn is hidden (not snoring, not wakeable) until the mound is dug |
   | `home:dig-glade` | `near((−36.7, −71.9), 4, 9)`, ≥ 3 m from the heart chest | 25-gem pouch |
   | `home:dig-peaks` | `near((75.2, −21.7), 4, 10)` | 25-gem pouch |
   | `home:dig-dune` | `near((−20, 110), 0, 12)`, Shores sand | 20-gem pouch |
   | `home:dig-bench` | `near(Shellby's Bench + 2.5 m behind it, 0, 3)` (Shellby's Bench from row 9) | key item `shellby_hat` |
   | `home:dig-undercap` | `near(Grumble's Crown centre + (r + 2.5) m on bearing 250°, 0, 3)` | `{ flag: 'gate:home:undercap' }` and a root-hole prop (R41) |

   Positions are a pure function of the seed (rule 6); runtime shows each mound unless its `gate:home:<id>` flag is set.
   The Undercap hole is a walk-in portal record `home:undercap` (`to: { realm: 'undercap', spawn: 'entry' }`, `needs:
   'flag:gate:home:undercap'`) whose target realm does not exist until S-194: until then the hole's Look says "A root
   hole. It goes down a long way. Something small is snoring down there." and the portal is not offered (the portal
   code already skips realms that are missing or hidden). Register `undercap` in `REALMS` only in S-194.
2. **Gem pouches**: a `pouch` reveal pops a small cloth bag that pays its gems with the shipped coin pickup feed
   ("+25 ✦"); stored as `S.chests['home:dig-glade']` etc. (systems: caches are namespaced entries in `S.chests`).
3. **Shellby's old hat** (R15): `ITEMS.shellby_hat = { name: "Shellby's old hat", kind: 'key', icon: 'icon_shellby_hat',
   desc: 'A straw sun hat, faded, much too big for anyone small. A leaf is still pinned to the band.' }`. Item-get:
   "You found an old straw hat! Much too big. Somebody small wore it a long time ago." The Nook shows it on a hat stand
   (a `hat_stand` prop on the Nook's north wall slot, or the slot atlas-home#pips-nook names if S-056 shipped one;
   grep `hatStand`). Shellby's first talk after `item:shellby_hat` (priority 60, `seen:shellby.hat`): "Oh my. Where
   did you... I wondered where that went. It never fit me either, dear. Keep it."
4. **Digby's rumours** (`js/content/dialogue/home.js`, priority 40, condition `ability:dig`; one per talk in this order,
   each sets `seen:digby.rumour.<n>` and drops a pencil cross on the home map with `mark: { realm: 'home', x, z, label:
   'Soft earth?' }` at its spot):
   1. "Something under my windmill snores. Not me. I checked." (windmill)
   2. "There's a hollow in the Woods where the soil goes soft as pudding. I'd know. I fell in." (glade)
   3. "Behind the big boulders on the Peaks, the snow's warm underneath. Warm snow is suspicious." (peaks)
   4. "The sand by the islet bridge jingles when you walk on it. Something down there is jingling back." (dune)
   5. "Right behind Shellby's bench, the ground sounds hollow. I knocked. Nobody answered. Very rude ground." (bench)
   After all five: his normal lines resume. (The Undercap has no rumour: Grumble mentions it in ss-11.)
5. **Tests** (`tests/world.test.js`, SLOW group, seeds 1-40, atlas-home §1.1 rule 9): (a) `generateWorld(seed)` and
   `generateWorld(seed, { placeDigSpots: false })` have equal `placementPrint`, `fingerprint`, `walk`, `reach`,
   `unreachable`; (b) ids in order; (c) every spot obeys `ok()`; (d) every spot reachable on foot in the post-solid
   walk grid. `home:dig-windmill` equals a11's position to 0.001 m on every seed.

#### Assets
- `shellby_hat` (R15 replaces atlas-home's felt-hat brief): code-built straw sun hat 0.5 m wide, straw `#d9bf6a`, band
  `#a8723e`, leaf `#8fbf5a`, a `cyl` brim + `sphere` crown, ≤ 200 tris. ART BRIEF: silhouette a wide flat straw sun hat
  with a leaf on the band, faded; 0.5 m; materials toon straw; static on the stand; image prompt "a small faded straw
  sun hat, far too big for a child, a green leaf pinned to the band, cosy storybook style, isolated, transparent
  background"; 3D prompt "low-poly toon straw sun hat 0.5 m wide with a leaf on the band, 200 tris".
- `icon_shellby_hat` (items atlas cell, same prompt as an icon); `prop_root_hole` code-built (a 1.4 m dark disc decal
  ringed by three root arcs `#6f4a30`, 120 tris; brief: "a small round hole between mushroom roots, dark inside, roots
  curling round its rim").

#### Acceptance criteria
- World test rule 9 (a)-(d) passes on seeds 1-40; `tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` byte-identical.
- In-page on home with `mitts`: each of the five mounds digs and gives its reward; acorn a11 is hidden before and
  wakeable after; the hat appears on the Nook stand and Shellby says his line once.
- Digby gives the five rumours in order, one per talk, each adding its map cross.
- The Undercap hole shows its Look line and no portal prompt (no `undercap` realm yet).
- `test:playthrough` unchanged (nothing visible changes before `ability:dig`). Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (new pass group), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs tools` (add "home dig spots pay out", "a11 hidden until dug"), `node tests/inpage.mjs wp9`.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope
- The Undercap realm and its portal (S-194); Grumble's ss-11 line (S-195); memory 4's hat (S-212 era).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a mound lands where row 16's acorn ring rule rejects it; rule 4 makes this pass yield to earlier passes, never
the reverse. Rollback: revert; prints never changed.

<a id="s-182"></a>
### S-182 · feat: root-gates: underground fast travel between lands, ferried by Lumbra
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-080, S-181a · Size: M (about 460 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden (home's gate shows sealed until S-191b) · Bible: [systems#root-gates](../bible/systems.md#root-gates), [systems#travel](../bible/systems.md#travel), [atlas-far#root-gate-network](../bible/atlas-far.md#root-gate-network), [atlas-home#placePortals](../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals), [atlas-isles#kit-root-gate](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-root-gate), [atlas-far#chart](../bible/atlas-far.md#chart)

#### Why
At 100 hours a world must fold up. Root-gates are the Act III fast travel: one per Wide Blue land, joined under the
sea through the Rootdeep, and after Lumbra is calmed she carries Pip between any two that are open. They also make the
underground map agree with the sea chart (the spurs run on the same bearings).

#### What
1. **Gate object `root_gate`** (`js/gates.js`, model `kit_rootgate` from S-082 or `gate_root_gate`): sealed look (a
   knot of roots round a burrow sealed with a disc carved with a gold spiral) and open look (seal sunk 0.4 m, warm light
   `#ffcf7a` inside). First-person cue: a deep creak within 10 m every 6 s while sealed.
   - Land side, sealed: `[T] Dig · Root-gate` with `ability:dig` (1.0 s, `digAt` from S-181a) → sets
     `S.travel['gate:<realm>'] = true` and `gate:<realm>:rootgate`. Without the Paws: "Sealed with roots."
   - **Home's gate** (R23): `[E] Kindle · Root-gate` with the Lantern (no Paws yet in ch13), shown when
     `flag:act2done` and the `rootdeep` realm is not hidden; before that it is sealed scenery with "Sealed with roots."
   - Open: `[E] Enter · Root-gate` opens the **root-gate menu**.
2. **Placement:**
   - Home: append entry #9 `home:portal-rootgate` to `placePortals` (row 3, `new RNG(7151 * 61 + 11)`): the root arch at
     `near(windmill + 7 m on bearing 315°, 0, 5)`, exit spawn `rootgate` 1.5 m out on its facing side; `to: { realm:
     'rootdeep', spawn: 'gate-home' }` (R21). Rule 9 world test on seeds 1-40 (append-only; entries 1-8 unchanged).
   - Isles: wire the sealed stubs their finds steps placed (`placeIsleRootGate`, atlas-isles `seed * 71 + 17`):
     Duskwick (52, −112), Pinewhistle (−150, 40), Archwater (−60, 110), Threemills (90, −150); each spawn `rootgate`.
     If a stub is missing, place it here on the same stream and note it in the PR.
   - Rootdeep: eight gate rooms at the spur ends of S-175 with a `kit_rootgate` knot each, ids `rootdeep:gate-<land>`
     for `home` (−118, −58), `pinewhistle` (−100, −242), `kettle` (231, −96), `archwater` (231, 96), `duskwick`
     (96, 231), `glassway` (0, 250), `saltglass` (−96, 231), `threemills` (−231, 96); spawn `gate-<land>` at the room
     centre facing back down the spur; Lumbra's talk point `rootdeep.ferry-<land>` = centre + 3 m (used by S-185).
     These are recipe anchors (prints refreshed, realm hidden).
3. **Rules** (`js/travel.js`):
   - The Rootdeep side of every gate is open from `flag:lumbraCalm`. Before it, the Rootdeep's `gate-home` room leads
     only back home, and home's gate leads only to `gate-home`.
   - A land is in the menu when `S.travel['gate:<realm>']` is set **and** its realm is not hidden.
   - Menu (from a land): "The Rootdeep" (arrive at that land's `gate-<land>` room) and, after `lumbraCalm`, every other
     opened land ("Lumbra carries you" card). From a Rootdeep gate room: that room's land first, then every other
     opened land. Not in a fight, scene, dungeon or Dream; not while airborne.
   - **Ferry** (R36): card "Lumbra carries you through the roots" for 2.0 s (the `load_rootdeep` card with a caption
     strip; no new image), then `RealmManager.enter(<land>, 'rootgate', 'ferry')`.
   - New effect op in `js/cond.js`: `rootgates:visited` sets `S.travel['gate:<land>']` for every land with a root-gate
     whose `realm:<land>.visited` holds (S-191b runs it at `stoneSites`, R4). Unit-tested here.
   - `S.travel` keys `gate:<realm>` (systems save-v8 format); condition `travel:gate:<realm>`.
4. **Chart Under tab** (atlas-far#chart; art `ui_chart_under` from S-177): shown from `flag:ch13` and only when
   `rootdeep` is not hidden; the Taproot at the centre, eight spurs at the chart bearings, each spur's root-door icon
   lit when that gate is open; a lit icon is a travel target (same menu).
5. **Solver:** root-gate edges between the land and its Rootdeep gate room, `needs` = `travel:gate:<land>`
   (land side) and `flag:lumbraCalm` for land-to-land ferries.
6. **Debug:** `G.debug.travel.openGate(land)`, `G.debug.travel.gates() → [...]`.

#### Assets
None new (kit_rootgate from S-082, `ui_chart_under` from S-177, `load_rootdeep` from S-177). If S-082 did not build
`kit_rootgate`, build it here from [systems#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates) `gate_root_gate` (torus of
brown tubes + disc, 900 tris) with that ART BRIEF.

#### Acceptance criteria
- World test rule 9 on seeds 1-40 for `placePortals` entry 9; home prints byte-identical.
- In-page (debug flags): dig the Duskwick gate with the Paws → menu lists "The Rootdeep" → arrive at
  `gate-duskwick` facing down the spur; with `lumbraCalm`, the menu from Duskwick lists home and Threemills when
  those gates are open, and the ferry card shows 2.0 s ± 0.2.
- Before `lumbraCalm` no land-to-land ferry is offered; with `rootdeep` hidden, home's gate is sealed scenery.
- `rootgates:visited` opens exactly the visited lands (unit).
- Every gate spawn is on walkable ground (realm suite) and the Rootdeep's POI total now reaches ≥ 116 (S-180's count
  test raised to its full bound).
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/world.test.js`, `bun test tests/travel.test.js` (menu rules, effect op), `bun test tests/cond.test.js`,
  `bun test tests/progress-graph.test.js`, `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js` (gate rooms, POI ≥ 116).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs tools` ("dig a root-gate", "ferry between two gates"), `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope
- Lumbra as a ferry character at the gates (S-185), opening gates on visited isles at `stoneSites` (S-191b), the Kettle,
  Saltglass and Glassway surface gates (their finds steps: S-205 for the Glassway).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a ferry starts while a boss intro or scene runs; the menu checks the shipped "busy" guard. Rollback: revert;
nothing player-visible changes while `rootdeep` is hidden.

<a id="s-183"></a>
### S-183 · feat: the Rootdeep Heart rooms 1-7: Sunglass beams grow root bridges
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-181a, S-160, S-053, S-178 · Size: M (about 470 lines with the map data) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [dungeons#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6), [dungeons#format](../bible/dungeons.md#format), [dungeons#reach](../bible/dungeons.md#reach), [dungeons#solver](../bible/dungeons.md#solver), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [charter#tone](../bible/charter.md#tone)

#### Why
The Heart is chapter 13's dungeon, two hours under the Taproot. Its signature is the **light-root**: Pip's Sunglass
beam (Act II's last tool) wakes a sleepy root into a glowing bridge for 20 s, so an old tool learns a new trick before
the new tool (the Paws) is found in its middle rooms.

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/rootdeep_heart.js`: the **whole** composite map `rootdeep_heart L1` (41 × 43 tiles),
   the legend extension (`j` light-root, `d` soft earth, `o` worm hole), the full room table r1-r14 and the hints,
   copied **verbatim** from [dungeons#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6) (S-049's `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` compares them;
   S-184 edits only the r14 `o` tiles per R7, in both the bible and the repo). Fields: `id: 'rootdeep_heart'`, `name:
   'The Rootdeep Heart'`, sub-line "beneath the Taproot", `kind: 'dungeon'`, `tile: 2`, `palette: 'rootdeep_heart'`
   (stone `#8a5a3c`, trim `#1c1a24`, accent `#ffcf7a`, teal glow `#7be0c8`, dark tint `#1c1a24`), `music:
   'rootdeep_heart'`, `light: { ambient: 0.45, dark: ['r2', 'r7'] }`, `parent: 'rootdeep'`, `door: 'heart_door'`,
   `needsOnEntry: ['lantern', 'sunglass']`, realm grid 102 m.
2. **Scope of this step:** entities, waves and chests of **r1-r7** only (`&` map r2, `%` Chime Stone r6, `r3:c1`, `r4:c2`,
   `r4:k1`, `r7:k1`, the r3/r4/r5/r7 light-roots, the locked door (25,8)). Rooms r8-r14 are compiled as geometry with
   their entities absent; the r7 south door (5,16) is replaced for now by a root wall entity `r7:seal` ("Roots grow
   thick here. Not yet.") that S-184 removes. The solver runs in `scope: ['r1'..'r7']` mode: map, Chime Stone, `c1`, `c2`,
   both small keys and the exit are reachable; keys unused inside the scope are allowed only in partial mode.
3. **Light-root entity** (`js/dungeon-mod.js`, used by dungeons, Hollows and the overworld): one entity per straight run
   of `j` tiles (`{ id, tiles: [[c,r]…], axis }`). States `dim` (lying in the pit, 0.5 m thick, `#5a4a3a`, not
   walkable) → `rising` (1.0 s, `bridge_rise` sound) → `lit` (a 1.8 m-wide flat-topped glowing deck `#ffcf7a` with teal
   flecks `#7be0c8`, a `W.bridges` deck record with `active: true`) for **20 s**, flashing for the last 3 s (a 4 Hz
   emissive blink plus a soft tick every 0.25 s as its sound twin) → `sinking` (0.6 s) → `dim`. A Sunglass beam on any
   tile of the run for 0.5 s (accumulated while the beam is held) starts it; beaming a lit root resets its timer to 20 s.
   If the root sinks under Pip he is put back on his last safe tile with no damage (dungeons decision 3). Entity state
   is not saved (it always resets to `dim`).
4. **The overworld crevasse** (S-180's inert `light_root` record across G3 for `rootdeep:c10`): now uses the entity
   (outdoor deck height 0 m over the 4 m-deep crevasse, run 12 m). The solver marks c10 reachable with `sunglass`.
5. **Solver timer model** (`js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`): a light-root edge is open for 20 s after a beam from a tile
   with line of sight along the run's axis (beam reach 9 tiles, dungeons#reach); crossing cost = run tiles × 2 m /
   run speed (`TM.run`); the edge is valid only if the crossing takes < 20 s − 1 s margin. Every crossing in d6 is
   ≤ 7 tiles (< 4 s).
6. **Waves** (S-052's room waves; respawn only on re-entry from outside; `clear` doors stay open): r2 Glow Grub ×2 at
   (12,6), (18,6); r4 Glow Grub ×4 at (33,2), (37,2), (33,6), (37,6) + Drip Bat ×1 from the ceiling; r5 Drip Bat ×2 at
   (27,10), (27,13); r6 Pebble Mimic ×3 at (13,10), (17,10), (13,14); r7 Glow Grub ×2 on the strips. The room notes win
   over the summary row's counts (R47).
7. **Dark rooms** r2 and r7: ambient 0.22 (R8, charter decision 3), wall-lanterns `r2:l1-l3` optional (Kindle); the
   Lantern lights itself on entry (systems: auto-lit in dark rooms). Check: mean frame luminance ≥ 0.10 at each dark
   room's entrance with the Lantern off.
8. **Signs and text** (`js/text/en/rootdeep.js`): r1 sign `?`(3,3) "Down here nobody can see. So you're just like me.
   — D." (Digby's careful letters); roots on r1's walls throb gold every 2 s (the heartbeat, `sfx.heartbeat` soft
   60 bpm). Hints 1-3 verbatim for Ask the Tree.
9. **Portal** `rootdeep:heart` (realm `rootdeep`, anchor `w:heartDoor` (0, 9.5), the Taproot's south root door, a
   `glowbud_door`-style arch of roots 2.4 m wide): `[E] Enter · The Rootdeep Heart`, **always shown**, `locked: { needs:
   'met:gran_burrows', say: "Digby's Gran first, I think. She's expecting you." }` (R22). `to: { realm:
   'rootdeep_heart', spawn: 'entry' }`; exit `X` tiles → Rootdeep spawn `heart` (0, 13) facing 180°. Fix systems.md's
   portal row (`quest:mq-ch13>=2` → the R22 rule) in this PR.
10. **Music** `rootdeep_heart` (art-audio#track-data: `{ from: 'depths', transpose: -12, voices: { lead: 'cello' } }`)
    plus a slow heartbeat drum at 60 bpm and the `drips` bed.
11. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/rootdeep_heart.json`; realm-suite entry with 4 views (r1 from `@`, r2 lit
    root, r6 gallery, r14 empty arena from its door).

#### Assets
- `int_light_root` code-built per the d6 art table (tapering root 0.5 m thick, rises and flattens to a 1.8 m deck;
  60 tris per tile) with the ART BRIEF from [dungeons#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6) ("Art (d6)") in the PR; slot
  commented out.
- `int_glowcap` code-built (mushroom 0.6-2.0 m, cap `#7be0c8` emissive, cream stem; the home glowshroom recoloured).
- Walls use `tex_rootwall` (S-177's `rootdeep_rootwall`, same file, alias key); fallback `tex_dungeon_root`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes (repo map equals the bible map, character for character).
- The partial solver reaches map, Chime Stone, `c1`, `c2`, both small keys and the exit; no soft-lock in r1-r7.
- In-page: a beam held 0.5 s on r2's root grows the bridge in 1.0 s; it lasts 20 s, flashes for 3 s, sinks; standing on
  it as it sinks returns Pip to his last safe tile with full hearts. The overworld crevasse root works the same.
- The Heart door says Gran's line until `met:gran_burrows`, then enters; the exit returns to (0, 13).
- Dark-room luminance ≥ 0.10 with the Lantern off; ≤ 120 draw calls per room view.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js`, `bun test tests/dungeon-compile.test.js` (partial-scope solver; light-root
  timer edge; a fixture where a 12-tile root fails the timer), `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (add "light-root grows, lasts 20 s, sinks safely", "Heart door locked until Gran").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope
- Rooms r8-r14 entities, the Paws chest and the boss door (S-184); Lumbra (S-185); ch13's quest (S-191a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the beam's 0.5 s accumulation is hard to hold on a narrow root in TP; the beam's 18 m reach and the run-wide hit
area keep it easy, and the in-page test aims from the room's south strip. Rollback: revert; the realm is hidden.

<a id="s-184"></a>
### S-184 · feat: the Rootdeep Heart rooms 8-14: soft earth and the Warden's hall
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-183 · Size: M (about 360 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [dungeons#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities), [dungeons#state](../bible/dungeons.md#state), [creatures#boss-lumbra](../bible/creatures.md#boss-lumbra)

#### Why
The second half of the Heart gives Pip the Digging Paws in r8 and immediately asks him to use them: dig through walls,
dig out a buried root tip so the beam can wake it, dig a key out of a pillar of earth. Then the Root Key, the larder
and the Warden's round hall.

#### What
1. **Remove `r7:seal`** and add the r8-r14 entities, waves and chests exactly as [dungeons#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6)'s
   room notes:
   - r8 The Paws Chamber: treasure chest `$` (5,20) gives `mitts` (S-181a item, ability `dig`) with the item-get line
     "You got the Digging Paws! Stand by soft earth and press {tool} to dig. Digby would be proud." (R9: `{tool}`, not
     `{interact}`; fix dungeons.md's line in this PR). Mounds `r8:d1-d3` (8,19)-(8,21): dig any one to reach door (10,20).
   - r9 The Burrow Maze: column 15 sealed by walls and mounds (15,17), (15,22), (15,23); `r9:c3` (19,17) walled in by
     mounds (18,17), (19,18); Pebble Mimic ×2 at (12,18), (18,20); locked door (20,20) east (`r7:k1`).
   - r10 The Lumen Pit: root `r10:j` (24-26,20) whose tip is buried under mound `r10:d1` (23,20) (`buriedTip: true`:
     the root ignores beams until the mound is dug); Drip Bat ×2; door (30,20).
   - r11 The Knot Hall: Root Key chest `K` (35,17) on an island (35,18); root `r11:j` (35,19-20) beamed from (35,21);
     mound `r11:d1` (35,23) blocks the south door (35,24); door (32,16) north to r12.
   - r12 The Root-Gate Hall: a sleeping root-gate arch (decor only); switch `r12:s1` (35,9) opens door (35,8) to r4 for
     good (`S.dungeons.rootdeep_heart.open['35,8']`); Glow Grub ×2.
   - r13 Lumbra's Larder: leaf-mould heaps (decor, `int_leafmould` code-built), four root pillars `I`, gem chest
     `r13:c4` (38,30), boss door `B` (30,28) (Root Key).
   - r14 The Heart Hollow (ceiling 14 m): arena 19 × 17 tiles, boss spawn `M` (20,32), the glowing Heart root (r 2 m)
     at the centre, **six worm holes at the R7 tiles (20,28), (24,30), (24,34), (20,36), (16,34), (16,30)** (8.0-8.9 m
     from `M`, the closest tiles to creatures.md's 9 m hexagon). Edit dungeons.md's d6 map and r14 row to these tiles
     in this PR (re-render with `tools-dungeons` if that tool ships in the repo; otherwise edit the map block by hand and
     re-run the map test). Each hole: `int_worm_hole` (2.4 m ring of loose earth) with a soft-earth rim (`d` entity, dig
     disabled outside the fight). `X` (20,42) → Rootdeep spawn `heart`.
   - Until S-185 the arena is empty: entering shows "The hollow is quiet. Something very long is asleep further down."
     and the exit works.
2. **Soft earth in dungeons**: the map's `d` tiles are `soft_earth` entities that call `digAt` (S-181a); a dug tile
   becomes floor (`S.dungeons.rootdeep_heart.open['<c>,<r>'] = true`, so digs survive leaving); before `mitts` is
   owned the prompt is the Look line. Soft earth blocks beams (it is solid until dug).
3. **Solver** (full scope now): r1 → … → r14 → `X` exactly as the bible's "Solver notes"; every gem chest (c1-c4 = 160
   gems), map, Chime Stone, Paws, Root Key, the boss door and the exit reachable; no soft-lock in any order the solver
   tries (dig choices in r8/r9 are interchangeable).
4. **Dungeon state** (`S.dungeons.rootdeep_heart`): `keys`, `bigKey`, `map`, `compass`, `open {}` (doors, digs, the
   r12 shortcut), `levers {}`. Leaving and re-entering keeps it; dizzy wakes Pip at `@` with every state kept.
5. **Prints** refreshed for `rootdeep_heart`.

#### Assets
- `int_soft_earth` (shared with S-181a's gate model), `int_worm_hole` (code-built 2.4 m ring of dark earth with small
  pebbles, sunken centre; fallback a dark disc decal), `int_leafmould` (three flattened spheres `#6b4a32` with leaf
  decals; 120 tris). ART BRIEFs for `int_worm_hole` and `int_leafmould` in the PR (silhouette, size, palette `#3a2a20`
  / `#6b4a32` / pebbles `#8a7a6a`, poly 200 / 1,000, image prompt "a ring of loose dark earth and small pebbles around a
  round hole in a cavern floor, soft painterly", 3D prompt "low-poly toon earth ring 2.4 m, sunken centre, 200 tris").

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the R7 hole tiles in both the bible and the repo.
- Full solver: all 4 gem chests, map, Chime Stone, Paws, Root Key, boss door and exit reachable; no soft-lock.
- In-page: taking `$` gives the Paws and equips them; digging `r8:d2` opens the way; `r10`'s root ignores the beam
  until `r10:d1` is dug; `r12:s1` opens (35,8) for good; a reload keeps every dig.
- Every room view ≤ 120 draw calls in both cameras.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js`, `bun test tests/dungeon-compile.test.js` (full d6 solve, dig edges, buried-tip
  root).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (add "Paws from r8", "buried root tip", "digs persist").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope
- Lumbra and the handover (S-185); the ch13 quest steps (S-191a).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: digging the only mound to a key in the wrong order soft-locks; the solver tries every dig order and every mound
is single-use and harmless to leave. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-184b"></a>

### S-184b · feat: The Rootdeep Heart grows the Sap Cistern and the Root-Keeper's Nook: the roots hold their breath (depth rule R14)

Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-184, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d6-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d6-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d6](../bible/dungeons.md#d6)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-184 and before S-185, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M21 · The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart and Lumbra**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so The Rootdeep Heart played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
The Rootdeep Heart's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d6-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d6-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `rootdeep_heart LA` (`at` [43,0]): r15 The Sap Cistern; `rootdeep_heart LB` (`at` [43,10]): r16 The Root-Keeper's Nook (dark); extent 54 × 43, realm grid 124 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r15 `^`(1,7) ↔ r13 (35,26), under roots that part while the new light-root `r13:j1` is grown; r16 `^`(1,7) ↔ r10 (21,22), sealed until the flip.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r13 (35,26) `.` → `^`; r13 (35,27) `.` → `j`; r13 (35,28) `.` → `j`; r10 (21,22) `.` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Sap Valve** `r15:s1` (8,1), flag `rh.sap`, line `dg.rh.flip` "The valve squeals shut. The roots nearby dim a little, as if holding their breath.". From then on: **Q = r11**: the soft earth at the south door (35,23) regrows (dig again) and r11's light-root lasts 8 s instead of 20. **P = r10**: its light-root (24-26,20) lasts 8 s and the mound (23,20) regrows: beam, run west, dig from the root's west end before it sinks, reach the unsealed stair (21,22). Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (30,28) opens on `key:K && latch:r16:s1 && clear:r13`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r13 becomes `dark: true` with a new wave (Glow Grub ×2, Dimling ×1; `clear` for `B`); beam the new light-root `r13:j1` (35,27)-(35,28) to part the roots over the stair for 20 s.
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "Lumbra's larder has roots over a hatch." / "Beam the little root by the larder hatch, go down to the Sap Cistern, cross on the root, dig, and turn the Sap Valve." · "The roots tire quickly now. Be quick." /
   "In the Lumen Pit, beam the root, run west and dig the mound before the root sinks; the stair is on the west side." (text ids `dg.rootdeep_heart.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 16 rooms, 14 counted, ≥ 23 beats (planned 25), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 6); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.
9. **Two waves from Act III** (dungeons.md R14; CANON §10.3): every combat room of this dungeon gets a second wave of mixed archetypes at the land's pattern tier (creatures.md#pattern-tiers), spawning 1.5 s after the first is calmed, at most 2 attackers at once; each room's `clear` waits for both. List the second waves in each room's entry in `dungeons.md`.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_root_valve`** (code-built, ≤ 120 tris; the flip prop): a valve wheel carved from root wood on a glowing amber sap pipe. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 1.0 m; palette `#5a3d2b` `#ffcf7a` `#6f8f3a`; toon materials; 120 / 500 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a valve wheel carved from root wood on a glowing amber sap pipe, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a valve wheel carved from root wood on a glowing amber sap pipe, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 500
  triangles, 1.0 m". Manifest key `int_root_valve` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `rootdeep_heart_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-185); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.

<a id="s-185"></a>
### S-185 · feat: Lumbra the Long, the 14 m earthworm who keeps the roots healthy
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-184b, S-045 · Size: M (about 480 lines; S-185b if over) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [creatures#boss-lumbra](../bible/creatures.md#boss-lumbra), [creatures#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def), [creatures#boss-grammar](../bible/creatures.md#boss-grammar), [creatures#hp-formula](../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula), [creatures#intro-handover](../bible/creatures.md#intro-handover), [cast#lumbra-the-long](../bible/cast.md#lumbra-the-long), [atlas-far#rootdeep-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-states), [side-content#plushies](../bible/side-content.md#plushies)

#### Why
Lumbra is Act III's first Warden: not a monster but a worried root-keeper who has been rushing round the dimmed roots
for a hundred years and could not stop. Calming her relights the Heart, warms the whole Rootdeep and gives the
root-gates their ferry. Her fight is the Paws' graduation: dig the rim of her hole and she pops out, wobbling.

#### What
1. **Boss def** `BOSS_DEFS['rootdeep.lumbra']` (`js/boss/lumbra.js`), save key `rootdeep_heart:lumbra`:

   | Field | Value |
   |---|---|
   | name / title | Lumbra the Long / Root-Keeper of the Rootdeep |
   | kind | `bout` |
   | model / height / r | `boss_lumbra` / 1.2 (body thickness) / 1.1 |
   | hp | **128** (a number; she is not in a group) |
   | enrageAt / enrageAt2 / arenaChange | 0.5 / **0.33** / `holes` (the runner hook S-164 added) |
   | cool | P1 [1.4, 2.4], P2 [1.0, 1.8], P3 [0.85, 1.55] |
   | arena | anchor r14 `M` (20,32), round r 18 m, seal: the boss door drops; six holes at the R7 tiles |
   | phases | P1 (below 1.0): `arc` 50, `spray` 50 · P2 (below 0.5): `arc` 40, `spray` 40, `doublearc` 20 · P3 (below 0.33): `arc` 45, `spray` 35, `doublearc` 20 |

   Moves (wind = drawn tell ≥ 0.55 s; damage always 1 HP):
   - `surface` (forced before every other move): one hole's dirt shakes with a positional rumble first (sound before
     shape, archetype burrower rule), then a ring r 1.6 m on that hole; wind 0.9 s, burst 0.3 s. She is untargetable
     underground (lock-on skips her).
   - `arc`: she leans toward another hole; a 2.2 m-wide stripe from hole to hole is drawn; wind 0.8 s; damage only
     during the 0.6 s landing; then her middle stays arched over the floor with her glasses slipping: **2.5 s gold**.
   - `spray`: she shakes her head, clods gather; fan of 5 lines (7 in P2), clods 8 m/s; wind 0.6 s. Clods are glint
     shots (swat back for no damage to her, just a pop).
   - `doublearc` (P2 only): an arc, the second lane drawn as the first ends (0.6 s ahead); after the second landing
     2.5 s gold.
   - **The Paws opening:** while she is up at a hole after a `spray`, `[T] Dig · Soft earth` on that hole's rim (1.0 s,
     `digAt`) pops her fully out, wobbling: **2.0 s gold**. Only this hole's rim is diggable, and only then.
   - At most two threats live (a stripe and clods never overlap: `spray` never starts while an arc stripe is live).
   - **P3 at 33 %, arena change `holes`** (creatures.md#boss-lumbra): she burrows under for 3 s (a long rumble circling the
     ring, no threat, untargetable). Holes 2 (60°) and 5 (240°) **collapse** (the hole mesh fills with a soil mound in 1.0
     s; each becomes a soft-earth spot that yields 5 gems once to `[T] Dig`); two **new holes** open at radius 13 m at
     150° and 330° (each drawn as a shaking dirt ring for 2 s before it opens, with the soft-earth rim of the others).
     Arcs from a ring hole to an outer hole are up to 15 m long; after them her middle stays up **3.0 s gold**. `surface`
     keeps its 0.9 s tell; the hole set lives in the def (`holes: [...6], holesP3: [...6]`) so the dizzy reset restores
     the first set.
   - Body: 12 segments on a spline; up to 9 m of her shows at once; passive contact shoves (5.5 m/s), never hurts.
2. **Intro**: 2.4 s, name card "Lumbra the Long" over "Root-Keeper of the Rootdeep"; music `warden` with a cello lead
   (art-audio `warden` per-Warden voice).
3. **Handover** (three lines, her portrait, she sits dizzy with glasses askew): "Oh dear, oh dear. My glasses. Has
   anyone seen my... ah. Thank you, dear." / "The roots went dim and I've been rushing round all of them, trying to keep
   them warm. I didn't mean to block the way. I just couldn't stop." / "Go on, little lamp. After this, I'll carry you
   anywhere the roots go." Effects: `set:lumbraCalm`, `boss:rootdeep_heart:lumbra` calmed, bestiary calmed 1. The
   Rootdeep's glow ramp of S-176 (0.35 → 1.0 over 8 s, outward from the Heart door) plays when Pip next stands in
   `rootdeep`; the Taproot grain brightens bottom to top; S-177's dim music layer lifts (the cello `lead2` returns).
   Scene `ch13_heart` is S-191a's (it lights knots 1-3 through S-190).
4. **Afterwards** (`def.after`): talk in the arena until Pip leaves the realm (`[E] Talk · Lumbra the Long`: "Hold on to
   the third band. That's the comfy one."), then her **resting coil** at `rootdeep.lumbra` (0, 238) in Drip Bay with a
   contented sigh every 30 s within 20 m. **Ferry visual** for S-182's root-gate menu: when Pip uses an open gate (land
   side or Rootdeep side) after `lumbraCalm`, her head rises from the gate-room floor 3 m from the knot during the 2.0 s
   ferry card's fade-in (0.8 s), and `[E] Talk · Lumbra the Long` works at `rootdeep.ferry-<land>` while Pip stands in a
   gate room (same line). Her pronoun is **she** everywhere (R1).
5. **Plushie** `plushies['rootdeep_heart:lumbra']`: 100 gems at Miso's once calmed and the shop is bought out (S-045 rule);
   desc "Fourteen metres of cuddle, folded small."; Miso's got line "A Lumbra plushie! The glasses are stitched on, so
   they never fall off~". **Rematch:** `[E] Wake · Lumbra the Long` at her resting coil (0, 238) after she naps (she
   naps once Pip has left the realm after the handover); plays the rematch card "Lumbra the Long · Rematch", loads d6
   r14 through the portal system, returns Pip to (0, 236); HP round(1.25 × 128) = **160**, enraged from 75 % and again
   from 40 % (the holes change then); first win
   60 gems once (`rematches['rootdeep_heart:lumbra']`). Dream: `null` until S-295.
6. **Bestiary** `card_boss_lumbra`: Hoot "Lumbra the Long. Measured her twice. Got two different answers. Both very
   long." / Clover "After she jumps hole to hole, her middle stays up. Bonk it!"
7. **Balance test** (S-045 harness, creatures#balance-targets: the act-opening Warden window): careful bot wins ≥ 90 %
   of 50 seeded runs in **120-180 s** losing ≤ 3 hearts with the Shiny Sword and 10 hearts, reaching P3 in every win;
   with the wooden sword ≤ 300 s; a never-dodging bot wins ≤ 50 %. Unit: every move wind ≥ 0.55 s, damage 1.
8. **Voice marker:** reserve Lumbra's "Mmm, loamy." and "and-so-on" in the content lint's marker table (her later
   lines in S-192 use them).

#### Assets
- `boss_lumbra` code-built (R48: cast.md's look wins over creatures.md's art palette): 12 spheres r 0.6 on a spline
  `#c98a7a`, ring tori `#b06f62` between, saddle band `#e0a090`, head with the shipped `face()` helper, two torus lenses
  `#f2c94c` with pale glass discs, a short moss scarf `#6f8f5a`, glowing soil specks `#ffcf7a` as a particle at the
  hole; ≤ 6,000 tris. GLB slot `models.boss_lumbra` (12 nodes `seg0`..`seg11`, head `seg0`) commented out. ART BRIEF
  from cast.md (`boss_lumbra`, batch 10) plus creatures.md's animation list in the PR.
- `portrait_lumbra` (core: she ferries from every land; art-audio bundles): cast.md's prompt; processed like S-178.
- `card_boss_lumbra` (realm `rootdeep`, 384², ≤ 45 KB): "a very long gentle pink-brown earthworm wearing tiny round
  glasses, smiling kindly, curving through glowing roots in a warm underground cavern".
- `int_lumbra_coil` reuse: the resting coil is the same model posed in a spiral (no new asset).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Balance test passes; every move's tell ≥ 0.55 s and damage exactly 1 (unit over `moves`).
- In-page: `surface` rumble plays before the ring; `arc` opens 2.5 s gold; digging the rim after a `spray` opens 2.0 s
  gold; only that rim is diggable; at most two threats live at any sampled frame.
- On the last hit: handover lines, `lumbraCalm` set, the Rootdeep glow ramp and music lift on return.
- Dizzy in the fight: Pip wakes at the dungeon entrance, Lumbra is at full HP and napping (grammar rule 7).
- At 33 % the two holes collapse and two outer holes open after a 2 s drawn ring; no threat is live during the 3 s
  burrow; live threats never exceed 2 in P3; a dizzy reset restores the first hole set.
- Rematch from (0, 238) at 160 HP pays 60 gems once; plushie stocked at 100 gems after calm.
- Ferry: her head rises in the gate room during a ferry; Talk works there.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/bosses.test.js` (def lint: wind, damage, threats, HP), `bun test tests/balance.test.js -t lumbra`
  (or the S-045 balance harness entry), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses` (add "lumbra: surface tell, arc gold, mitts opening, handover sets lumbraCalm",
  "lumbra P3 holes change at 33 %", "lumbra rematch 160 HP").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs rootdeep` ("glow ramps after lumbraCalm").

#### Out of scope
- Scene `ch13_heart` and quest steps (S-191a); knots lighting (S-190); ss-32 (S-192); the Dream (S-295).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the spline body costs draw calls; the 12 segments are one instanced mesh. Risk: digging under time pressure
feels unfair in FP; the rim prompt appears 0.3 s before the window and the opening without the Paws (`arc`) stays
available. Size: the P3 hole change pushes the step past ~500 lines; if so, ship the P3 change, the balance test and
the rematch as **S-185b** (INDEX §1.6). Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-186"></a>
### S-186 · feat: the Rootdeep's three Hollows and the Rootdeep Dash
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-180, S-073, S-183, S-181a, S-074 · Size: M (about 330 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [dungeons#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [dungeons#h14](../bible/dungeons.md#h14), [dungeons#h15](../bible/dungeons.md#h15), [dungeons#h16](../bible/dungeons.md#h16), [side-content#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash), [atlas-far#rootdeep-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-pois)

#### Why
Hollows are the bite-sized puzzles that carry heart petals; the Rootdeep gets three, one per signature mechanic of the
land (dig, dark, beam). Clover's Dash adds the land's race (course 6), so the cavern has a reason to be crossed fast.

#### What
1. **Hollows** (`js/realms/hollows/hollow_rootdeep_{1,2,3}.js`, maps copied verbatim; R38 maps atlas door ids):

   | Realm | Name | Door (S-180) | Door gate | Map and solution | Prize |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `hollow_rootdeep_1` | The Mole Hollow | `rootdeep:hollow1` (−40, 226) Drip Bay SW grotto | none (Paws inside) | [dungeons#h14](../bible/dungeons.md#h14): three root walls (rows 2, 4, 6) each with two mounds; dig one per wall; Pebble Mimic ×2 at (3,3), (7,5) | 60-gem bag `*` (5,1) |
   | `hollow_rootdeep_2` | The Dark Hollow | `rootdeep:hollow2` (98, −226) Glowcap east wall | Lantern (a `dark_mouth` before the door) | [dungeons#h15](../bible/dungeons.md#h15): dark; kindle six lanterns (1,4), (9,4), (3,6), (7,6), (1,8), (9,8): the `=` bridge rises; Dimling ×3, Drip Bat ×1 | heart petal `+` (5,1) |
   | `hollow_rootdeep_3` | The Sunroot Hollow | `rootdeep:hollow3` (240, 22) G3 east wall | Sunglass (the door is a `receiver`: 1.0 s of beam) | [dungeons#h16](../bible/dungeons.md#h16): two light-roots in a column (5,5)-(5,6) then (5,2)-(5,3), beam each from its south end | heart petal `+` (5,1) |

   Portals `rootdeep:hollow1..3` `[E] Enter · Spiral door` (`to: { realm, spawn: 'entry' }`); exit `X` back to 1.5 m in front
   of the door. Name toast on entry. Saved: `collect.hollow['hollow_rootdeep_<n>']`, petals `collect.petal
   ['hollow_rootdeep_<n>:p1']`; puzzle state in `S.dungeons['hollow_rootdeep_<n>']` until solved. Item-get lines as
   dungeons#hollows ("You found a heart petal! Six petals make a whole heart." / "+60 gems!"). Ask the Tree pairs
   verbatim ("Three walls, three digs." / "Dig one mound in each of the three root walls."; "Six lights in the dark." /
   "Kindle all six lanterns; the Dimlings are only hittable in the light."; "Sleepy roots in a line." / "Shine the
   Sunglass on the first root, cross, then shine on the second."). Dark Hollow ambient 0.22 (R8). The solved door glows
   gold outside; the map shows a gold spiral.
2. **Rootdeep Dash** (course 6, R24 and R49: atlas-far's route, 12 rings, no tool on the line): Dash post
   `rootdeep:dash` at the Burrowbottom square (−165, 14) beside bench b1, `[E] Use · Clover's Dash`. Route polyline
   (−165, 14) → (−120, 0) tunnel → (−95, −40) west shore → (−40, −95) north shore → (0, −60) north causeway → finish post
   (12, −10) by the Taproot bench. Rings 1-11 every 1/12 of the path length along the walk-grid path (F27 `findPath`
   between the waypoints), snapped to walkable cells 2.4 m wide clear; ring 12 is the finish. Record the path length in
   the PR (atlas ≈ 330 m). Medal times from par P = the careful-bot time measured by the in-page test (gold ≤ 1.10 P,
   silver ≤ 1.30 P, bronze ≤ 1.60 P), written into the course data. Rewards: first bronze 15 gems, first silver 20
   gems, first gold a ribbon on Clover's windowsill (slot "the Rootdeep", colour `#7be0c8`). Save
   `collect.dash['dash:rootdeep'] = Math.round(seconds * 10)` (tenths of a second, 1-36000; S-112's format). Clover's lines are her shipped dash lines.
   Rule: no enemy spawn point within 6 m of the route while a run is active (the route passes Underlake shore cells);
   getting dizzy during a run is impossible. Edit side-content.md's act-dash row 6 to this route in this PR.
3. **Completion**: Rootdeep Hollows row 3; the Dash row counts course 6 at top tier.

#### Assets
None new: Hollow kit, lanterns, light-roots, soft earth, Dash post and rings exist (S-073, S-112, S-181a, S-183).

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` compares the three Hollow maps; the Hollow solver proves each prize and exit reachable.
- In-page: each Hollow solves by its listed solution and pays its prize once; the petal count rises; leaving mid-puzzle
  keeps block/lantern state; the Dark Hollow's Dimlings are hittable only in light.
- The Dash runs start to finish with 12 rings in order; a skipped ring blinks; medal times are recorded from P.
- Completion counts: Hollows 3 and Dash course 6 in the Rootdeep group (stats test).
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js`, `bun test tests/dungeon-compile.test.js` (three Hollows), `bun test
  tests/stats.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` ("rootdeep hollows 1-3 solve"), `node tests/inpage.mjs activities` ("rootdeep dash:
  careful bot sets par; rings in order").

#### Out of scope
- Burrowbottom's lamps (S-192), fish spots (S-207), thornglass (S-233).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the Dash path crosses the causeway lip where a hop is needed; the route uses only walk-grid paths. Rollback:
revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-186b"></a>

### S-186b · feat: the Rootdeep's Hollows get their second stage, and The Dark Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-186, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h15](../bible/dungeons.md#h15), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-186.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M21 · The Rootdeep: the land, the Heart and Lumbra**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H14 The Mole Hollow, H16 The Sunroot Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H14 The Mole Hollow** (`hollow_rootdeep_1`): a receiver lock: hold the Sunglass beam on receiver `R` (9,1) for 1.0 s; condition `lit:[R]`.
   - **H16 The Sunroot Hollow** (`hollow_rootdeep_3`): the petal sits under a soft-earth mound at (5,1): from the end of the second light-root (5,2), dig it (1.0 s) before the root sinks; condition `dug`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H15 The Dark Hollow** (`hollow_rootdeep_2`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h15](../bible/dungeons.md#h15) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Root Stair**: (dark) beam the light-root (13-17,5); cross within 20 s; dig the mound (18,5) from the root's east end; exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 18** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in the Rootdeep: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "Dark down here. Not cold, though. Someone keeps it warm. I left that alone. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.rootdeep`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:rootdeep`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "Beam, run, dig." / "Shine the Sunglass on the long root, run across and dig the mound at its far end before the root sinks.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:rootdeep`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_rootdeep`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.

<a id="s-187"></a>
### S-187 · chore: close M21: ARCHITECTURE on cross-realm post-passes
Milestone M21 · Depends on: S-182, S-185, S-186 (and every M21 step, splits included), S-001, S-030, S-186b, S-184b · Size: S (docs and records) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [charter#close-out](../bible/charter.md#close-out), [charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone), [charter#progress-template](../bible/charter.md#progress-template)

#### Why
M21 built a whole hidden land, a dungeon, a Warden and a tool that reaches back into four shipped realms. The next
milestones (and every later tool) depend on one rule written down clearly: how a later tool adds finds to an earlier
land without moving that land's frozen prints.

#### What
Follow [charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone) steps 1-10 in order, and:
1. **`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`**, new section "Cross-realm post-passes": (a) the rule: a realm's prints freeze when it is
   unhidden; afterwards only post-passes add to it, each on `new RNG(seed * k + c)` with the table of streams
   (home: atlas-home#passes rows 1-26 + v1-v3; isles: atlas-isles `seed * 71 + c`, `seed * 73 + c`, `seed * 79 + 1`; far
   realms: atlas-far#far-passes rows 71·3 … 109·21); (b) a pass writes only its own list, restores `W.walk/W.reach`,
   attaches solids/decks through `attachPost(W)` after generation; (c) its test: the pass-off comparison on the realm's
   seed plus seeds 1-20 (1-40 for home); (d) worked example: S-181a's `placeBuried` on the isles and S-181b's
   `placeDigSpots` on home; (e) how a gate find is registered with the solver so the "what each tool opens" report
   includes it.
2. ARCHITECTURE also gains: the `cavern` realm kind (sky off, fixed lighting, `over` ceilings, rock-chunk skip), the
   light-root entity, the `soft_earth` gate and `digAt`, root-gate travel rules and the `rootgates:visited` effect,
   F27 schedules (if S-174 did not already add it).
3. **`docs/DESIGN.md`**: the Digging Paws in the tools table; root-gates in the travel table; Lumbra in the Wardens
   table (128 HP, rematch 160, second escalation at 33 %); the "what each tool opens" table pasted from the solver report.
4. **`docs/roadmap/progress.md`** entry (template): steps S-174 … S-187 with splits S-181a/S-181b; hours "0 h visible,
   ≈ 3.5 h built"; realms shipped none, hidden `rootdeep`, `rootdeep_heart`, three Hollows; budgets for the Rootdeep's 4
   views and d6's room views; `rootdeep` bundle size; save v9 unchanged.
5. **Release check**: `rootdeep` is still `hidden: true`; home's root-gate is sealed scenery; nothing new visible
   before `act2done` except schedules.
6. Bible edits owed by M21 steps are present (R1 partially, R7, R9, R13, R16, R22, R24, R36, R48; the rest land in M22).
   List any missing one and make it here (docs only).

#### Assets
None (no brief batch scheduled for M21; Rootdeep briefs ship in batch 10 at S-197).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every close-out step 1-10 done; the full local suite results recorded (pass counts, wall times).
- ARCHITECTURE's new section exists with the stream table and the worked example.
- `bun test tests/docs.test.js` passes.

#### Verification plan
`free -h` (≥ 6 GB available), then in order: `bun test`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa`;
`bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:opening`; `bun run test:playthrough`; `node tests/inpage.mjs <suite>` for every
matrix entry (wp1, wp2:1/3-3/3, wp3-wp7, wp8:1/3-3/3, wp9, core, realm, npc, rootdeep, tools, dungeons, bosses,
species, activities, …); `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>` for every shipped chapter in story order.

#### Out of scope
Feature code of any kind.

#### Risk & rollback
None beyond docs; revert the PR.

<a id="s-188"></a>
### S-188 · feat: variant realms reuse a land's geometry with a new skin; the Remembered Isle skeleton
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-027, S-018 · Size: M (about 480 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.remembered.hidden = true` · Bible: [systems#variants](../bible/systems.md#variants), [systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [atlas-home#remembered-isle](../bible/atlas-home.md#remembered-isle), [atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes), [art-audio#variants](../bible/art-audio.md#variants), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

#### Why
Four of the game's places are the home island seen differently: a hundred years ago, by moonlight, on the Hundred-Year
Night and as Hoot's sketch of Pinewhistle. A variant realm reuses its parent's frozen World and changes only its skin,
so those places cost data, not new geometry. The first is **the Remembered Isle**, where Act III's memories play
(vision item **The Remembered Isle, chapter 13 and Grumble's Undercap**).

#### What
1. **Variant kind** (`js/realms/index.js`, `js/realms/variant.js`): `REALMS[id] = { kind: 'variant', variantOf, skin }`.
   `generateRealm(id)` calls the parent's generator with the parent's seed and options, so the World's placement print,
   fingerprint, `walk`, `reach` and `unreachable` **deep-equal the parent's**; then applies the skin:
   - `palette`: a palette record (S-027 shape) plus the **grade uniform** `{ sat, tint, k }` (art-audio: one shared
     colour-grade uniform on the toon materials, no new shader programs; home's grade is the identity).
   - `scatterRemap`: prop/deco type → replacement type with the same footprint and collider (none for `remembered`).
   - `hide`: list of post-pass lists and prop types not drawn, their colliders `active = false`.
   - `enemies` (`'none'` or a table), `npcs` (replaces the parent's NPC set; `[]` here), `music`, `ambience`, `tod`
     (frozen), `exit` (portal id + paired spawn), `post` (one variant post-pass on its own stream), `lookAt: false`
     (NPCs never look at Pip), `playerShadow: false`.
   - Variants never mark the parent's regions visited (`visited` untouched) and their finds count in the parent land's
     completion group.
2. **`remembered`** (systems registry row; R30, R50):
   ```js
   remembered: { id: 'remembered', name: 'The Remembered Isle', kind: 'variant', variantOf: 'home', seed: 7151,
     hidden: true, open: 'flag:lumbraCalm', arrive: 'knot', tod: 0.70, bundle: 'remembered', enemies: 'none',
     skin: 'remembered', parent: 'rootdeep', exit: { portal: 'remembered:knot', to: { realm: 'rootdeep', spawn: 'taproot' } },
     views: [ /* below */ ] }
   ```
   Skin (art-audio#palettes `remembered` row wins on colours and fog, R50; atlas-home on what is placed and hidden):
   grade `{ sat: 0.55, tint: '#e9d8b0', k: 0.45 }`; sky by day `#c9b07a` / `#f2e2bc`; fog `[30, 230, '#e9d8b0', .35]`;
   sun `#ffd9a0`; hemisphere `#fff0d0` / `#6f5a44`; water `#b8a06a` deep → `#e2c9a0` shallow; terrain layers = home's
   (graded, no new textures). Hidden: every home post-pass list rows 1-26 **except** the Stairfoot (row 14's platform) and
   the old beacons (row 17, shown whole below); also the shop awning, bunting and the parade lanterns. Cottage roofs
   recoloured thatch `#c9a46b` (a per-instance tint). Music `remembered` (art-audio#track-data: `{ from: 'village',
   bpm: 92, voices: { lead: 'musicbox', comp: 'harp' }, drop: ['perc'], add: { pad: { voice: 'pad', vol: 0.5 } }, rev:
   0.4, sparkle: false, startLayer: 3 }`); ambience = home beds with `ev` rates × 0.6 and `ambChime` × 3
   (`remembered_<region>`). No enemies; no gems or chests; dizzy cannot happen (no attacks).
3. **Variant pass v1** (`new RNG(7151 * 193 + 127)`, atlas-home row v1; writes `W.remembered = { scaffolds, beacons,
   stair, stalls, lampPosts, acorns: [] }`, nothing else; runs only for `remembered`):
   - **Scaffolds:** three timber rings round the Great Tree at tree-local heights 4, 8 and 12 m (radii = the trunk
     radius at that height + 1.2 m), four ladders between rings at bearings 45°, 135°, 225°, 315°, a lamp hung every 30°
     on each ring (36 lamps, instanced `remembered_lamp`, `nightGlow`). No collider (seen, not climbed).
   - **Whole beacons:** the four old beacons stand whole and lit on the Summit Crystal Cairn's crystal, the lighthouse cap,
     Grumble's Crown top and the windmill cap (S-169's `oldBeacons` anchors, a lamp cage glowing `#ffcf6a`).
   - **The intact Starstair:** four flights of 22 floating stone steps (step 1.2 × 0.6 × 0.3 m, rise 0.45 m, run 0.9 m)
     from the Stairfoot toward bearing 45°, flights 1-3 as atlas-home#starstair describes and a fourth continuing up
     until it fades into the sky at 120 m; glowing edges `#ffcf6a`; one instanced type `remembered_step`. Memory scripts
     address them as set `stair` (steps 1-88) for `memory.lamps` (S-190a).
   - **The Lamplighter market:** six moth-folk stalls in a ring on the plaza at r 10-14, bearings 30°, 90°, 150°, 210°,
     270°, 330°, each nudged to `ok()` (atlas-home §1.3) and clear of the z = 9 line.
   - **Lamp posts** at the nine waymarkers (g5's `waymarkers` positions, offset 1.2 m to the path side).
4. **Spawn `knot`** (where a memory starts when it has no spawn of its own): `near((0, 13), 0, 3)` facing the Tree; the
   exit mote is S-190a's. `G.debug.realm('remembered')` and `?realm=remembered` enter it from anywhere.
5. **Views** (realm suite): densest `{x: 0, z: 22, yaw: 0, pitch: 6}` (plaza market and scaffolds), landmark `{x: 70,
   z: −30, yaw: 60, pitch: 14}` (the Cairn and the floating stair), town `{x: −12, z: 6, yaw: 90, pitch: 0}`, widest
   `{x: −20, z: 110, yaw: 0, pitch: 4}` (from the Shores toward the Tree).
6. **Prints:** no new prints file: `tests/realms/variant.test.js` asserts `generateRealm('remembered')` equals
   `generateWorld(7151)` on placement print, fingerprint, walk, reach and unreachable, and that the v1 pass's lists are
   deterministic (snapshot `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/remembered-v1.json`, positions to 0.001 m).

#### Assets
- `load_remembered` (1600 × 900 ≤ 200 KB + lo 960 × 540) with art-audio#variants' prompt verbatim ("The same small island
  with its great tree a hundred years ago, painted in warm sepia and gold like an old storybook: wooden scaffolds and
  little stone lamp towers on every hill, moth-folk carrying lamps, a stone stair rising from the mountain toward a
  floating island. Palette: #e9d8b0 #b8894a #6f5a44 #ffd98a."), `remembered_card_scaffold` ("a wooden scaffold with a
  string of lamps, sepia tones") and `remembered_card_dust` ("a few soft gold motes of light", drifting ambient
  particles); all realm `remembered`. Fallbacks: the home loading card graded; canvas scaffolds; no motes.
- Code-built kit `remembered_kit` (scaffold ring segments and ladders ≤ 2,000 tris per ring, stall ≤ 500, beacon cage
  ≤ 200, lamp post, `remembered_step`, `remembered_lamp`) with atlas-home's ART BRIEF `remembered_kit` in the PR
  (timber `#b8894a`, canvas `#e9d8b0`, ink `#6f5a44`, lamps `#ffcf6a`).

#### Acceptance criteria
- `generateRealm('remembered')` deep-equals `generateWorld(7151)` on the five print fields; home prints byte-identical.
- In-page: entering `remembered` shows the grade (sampled plaza pixel saturation ≤ 0.6 of home's at the same view),
  the scaffolds, four whole beacons, the four-flight stair, six stalls and nine lamp posts; no enemy, no chest, no gem
  trail, no shop awning or bunting; NPC look-at off; Pip casts no shadow.
- The realm suite enters and leaves `remembered` with no leak; 4 views within budget in both cameras.
- Entering `remembered` does not change `S.visited`.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/variant.test.js` (new), `bun test tests/world.test.js` (home unchanged).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (picks up `remembered`), `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` (new suite: "grade applied",
  "hidden lists not drawn", "no shadow, no look-at", "visited untouched").
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa` (`remembered` bundle ≈ 440 KB).

#### Out of scope
- Young cast (S-189), knots and memory scripts (S-190a, S-190b), the four Remembered acorns (S-190b), Moonmere (S-218),
  the Night (S-288), the Margins (S-301).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: regenerating home for the variant costs home's genMs again on every memory entry; the resident-parent rule keeps
`rootdeep` resident and the swap budget (≤ 1.2 s) is measured by the realm suite. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-189"></a>
### S-189 · feat: the young cast: scaled and recoloured models of Shellby, the Catchers, Granny Wick and Pelagia
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-188, S-068a, S-120a · Size: M (about 430 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [cast#young-cast](../bible/cast.md#young-cast), [lore#memory-extras](../bible/lore.md#memory-extras), [art-audio#young-cast](../bible/art-audio.md#young-cast), [charter#tone](../bible/charter.md#tone)

#### Why
The memories are only moving if the player recognises who they are watching: a tiny turtle in a hat too big for him,
a cheerful small Grumble, a hatless Pinch, a rabbit-sized Frostbun, Granny Wick at twelve. CANON §14 makes them the
existing models scaled and recoloured, so no shipped GLB (and never Pip's) is touched.

#### What
1. **Look records** (`js/content/looks.js`), all with `realm: 'remembered'` and the memory tint
   `mix(color, '#e9d8b0', 0.25)` applied to every colour (a `tint: 0.25` field the builders read). **cast.md wins over
   art-audio.md 8.4 (R14)**; edit art-audio's table in this PR.

   | id | Build | Size | Look and palette | Label in memories |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `young_shellby` | `turtle` code rig (`npcDef('shellby')` variant) × 0.48: no beard, no brows, no staff | 0.55 m | skin `#b8d070`; shell all moss `#7aa63a`, no mushrooms; robe `#f0e2c0`; straw sun hat 0.5 m wide `#e7c45a` with a green leaf `#7cc048`, tipped 12° over his eyes; satchel `#a86a3a` | "A young turtle" until `trueEnding`, then "Young Shellby" |
   | `young_grumble` | `boss_shroom` GLB × 0.5 (code fallback `bossDef('shroom')` × 0.5); cap materials cloned and tinted toward `#b08ae0`; hide the crown and baby-shroom nodes only if the GLB has them as separate nodes (log the node names in the PR) | 1.6 m | lighter, warmer cap; short beard as shipped | "Young Grumble" |
   | `young_pinch` | `boss_crab` GLB × 0.55 with the hat node hidden if separable; **else** the shipped code `bossDef('crab')` × 0.55 without its hat | 1.4 m | shell brighter `#f0705a`, no barnacles (code) | "Young Pinch" |
   | `young_frostbun` | `boss_frost` GLB × 0.18 | 0.6 m | as now, ice crystals tiny | "Young Frostbun" |
   | `young_wick` | `moth` rig × 0.62 | 0.6 m | wings mint `#dff8ea`, eyespots `#c9b6ec`, no spectacles or shawl, hair ribbon `#ff8f7a`, a toy lamplighter's pole | "Young Wick" |
   | `young_pelagia` | code-built here (a `sphere` shell sz 0.55 `#5f9f86`, four flattened `capsule` flippers, `sphere` head with `face()`), × 0.35 of Pelagia's 3.0 m | 1.0 m long | no barnacles, no bell; the pink ribbon `#e88a8a` is a separate part, hidden until memory 6 ties it on | "Young Pelagia" |
   | `keeper_tallow` | `moth` rig, a Lamplighter with extras | 1.0 m | wings dusk lilac `#b9a6d8` with cream eyespots `#f3e6c8`, long navy coat `#2b2238` with brass buttons and a spiral on the back `#f2c94c`, brass chain of office `#c9a24a`, long-handled lamp glowing `#ffd27a`, antennae combed straight | "Keeper Tallow" |
   | `lamplighter_1..5` | `moth` rig recolours | 0.8-1.0 m (0.8, 0.85, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0) | wings pale mint `#dff3e4`, gold `#f2d48a`, lilac `#d6c6ee`, mint, gold; navy coats `#2b2238` with brass buttons; a lamplighter's pole with a lit lamp `#ffd27a`; spiral on every coat back | "A Lamplighter" |
   | `villager_frog`, `villager_duck`, `villager_hedgehog`, `villager_rabbit` | shipped `frog` rig, `bird` rig (`beak: 'flat'`), `rodent` rig (`spines`), `bunny` rig; recoloured toward `#e9d8b0` / `#b8894a` / `#6f5a44` | as their kind | ordinary islanders, never named | "A villager" |

   Mote in memories (2, 7-11) is `npc_mote` in the `open` state at 1.15 × emissive; his cloak patches hidden if they are
   a separate node (lore#memory-extras). In memories 1-3 he is only a light on `sky:skyIsle`.
2. **No shipped asset is modified:** scaling is `object.scale`, recolour uses cloned materials; a unit test asserts the
   shared GLB materials are untouched after building every young look (`material.color` of the cached source equals its
   value before). **Pip's GLB is never loaded into a young look.**
3. **Animation:** young Shellby's hat slips 5° on each step and resets (a `hatSlip` part param); young Wick's pole bobs;
   young Pelagia paddles. All use the idle-bob, head-turn and arm-wave parts only (look-at stays off in `remembered`).
4. **Speech labels** (`js/content/memories.js` uses them): the label table above; the speaker name for `young_shellby`
   is computed `flag:trueEnding ? 'Young Shellby' : 'A young turtle'` (journal replays included). Content lint: no line
   whose speaker is in `remembered` contains "Shellby" (charter decision 6).
5. **Portraits for memory dialogue:** young Catchers reuse their adult portraits with CSS `filter: sepia(0.35)` on the
   dialogue portrait; Keeper Tallow, the Lamplighters and villagers show the name only.
6. **Debug:** `G.debug.npc.spawnLook(id, {x, z, realm})` to place any look for screenshots and tests.

#### Assets
- `portrait_young_shellby`, `portrait_young_wick`, `portrait_young_pelagia` (realm `remembered`, 512² webp ≤ 60 KB,
  cast.md "Portraits" recipe) with cast#young-cast's prompts verbatim (house preamble + portrait suffix +: "A tiny young
  turtle boy with bright green skin and a mossy shell, a straw sun hat far too big for him tipping over his eyes, a cream
  robe and a little satchel, sepia storybook memory tones, determined little smile."; "A 12-year-old moon moth girl with
  bright mint wings with lilac eyespots, a coral hair ribbon, holding a toy lamplighter's pole, sepia memory tones,
  eager."; "A young sea turtle girl with a smooth sea-green shell and a pink ribbon on one flipper, splashing in sunny
  shallows, sepia memory tones, laughing.").
- ART BRIEFs in the PR (batch 10 collects them at S-197): `npc_young_shellby` (cast.md's, ≤ 2,500 tris), `npc_young_wick`,
  `npc_young_pelagia`, `npc_keeper_tallow`, `npc_lamplighter` (one brief, five colourways), each with silhouette, size,
  palette, materials, pose, image prompt (turnaround) and 3D prompt starting with cast.md's 3D preamble. GLB slots
  commented out.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every young look builds under `?noassets=all` (code fallbacks) and with the shipped GLBs present.
- Shared GLB materials unchanged after building (unit); `npc_turtle` / Pip GLB never referenced by a young look.
- In-page in `remembered` (debug-spawned in a row on the plaza): heights within ±5 % of the table; young Shellby's hat
  is ≥ 0.9 × his height wide; the row's view stays within budget.
- Content lint: no `remembered` speaker line contains "Shellby"; the label function returns "A young turtle" without
  `trueEnding`.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/models.test.js` (young looks, material isolation), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` (add "young cast lineup heights and budget").
- `bun run test:smoke` with `?noassets=all&realm=remembered&debug=1`.

#### Out of scope
- Memory scripts that place them (S-190a, S-190b, S-212 onward); Pelagia's adult model (S-209 era).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the Catcher GLBs have baked hats or crowns that cannot be hidden; the code-rig fallbacks are named per row and the
PR records which path each took. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.
