<a id="s-190a"></a>
### S-190a · feat: memory knots on the Taproot open the Remembered Isle at a memory (runner and memory 1)
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-188, S-189, S-034, S-176, S-185 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden (the Rootdeep) · Bible: [lore#memories](../bible/lore.md#memories), [lore#memory-rules](../bible/lore.md#memory-rules), [lore#mem-01](../bible/lore.md#mem-01), [story#ch13](../bible/story.md#ch13), [story#scene-format](../bible/story.md#scene-format), [systems#portals](../bible/systems.md#portals), [atlas-far#rootdeep-anchors](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-anchors)

Split from INDEX S-190 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): part a is the knots, the memory runner and memory 1;
part b ([S-190b](#s-190b)) adds memories 2-3, the Remembered acorns and replays.

#### Why
Act III is the Remembering: Wenna tied twelve memories into knots on her Taproot so she could sleep, and Pip touches
them to see what happened a hundred years ago. This step builds the machinery every memory uses (twelve knots, entry,
the script, Look, leaving and replay) and the first memory, so the chapter that needs them (S-191a/b) is pure content.

#### What
1. **Knots** (`js/content/knots.js`, runtime in `js/memories.js`): twelve `rootdeep_knot` instances (S-176) at
   `w:taproot.knot<n>` = bearing 15° + 30°·(n−1), r 6.6, y 2.2. Portal records `rootdeep:knot1..12` (`how: 'touch'`,
   `[E] Touch · Memory`, reach 1.6 m; in FP the pill shows when the crosshair is on the knot). Group gates (lore#memory-rules):
   knots 1-3 `flag:lumbraCalm` (story: "knots 1-3 glow from `lumbraCalm`"), 4-6 `stones>=1`, 7-9 `stones>=2`, 10-12
   `stones>=3` (`stones` is story.md's derived count; until S-212 it is 0). States: **dark** `#4a3a2a` with a faint pulse
   (closed: no prompt), **pulse** in the group colour (1-3 sky gold `#ffe9a8`, 4-6 leaf `#8be07a`, 7-9 lilac `#c9b6ec`,
   10-12 sea `#7fd3d0`) thrumming every 1.6 s (`sfx.knotThrum`, audible within 12 m; the thrum is the sound twin),
   **steady** (watched: `flag:mem:<n>`, glows steadily, 0.85 × size). A knot never closes again.
2. **Entering** (`RealmManager.enter('remembered', <memory spawn>, 'memory')`): fade to warm white `#fff4dc` 0.6 s; a
   card in the `memory` frame for 2 s ("Memory 1 · The Letter Goes Up"); the memory's `tod` is applied to `remembered`
   (frozen per memory); fade in at the memory's spawn facing its direction; the script starts on arrival (`start:
   'auto'`, `when: ['calm']`). The loading card `load_remembered` shows only if generation is slower than the fade.
3. **Memory data** `js/content/memories.js`: `{ id: 'mem01', knot: 1, group: 1, title, when, tod, spawn: {x, z}, face,
   cast: [{ look, x, z, face, label, look_lines: [...] }], dressing: [...], scenes: [ scene ] }`. Scenes use the seven
   step kinds of story#scene-format plus two **memory events** added here: `memory.place {at, face}` (move Pip during a
   fade) and `memory.lamps {set, from, to, on, rate}` (switch a run of instanced lamps; S-190b's memory 3 uses it).
   Every line is a text id `rm.mem01.<n>` in `js/text/en/remembered.js`; Look lines `rm.mem01.look.<who>.<n>`.
4. **Rules while in a memory:** NPC look-at off; player shadow off; no enemies, damage, gems or chests; tools work.
   Every cast figure has `[E] Look · <label>` (shipped verb) showing its Look lines in order, the last repeating, in the
   `memory` frame (`#e9d8b0` / `#b8894a` / ink `#4a3a2a`; the frame is a CSS style in the dialogue box, S-034's frame
   table). First-person staging: a speaker stands within 8 m of the spawn inside the default view cone, or the step
   before its line is a `shot` (`two` or `look`) to them.
5. **Flags** (story#flags wins, R52): `mem:<n>` is set at the memory's **first step** (starting it is enough).
   Replays set nothing.
6. **Leaving:** after the last line, a gold mote rises at the spawn and stays (`remembered:knot`, `[E] Touch · Memory`)
   → `RealmManager.enter('rootdeep', 'taproot')` (0, −22) facing 180°. The pause menu gains "Leave memory" in this realm.
   On the **first** return from memory n, Wenna's return line `rd.wenna.mem<nn>` plays once in the `wenna` frame, voiced
   from the Taproot (`seen:rd.wenna.mem<nn>`).
7. **Memory 1 · The Letter Goes Up** (lore#mem-01, copied verbatim; R30: lore.md wins over atlas-home's m01 row, which
   this PR rewrites to match): knot 1; night 0 of the Ladder season, **22:00** (`tod` 0.9167); spawn near (92, −44), the
   Cairn's west slope, facing **west** toward the Great Tree; cast Keeper Tallow (97, −46), young Wick beside her
   (98.5, −45.5), five Lamplighters in a ring r 6 round the Cairn's foot (100, −48) at bearings 0°, 72°, 144°, 216°, 288°,
   lamps raised; young Frostbun on the Summit (114, −19). Dressing: the whole stair (`stair` set, all three flights, no
   lamps lit), the Cairn crystal glowing, the four old beacons lit (S-188).

   | # | Speaker | Line | Staging |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 1 | Keeper Tallow | Lamps up, everyone. High as you can. She's going to sneeze any moment, and the sky should see it go. | the Lamplighters raise lamps; a soft hum |
   | 2 | Young Wick | How do you know it's tonight? | |
   | 3 | Keeper Tallow | Because she's been sniffling since teatime. Hush now. Watch the Tree. | `shot look at w:greatTree.crown` |
   | - | - | - | `sfx.sneezeBig` from the west; three lights (green `#8be07a`, blue `#9fdcff`, pink `#ff9f8f`) streak up from the crown, arc high and fall north, east and south; three sparkles mark the catches (the Woods, the Peaks Summit, the Shores) |
   | 4 | Keeper Tallow | There it goes. *Still here,* she says. Still here. | |
   | 5 | A Lamplighter | Woods caught! Shore caught! ...Peaks? | `shot two at` the Summit (114, −19) |
   | 6 | Young Frostbun | Caught it! It's cold. Brrr-rr. I'll warm it up. | a small white figure on the Summit holds up a blue light |
   | 7 | Keeper Tallow | Three for three. Good catchers, the new ones. Now we wait for a kind hand. | the lamps lower |

   Look lines: A Lamplighter "Mind the third stone. It wobbles." · A Lamplighter "My arms are tired. Is it rude to hold a
   lamp with your feet?" · Young Wick "One day I'll hold the big lamp." · Keeper Tallow "Fourteen hundred years, and
   she's never once missed." Return line (Wenna): "Oh. I remember that night. I always sneeze better with an audience."
   Music: the music-box theme, slow, with a faint high choir hum (group 1).
8. **Sound twins** for off-camera events (lore rule): `sfx.sneezeBig`, `sfx.starFall` (descending glass chime),
   `sfx.lampOn` (soft tick, ≤ 8 per second).
9. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.enter(n)`, `G.debug.memory.skip()`, `G.debug.knots() → [{n, state}]`.

#### Assets
- `rootdeep_card_memoryknot` (S-177's 8-frame strip) drives the pulse; fallback the emissive knot material.
- Lore's ART BRIEF `prop_rootdeep_knot` ([lore#brief-knot](../bible/lore.md#brief-knot)) in the PR; no new models.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Before `lumbraCalm` every knot is dark with no prompt; with it, knots 1-3 pulse and thrum; 4-12 stay dark.
- Touching knot 1 enters memory 1 at (92, −44) facing west at 22:00 with the 2 s card; `mem:1` is set at the first
  step; the scene plays the seven lines with their staging; Look works on all eight figures; nobody looks at Pip; no
  shadow.
- The gold mote returns Pip to (0, −22); Wenna's return line plays once; knot 1 is steady; touching it again replays
  memory 1 and sets nothing new.
- Skipping the scene (charter: skip-safe) still sets `mem:1` and spawns the mote.
- Every line ≤ 220 chars, none says "Shellby"; dialogue frame `memory` used.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/memories.test.js` (new: data schema, knot gates by flag set, `mem:` set at first step, replays set
  nothing, label rule), `bun test tests/scenes.test.js` (the two memory events), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` (add "knot 1 enters memory 1", "leave by mote returns to taproot", "replay sets no
  flag", "skip still sets mem:1").

#### Out of scope
- Memories 2-3 and the Remembered acorns (S-190b); memories 4-12 (the stone-count beats from S-212 on); the chapter
  quest (S-191a/b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the memory's `tod` freeze leaks into the global clock; systems#realm-tod says frozen realms never write `S.tod`
and a unit test checks it after leaving. Rollback: revert; the Rootdeep is hidden.

<a id="s-190b"></a>
### S-190b · feat: memories 2 and 3, free roam and echoes in every memory, the Remembered acorns and journal replays
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-190a, S-074 · Size: M (about 340 lines, half text data) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [lore#mem-02](../bible/lore.md#mem-02), [lore#mem-03](../bible/lore.md#mem-03), [lore#memory-play](../bible/lore.md#memory-play), [lore#memory-echoes](../bible/lore.md#memory-echoes), [lore#memory-hours](../bible/lore.md#memory-hours), [atlas-home#remembered-isle](../bible/atlas-home.md#remembered-isle), [systems#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles)

#### Why
Chapter 13 needs the first group of three memories (the sky answers) before Wenna can show where the stones fell.
Memories are priced at 1.5 h but twelve watched scenes model at about 25 min, so every memory must be played, not only
watched: this step opens the Remembered Isle to free roam after each scene and adds the **echo** system (three
Look-able memory figures per memory, spread across the isle), with memories 1-3's nine echoes; the isle also hides
four Sleeping Acorns. The group 1 vignette is S-190c.

#### What
1. **Memory 2 · Someone Answers** (lore#mem-02 verbatim): knot 2; night 0, **23:30** (`tod` 0.9792); spawn near
   (95, −46) facing **north-east** (45°); cast as memory 1 (young Frostbun absent); stars overhead. Script: a high faint
   chord; one star leaves its place in the north-east sky and falls slowly (5 s) in a gold streak onto Driftholm
   (`sky.streak {to: 'sky:skyIsle'}` + `sfx.starFall`); the sky isle glows softly. Lines: 1 A Lamplighter "Keeper.
   Keeper! Something's falling." · 2 Keeper Tallow "...It's landed on the step of sky. Oh, my stars. Someone answered."
   (`shot look at sky:skyIsle`) · 3 Young Wick "Nobody ever answers. You said. Hundreds and hundreds of years, and
   nobody." · 4 Keeper Tallow "I did say that. Fetch the long lamps, Wick. We're going up the stair in the morning." ·
   5 A Lamplighter "Is it coming down?" · 6 Keeper Tallow "It can't. Not that far. Not on its own light." · 7 Wenna
   "...Mote?" (her voice faint, from far to the west; the Lamplighters all turn to look at the Tree). Look: A Lamplighter
   "Do you think it's lonely up there?" · Young Wick "I'm waving. Just in case." Return line (Wenna): "Oh, Pip. That was
   him. My little brother. He was always the first to try things."
2. **Memory 3 · The First Lamps** (lore#mem-03 verbatim): knot 3; day 1, **19:30** (`tod` 0.8125); spawn near (97, −50)
   at the foot of the needle facing up the first flight (45°); cast Keeper Tallow at the needle's foot, young Wick, five
   Lamplighters carrying crates up the Peaks path and the first flight; dressing six lamp crates (`prop_remembered_kit`
   node `crate`) stacked by the needle; the first flight's lamps light one by one: `memory.lamps {set: 'stair', from: 1,
   to: 22, on: true, rate: 2}`. Lines: 1 Keeper Tallow "One lamp a step. Two on each landing. Then a line of them down
   the mountain and all the way to her. He walks on light, not stone." · 2 A Lamplighter "That's every lamp from
   Duskwick. The pine isle's coming tomorrow, and the farm isle after." (sets down a crate) · 3 Keeper Tallow "Then
   it's every lamp from everywhere. Hang them." · 4 Young Wick "Mine too?" (holds up her small lamp) · 5 Keeper Tallow
   "Not yours, Wick. That's your naming lamp. You keep yours." · 6 Young Wick "...Hm." (she looks up the stair) ·
   7 A Lamplighter "He's at the top! He's waving! He's very small!" (`shot look at sky:skyIsle`: a tiny gold figure under
   Driftholm). Look: A Lamplighter "Forty more crates on the jetty. Mind your wings." · A Lamplighter "He told me his
   name. Mote. He said it twice, in case I forgot." · Young Wick "Can I light it? Can I light the next one?" Return line:
   "They gave him every lamp they had, and they never said a word about it. That's Lamplighters for you." Music for
   group 1 as memory 1.
3. **Remembered acorns** (append to v1's list, `W.remembered.acorns`, after S-188's items, so nothing earlier moves):
   `remembered:a1` `near(plaza stall 1, 2, 5)`; `a2` `near((−36.1, −44.8), 2, 6)`; `a3` `near(the lighthouse door (8.5,
   118.7) + 4 m on bearing 330°, 2, 6)` (memory 6's finish line); `a4` `near(windmill + (r + 4) m on bearing 90°, 0, 4)`.
   Snore within 10 m, woken with the Sunglass (1.5 s) exactly as on home; `collect.acorn['remembered:a<n>']`; counted
   in **home's** completion group (systems: variant finds count under the parent). They exist in every memory.
4. **Replays:** the journal's Lore tab gets a "Memories" list (S-037's tab; grep `LoreTab`): one row per watched memory
   (`mem:<n>`), title and `Replay ✿`, which enters that memory exactly as its knot does and returns to where Pip stood.
5. **Atlas fix (R30):** rewrite atlas-home's m02-m03 rows to lore's spawns and times.
6. **Free roam** (lore#memory-play; `js/memories.js`): when the last scene ends, control returns and the whole
   `remembered` realm is walkable in that memory's state until the mote or "Leave memory". Scene figures stay where the
   scene left them with their Look lines. The mote gets a compass target `{ realm: 'remembered', x, z }` with the label
   "Back to the Taproot" (≤ 32 chars) and a soft pull sound within 20 m (`sfx.motePull`, the sound twin of its glow).
   S-190a's mote placement is unchanged (the scene's spot).
7. **Echoes** (lore#memory-echoes; new field `echoes: [{ k, label, look, at: { x, z }, face, gesture, line }]` in
   `js/content/memories.js`; runtime in `js/memories.js`): spawned when free roam starts, despawned on leaving; each is
   the memory rig named by `look` (S-189's young cast, `lamplighter_*`, the villager recolours) with the memory grade
   plus a gold fresnel rim (`#ffe9a8` at 0.5; a `onBeforeCompile` uniform on the toon material clone, opaque), playing
   the rig idle and its `gesture` pose on a 4 s loop; one shared `fx_echo_sparkle` sprite above each until it is Looked
   at; `sfx.echoChime` (two soft notes) every 3 s within 15 m. `[E] Look · <label>` (shipped verb) shows `line` in the
   `memory` frame and sets `seen:echo:mNN.k` (the generic flags map, save v7; no bump). Placement: `at` is resolved with
   `near(x, z, 0, 4)` (nearest walkable); a unit test checks 25-160 m from the scene's spot, ≥ 20 m from each other and
   the mote, and outside every scene figure's 8 m circle. The journal's Lore → Memories row shows "Echoes k/3".
8. **Echo data for memories 1-3**, verbatim from lore#mem-01, #mem-02 and #mem-03 (the **Echoes** table under each:
   label, where, gesture, line; text ids `rm.mem0N.echo.<k>`). For example memory 1: A villager near (6, 16) "She's
   sniffling again. Hankies out, everyone. Last time she sneezed it blew my hat into the pond."; A Lamplighter near
   (45, 4.5) on the Peaks bridge; Young Grumble near (−3.2, −110). Memory 1's echoes ship here too (S-190a shipped only
   its scene).
9. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.echoes() → [{ k, label, x, z, seen }]`; `G.debug.memory.roam()` ends the current scene and
   starts free roam (for tests).

#### Assets
- `fx_echo_sparkle` (lore assets table): **generate** a 4-frame horizontal strip, 512 × 128 (four 128² cells), alpha,
  with the image prompt: art-audio preamble + `fx` suffix + "a tiny soft gold sparkle, a four-point star with a warm
  round glow, four frames of a gentle twinkle, soft painterly storybook style, transparent background. Palette: #ffe9a8
  #fff1c4 #ffd27a." Process with `tools/process_strip.py` (4 frames) and `tools/export_sprites.py` to webp ≤ 12 KB;
  manifest key `fx_echo_sparkle` in `assets/manifest.js` under the `remembered` group (lazy). Fallback: canvas 4-point
  star `#ffe9a8` with a radial glow.
- Sounds `sfx.echoChime` and `sfx.motePull` are synth recipes in `js/audio.js` (two soft sine notes E6-B6 at −24 dB;
  a 0.6 s rising shimmer at −26 dB). No other new assets.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Memories 2 and 3 enter at their spawns, times and facings; lines, staging and Look lines match lore verbatim; the
  first flight's 22 lamps light at 2 per second in memory 3; `mem:2`/`mem:3` set at first step.
- The four acorns stand at their rules, snore, wake with the Sunglass and count under home (stats test).
- `Replay ✿` from the journal plays a memory and returns Pip to his previous place; nothing is set.
- `variant.test.js`: the v1 pass is unchanged for S-188's items (snapshot equal) with the acorns appended.
- After each of memories 1-3, Pip can walk anywhere on the Remembered Isle until he touches the mote; the compass points
  to the mote.
- Each of memories 1-3 shows exactly three echoes at their lore positions (nearest walkable), with the rim, sparkle and
  chime; Look shows the lore line verbatim and sets `seen:echo:mNN.k`; the sparkle goes; "Echoes k/3" in the journal
  counts it; a replay shows all three again and sets nothing new.
- Echo lint: every echo line ≤ 220 characters, none says "Shellby", labels from lore#memory-rules' list; placement test
  (25-160 m from the scene's spot, ≥ 20 m apart, outside 8 m staging circles) passes for every memory's data present.
- Budget: the `remembered` densest view with memory 1's cast plus its three echoes stays ≤ 350 draw calls and
  ≤ 1.3 M tris.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/memories.test.js` (adds: echo schema, lint, placement, `seen:echo` flags, free-roam state machine),
  `bun test tests/realms/variant.test.js`, `bun test tests/stats.test.js`, `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` ("memory 3 lamps", "remembered acorn wakes", "journal replay returns", new: "free roam
  after memory 1 reaches the windmill", "echo look sets seen flag", "echo budget").

#### Out of scope
- The chapter that sends Pip here (S-191a/b); memories 4-12 and their echoes (S-212b, S-232b, S-254b); every vignette
  (S-190c, S-212e, S-232b, S-254d).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: replay from the journal while in a dungeon; the Lore tab disables `Replay ✿` where realm swaps are not allowed
(fights, scenes, dungeons) with "Later, on open ground." Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-190c"></a>
### S-190c · feat: the vignette runner, the Ladder's path lamps and vignette 1 The Lamp Path in memory 3
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-190b, S-030 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [lore#memory-vignettes](../bible/lore.md#memory-vignettes), [lore#vignette-lamp-path](../bible/lore.md#vignette-lamp-path), [lore#mem-03](../bible/lore.md#mem-03), [lore#memory-rules](../bible/lore.md#memory-rules), [lore#memory-hours](../bible/lore.md#memory-hours), [atlas-home#remembered-isle](../bible/atlas-home.md#remembered-isle)

Added by this file (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): the playable part of memory group 1.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M22 · The Remembered Isle, chapter 13 and Grumble's Undercap**. Each group of three memories has one
vignette, a stretch Pip plays with his own feet while the past plays at his pace; they are most of what makes the
memories' 1.5 h real (lore#memory-hours). This step builds the runner every vignette uses, the 142 path lamps of the
Ladder (memories 3, 5, 7 and 12 all use them) and group 1's vignette: Pip walks up the Cairn path at dusk while the
Lamplighters' first lamps light ahead of him. Pip changes nothing: the lamps light as they did that night, at his pace.

#### What
1. **Runner** `js/vignettes.js` (new, imported by `js/memories.js`): a memory may have `vignette: { id, mode, when:
   'before' | 'after', start: { x, z, face }, end: { x, z, face, r }, ...mode params, overheard: [{ at, speaker, from: {
   x, z }, text }] }`. `when: 'before'`: Pip arrives at `start` (the memory's `spawn`) and the vignette runs; on its end
   the memory's first scene starts where Pip stands (the scene's spot is the new field `sceneAt: { x, z, face }`; the
   first-person speaker rule is measured from `sceneAt`). `when: 'after'`: after the last scene, a 0.6 s fade with
   `memory.place` to `start`, an optional 2 s `card` in the `memory` frame, the vignette, then the mote at `end`. States
   `idle → running → done`. A mode registry `registerVignetteMode(name, { init, step, skip })`; this step registers
   `lead` only (S-212e adds `course`, S-254d adds `carry`); an unknown mode logs once and skips straight to `done`.
2. **`lead` mode**, two variants, both pure step functions in `js/vignettes.js` so they unit-test without a browser:
   - `lamps: { set, from, to, guide? }`: the next lamp in the run lights (`sfx.lampOn`) when Pip is within 9 m of the
     last lit one (at most one lamp per frame); `guide: { look, hop: 0.4 }` moves a figure to land beside each lamp as it
     lights (a 0.4 s arc). The run ends when lamp `to` is lit and Pip is within `end.r` of `end`.
   - `light: { sprite, set, from, to, ahead: [4, 10], wait: 14, vmax: 5 }` (used by S-232b): a glow sprite moves lamp to
     lamp along the run; each lamp it passes brightens to 1.4 for 0.6 s; it moves only while Pip is within `wait` m, at
     Pip's horizontal speed capped at `vmax`, keeping 4-10 m ahead of him; it never speeds up when he gets ahead.
   - `overheard`: each line fires once when the run passes `at` (a lamp index), in the `memory` frame with the
     speaker's label, its sound positioned at `from`.
3. **Ladder lamp sets** (lore#memory-rules "Ladder lamp sets"; built in `js/memories.js`, deterministic, no RNG, so
   no prints change): `path` = 142 instances of `prop_remembered_ladder_lamp` along the polyline (97, −50) → (77, −43)
   → (60, −14) → (54.7, 2.2) → (36.9, 6.8) → (16, 9) → (4, 9.5), evenly spaced by arc length, numbered from the needle's
   foot, each offset 1.2 m to the left of the direction of travel and nudged to the nearest walkable cell within 3 m;
   `landing` = 4 lamps, two on each of the first two Starstair landings; `stair` stays S-188's (lamps on steps 1-66).
   All three sets share the one instanced mesh (2 draw calls in all: mesh and glow). `memory.lamps` accepts `path` and
   `landing`. If a polyline point is not walkable on seed 7151, move it to the nearest walkable cell within 10 m and
   record the move in the PR and in lore#memory-rules.
4. **Memory 3's vignette** (lore#vignette-lamp-path, verbatim): memory 3's `spawn` becomes near (57.7, 1.5) facing east;
   `sceneAt` near (97, −50) facing 45° (the old spawn); `vignette: { id: 'lampPath', mode: 'lead', when: 'before',
   lamps: { set: 'path', from: 84, to: 1, guide: { look: 'lamplighter_a', hop: 0.4 } }, end: { x: 97, z: −50, r: 6 } }`;
   lamps 1-84 start unlit. Overheard lines (`rm.mem03.v.1-3`): at lamp 70 A Lamplighter (ahead, on the path) "Mind the
   crates on the bend! Lamps first, then crates, then tea." · at lamp 40 A Lamplighter (behind, on the bridge, from
   (45, 4.5)) "Wait for me! I've got the long hooks! Nobody can hang anything without the long hooks!" · at lamp 10
   Young Wick (ahead, from (98.5, −49)) "They're coming! I can see the lamps coming up the hill!". The scene then plays as
   S-190b shipped it (its `memory.lamps` on the first flight unchanged).
5. **Feel:** the first time any vignette starts (`seen:hint.vignette`), a one-line `memory` frame hint for 3 s: "Walk
   on. The memory follows you." Movement is unrestricted; NPC look-at stays off and Pip casts no shadow; no HUD timer.
6. **Skip** (the scene-skip input during a vignette): Pip is placed at `end` facing `end.face` (or `sceneAt.face`), the
   run's lamps are set to their end state, and what follows starts. `G.debug.memory.skip()` now finishes the whole
   memory (vignette and scenes; mote up), and `tests/chapter.mjs`'s `scene.skip` inside `remembered` calls it, so the ch13
   chapter test (S-191b) keeps its step count.
7. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.vignette() → { id, mode, state, progress }` and `G.debug.lamps(set) → [{ i, x, z, lit }]`.

#### Assets
- The 142 path and 4 landing lamps reuse `prop_remembered_ladder_lamp` (S-190; lore#brief-ladder-lamp).
- `prop_long_hook` (the guide's pole; code-built in `js/props.js:longHook()`, ≤ 40 tris: a 1.2 m wooden cylinder
  `#8a5a30` with a curled brass crook `#c9a24a`, held in the Lamplighter's hand node). **ART BRIEF:** silhouette a thin
  pole taller than the moth with a curled hook at the top; 1.2 m; palette `#8a5a30` wood, `#c9a24a` brass, `#6e5530`
  dark brass; toon; 40 tris (code) / 200 (GLB); pose straight; image prompt: art-audio preamble + `sheet` suffix + "a
  lamplighter's long wooden pole with a curled brass hook at the top for hanging lanterns, shown front, side and back,
  soft painterly storybook style. Palette: #8a5a30 #c9a24a #6e5530."; 3D prompt: "A stylised low-poly lamplighter's long
  wooden pole with a curled brass crook at the top, soft toy-like proportions, hand-painted texture. Original design.
  Single mesh, game-ready, under 200 triangles, base at the origin, no base plate, no text." Manifest key
  `prop_long_hook` (`models` slot, `remembered`, `height: 1.2`); fallback cylinder + half torus.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Unit (`tests/vignettes.test.js`, new): `lead.lamps` lights the next lamp only when Pip is within 9 m of the last lit,
  never more than one per step, and waits forever if Pip walks away; `lead.light` keeps the light 4-10 m ahead, stops
  when Pip is more than 14 m behind, never exceeds 5 m/s and never accelerates when Pip is ahead; an unknown mode goes
  to `done`; skip sets the end state.
- The `path` set has 142 lamps, every one on a walkable cell, consecutive spacing 0.6-1.2 m, lamps 1-84 between the
  needle's foot and the bridge's east end by arc length; `stair` 66 + `landing` 4 + `path` 142 = 212.
- In-page: knot 3 enters memory 3 at (57.7, 1.5) facing east at 19:30; walking the path (the bot warps along the
  polyline in 3 m steps) lights lamps 84 → 1 in order with the guide landing beside each; the three overheard lines
  fire once each, in order; within 6 m of (97, −50) the scene starts; `mem:3` was set at the vignette's start; after the
  scene, free roam and memory 3's echoes (S-190b) work and the mote is at (97, −50).
- Skip during the vignette places Pip at (97, −50) facing 45° with lamps 1-84 lit and starts the scene;
  `G.debug.memory.skip()` ends memory 3 with the mote up; a replay plays the vignette again and sets nothing.
- Budget: the view from (57.7, 1.5) toward the Cairn with all 84 lamps lit and the scene cast placed stays ≤ 350 draw
  calls and ≤ 1.3 M tris; the lamps add exactly 2 draw calls.
- Invariants: all lines (home prints unchanged; the lamp sets use no RNG and live only in `remembered`).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test tests/vignettes.test.js tests/memories.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` (new cases "lamp path vignette lights in order", "vignette skip", "path lamp set
  count and walkability", "lamp path budget").
- `bun run test:smoke` with `?noassets=all&realm=remembered&debug=1`; `node tests/chapter.mjs ch13` once S-191b is in
  the stack.

#### Out of scope
- The `course` mode and vignette 2 (S-212e); the `light` variant's first use, vignette 3 (S-232b); the `carry` mode and
  vignette 4 (S-254d); memory 5's use of the path set (S-212b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a polyline point lands on unwalkable Peaks rock and the bot cannot follow; the walkable-nudge rule and the
placement test catch it, and the PR moves the point (≤ 10 m) and records it. Risk: slow software-rendered walking in
CI; the bot warps in 3 m steps instead of walking. Rollback: revert; memory 3 spawns at the needle's foot again and
plays its scene as S-190b shipped it.

<a id="s-191a"></a>
### S-191a · feat: chapter 13 Into the Rootdeep, part 1: Digby leads, Gran Burrows, the Heart and Lumbra
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-190b, S-185, S-182, S-174, S-178, S-033a, S-036 · Size: M (about 450 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden (S-191b unhides) · Bible: [story#ch13](../bible/story.md#ch13), [systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests), [atlas-far#rootdeep-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-people), [cast#digby](../bible/cast.md#digby), [cast#gran-burrows](../bible/cast.md#gran-burrows), [charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person)

Split from INDEX S-191 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): part a is the quest, Digby's walk, Gran and the
Heart/Lumbra beats; part b ([S-191b](#s-191b)) is the roots-remember scene, root-gates, the unhide and the chapter test.

#### Why
Chapter 13 opens Act III. Wenna asked Pip to go down into her roots (the ch12 small-sneeze scene set `ch13`); Digby,
who can't see, leads where nobody can ("Finally, a place where everyone's as lost as me. Follow my voice."), and Pip
meets his Gran, wins the Paws in the Heart and calms Lumbra.

#### What
1. **Quest `mq-ch13`** (`js/content/quests/rootdeep.js`; n 13, kind main, owner `digby`, title "Into the Rootdeep",
   `start: 'flag:ch13'`; R2: story.md's `q:ch13` means this id). Steps (story#ch13 table; journal text, hint nudge and
   hint answer verbatim, with R1's pronoun fix in step 5):

   | # | id | done (on) | Journal | Nudge / Answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | 0 | `digby` | `seen:digby.ch13.0` (TALK_END) | Find Digby. | He's never far from the windmill. / Digby stands by the Meadow Windmill in Bloom Meadow, west of the village. |
   | 1 | `windmill` | `realm:rootdeep.visited` (REALM) | Open the root-gate under the windmill. | Something under the windmill wants a light. / Walk round the windmill's footing to the root arch and hold up the Lantern with {tool}, then press {interact}. |
   | 2 | `follow` | `flag:ch13.atGran` (Pip within 8 m of `rootdeep.gran` while Digby is there) | Follow Digby through the dark. | He knows the way. Stay close and listen. / Walk behind Digby. If you lose him, follow his voice; he waits at every turning. |
   | 3 | `gran` | `met:gran_burrows` (SCENE_END `ch13-gran`) | Meet Digby's Gran. | Someone in Burrowbottom has been expecting you. / Gran Burrows sits outside the round door with the lamp, in the middle of Burrowbottom. |
   | 4 | `heart` | `ability:dig` (ITEM_GET) | Find a way into the Rootdeep Heart. | Some earth down here is softer than the rest. / Enter the Heart past the Underlake. The Digging Paws are in its middle rooms; dig the sparkly brown mounds with {tool}. |
   | 5 | `lumbra` | `flag:lumbraCalm` (BOSS_DEFEATED) | Calm whatever is curled round the Heart. | It's very long. It's very sulky. / Lumbra surfaces from the ringed holes; wait for the ring, dodge the dirt, hit her while she's up. |
   | 6 | `knots` | `flag:mem:1` and `flag:mem:2` and `flag:mem:3` (SCENE_END) | Touch the memory knots on the Taproot (n/3). | They glow when they're ready. / Walk up to each glowing knot on the great Taproot and press {interact}. |
   | 7 | `roots` | `flag:stoneSites` (SCENE_END) | Listen to the roots. | She has something to show you. / Stand by the Taproot after the three memories. |

   Compass targets: step 0 `{ npc: 'digby' }`; 1 `{ realm: 'rootdeep' }` (the windmill root arch); 2 `{ npc: 'digby' }`;
   3 `{ npc: 'gran_burrows' }`; 4 `{ realm: 'rootdeep', x: 0, z: 9.5 }` (the Heart door); 5 the boss door inside d6;
   6 the nearest open unwatched knot; 7 `w:taproot`. Step 7's `set` and `after` are S-191b's (the scene). Short labels
   (≤ 32 chars): "Digby · the windmill", "Root-gate · the windmill", "Follow Digby", "Gran Burrows", "The Rootdeep Heart",
   "Calm Lumbra", "Memory knots", "Listen at the Taproot".
2. **Digby, step 0** (`js/content/dialogue/home.js`, priority 100 while `quest:mq-ch13==0`): "Gran knocked on the roots
   last night. That's how she writes. She wants to meet you. The root-gate's behind my windmill. I'll go ahead and
   wait." After it, Digby's home NPC is hidden until the chapter ends (he is in the Rootdeep at `rootdeep.stair`).
3. **Home's root-gate, step 1:** S-182's `[E] Kindle · Root-gate` (R23) at the windmill arch; the arch's seal sinks
   with warm light; Pip enters (`RealmManager.enter('rootdeep', 'gate-home')`). Needs `flag:ch13`; S-191b unhides the
   realm. Until then the chapter is only playable with `?realm` debug (the chapter test runs after S-191b).
4. **Digby leads** (a **chapter partner**, F27 path + charter first-person rule 4 / decision 19: while Pip moves toward
   the goal Digby stays 2-8 m ahead within 40° of the view direction; he walks 1.6 m/s; at each marker he waits until
   Pip is within 6 m, says the marker line, then walks on; if Pip is more than 12 m away he stops and calls "Over here!
   Follow my voice." with a positional chirp every 4 s, the sound twin of his helmet lamp blinking). Build it as a
   reusable `js/partner.js` (`startPartner(npcId, route, markers, opts)`), used again for later partners (Pelagia,
   the Twinklings). **Route (R17: atlas-far's route, story's six lines placed on it):**

   | Leg | Waypoints | Marker (id, where) | Line (story#ch13 verbatim) |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 1 (step 2) | `rootdeep.stair` (−128, −44) | `gate` at the landing (−120, −56) | Down here, you're the one squinting. Welcome to my world. It's very nice. Bit damp. |
   | | → stair foot (−134, −42) | `fork1` (−134, −42) | Left here. Smells like left. |
   | | → (−150, −20) | `townedge` (−150, −20) | Burrowbottom's just ahead. Gran will say I've grown. I haven't. She says it anyway. |
   | | → `rootdeep.gran` (−180, −32) | (step 2 done) | |
   | 2 (step 4, after Gran) | `rootdeep.lamp` (−160, 4) → tunnel mouth (−110, 0) | `drip` (−110, 0) | Mind the drip. That one's been dripping since I was a pup. Same drip. I call him Gerald. |
   | | → west shore (−84, 0) | `darkhall` (−84, 0) | Something's flapping. Your lamp'll show it. I'll stand very still and be brave. |
   | | → west causeway foot (−72, 0) → (−50, 0) | `mushrooms` (−60, 0) | You know what's funny? Up top, everyone tells me where things are. Down here I get to tell you. |
   | | → `rootdeep.taproot` (−6, −16) | (end) | This is as far as I go. Lumbra and me don't get on. She thinks I'm a root. |

   After leg 2 Digby stays at `rootdeep.taproot` for the rest of the game when Pip is in the Rootdeep ("listening to
   her"); his home schedule resumes when the chapter ends (`flag:stoneSites`). No enemy spawns within 15 m of Digby while
   he leads (the partner reserves a calm zone); a Drip Bat may show in the Underlake hall as his line says.
5. **Gran Burrows, step 3** (scene `ch13-gran`, `js/content/scenes/rootdeep.js`, talk scene, replayable):
   Gran "There's my Digby. You've grown." / Digby "I haven't, Gran." / Gran "You have. Sideways." / Gran "And you're the
   little sprout. Has he shown you his seed pouch yet? No? He will. When he's ready." / Gran "The roots went dim a
   hundred years back, and dim again this year. Something pulls at them. Go to the Heart. Lumbra won't like it. Lumbra
   doesn't like anything. Except me." Sets `met:gran_burrows` (opens the Heart door, R22). Then Digby starts leg 2.
6. **Scene `ch13_heart`** (dungeons#d6 names it; plays when Pip first stands at Rootdeep spawn `heart` after
   `lumbraCalm`, 14 s, skippable, replay true): `shot crane` up the Taproot as S-176's glow climbs; knots 1-3 begin to
   pulse (S-190a states); Wenna (`wenna` frame, from `w:taproot.face`): "Oh. That's better. I can feel my roots again,
   all the way down." / "...Three little knots, there by my foot. I tied those. I think I tied them so I could sleep."
   Digby (from `rootdeep.taproot`): "She hums when she's happy. Gran says it's the roots. I say it's her."
7. **Pronoun and id fixes in the bible (R1, R2), in this PR:** story.md ch13 (Gran's and Digby's lines are kept as
   written above with "She thinks I'm a root."; hint 5 "hit her while she's up"); story.md's `q:ch13`/`q:ch14`/
   `q:remember` → `mq-ch13`/`mq-ch14`/`mq-remember`.
8. **Flags** registered: `ch13.atGran`, `seen:digby.ch13.0`, `seen:rd.ch13_heart`, partner flags none (runtime only).

#### Assets
None new (all models and portraits exist: Digby shipped, Gran S-178, Lumbra S-185).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Quest lint: every step has text, done and a two-part hint; compass targets resolve.
- In-page with a `ch13-start` fixture and `?realm` unhide override: Digby's step-0 line, the windmill gate kindles,
  arrival at `gate-home`; Digby walks leg 1 and says three marker lines in order, waiting within 12 m; the Gran scene
  plays and sets `met:gran_burrows`; leg 2's three lines and the "as far as I go" line; the Heart door opens.
- Partner rule: while Pip walks toward the goal Digby stays 2-8 m ahead and within 40° of the view direction in ≥ 90 %
  of sampled frames (in-page measure in FP).
- After Lumbra, `ch13_heart` plays once on exiting the Heart and knots 1-3 pulse.
- No line says "he" of Lumbra; content lint passes. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/quests.test.js` (mq-ch13 lint), `bun test tests/partner.test.js` (new: route, waits, marker order,
  calm zone), `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs chapter13` (new suite, CI matrix: "digby leads leg 1", "gran scene opens heart door", "leg 2",
  "ch13_heart after lumbraCalm").
- `node tests/inpage.mjs npc` (schedules unaffected).

#### Out of scope
- The roots-remember scene, `stoneSites`, root-gates on visited isles, unhiding and the chapter test (S-191b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: Digby gets stuck on Burrowbottom's ramps; F27's re-request rule and a teleport-to-next-marker after 6 s stuck
(out of view only) keep him moving. Rollback: revert; the realm is still hidden.

<a id="s-191b"></a>
### S-191b · feat: chapter 13 part 2: the roots remember, root-gates open, the Rootdeep is unhidden
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-191a, S-190c, S-030 · Size: M (about 400 lines incl. fixtures) · Save: none · Flag: none (this step unhides) · Bible: [story#ch13](../bible/story.md#ch13), [atlas-far#rootdeep-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-states), [systems#root-gates](../bible/systems.md#root-gates), [atlas-home#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots), [cast#plaza-visitors](../bible/cast.md#plaza-visitors), [charter#invariants](../bible/charter.md#invariants)

#### Why
The chapter ends with Wenna remembering: her roots flare and show where the three Stair Stones fell. That opens the
middle of Act III (the Glassway, Kettle Isle, Saltglass) and turns on fast travel under the sea. This is the step that
makes the Rootdeep, the Heart, its Hollows and the Remembered Isle real for players.

#### What
1. **Scene `ch13-roots`** (50 s, at the Taproot, starts when Pip is within 10 m of `w:taproot` with `mem:1-3` set;
   `replay: true`), story#ch13 verbatim, Wenna's voice through the wood (`wenna` frame):
   1. "I remember the Lamplighters. I remember a little light falling onto the rock in the sky. ...I don't remember why it
      hurts."
   2. "Look. My roots still know where things fell." (three roots flare gold and the light runs off along them in three
      directions; `shot crane` up the Taproot)
   3. "Three stones from the old stair. They fell in the storm. One in the shallows, where a bell lies. One in a mountain
      that boils. One under an old school." (the chart gains three marks: Bell Shallows, the Kettle, the Academy)
   4. "If they were set back on the Cairn, the stair might stand again. And then... then I could see him."
   5. "Nell will have to sail past the reef. Tell her I said she's brave. She'll pretend she knew."
   `after: ['set:stoneSites', 'rootgates:visited', 'quest:mq-ch14.start', 'quest:mq-remember.start']` (R2 ids). If the
   scene is skipped, the flags and `after` still apply.
2. **Veins** (atlas-far#rootdeep-states, S-176's `rootdeep_vein`): from `stoneSites`, three chained vein strips run along
   the floor from the Taproot base to `gate-glassway` (0, 250), `gate-kettle` (231, −96) and `gate-saltglass` (−96, 231)
   (anchors `w:vein.bell`, `w:vein.kettle`, `w:vein.saltglass`; the route is the F27 path between the anchors, strips
   every 6 m), pulsing `#ffcf7a` once every 2 s, the pulse travelling away from the Taproot at 8 m/s. `stone:<site>` (later
   steps) turns its vein steady `#fff1b8`. Runtime only (no prints change).
3. **Chart marks:** the World tab draws three small gold star marks with labels "The Bell?", "The Kettle?", "The
   Academy?" at the Glassway's spire, Kettle Isle and Saltglass Flats chart positions (still fog banks); the Under tab
   lights the opened gates.
4. **Root-gates:** `rootgates:visited` (S-182) opens the gate of home and of every visited isle (Duskwick, Pinewhistle,
   Archwater, Threemills). R4: systems.md's "root-gates open on `ch13`" becomes "on `stoneSites`" (edit systems.md's
   realm registry `open` for `glassway` → `flag:stoneSites`, the quest skeleton row `mq-ch14` → start `flag:stoneSites`,
   the travel table and the flag dictionary row; this PR).
5. **Quest `mq-remember`** (n 23, kind main, owner `wenna`, title "The Knots in the Roots", `start: 'flag:stoneSites'`):
   steps `group1` (done `memories>=3`, already true), `group2` (`memories>=6`; journal "Touch the memory knots on the
   Taproot (n/6)"; nudge "The roots wait for a Stair Stone." / answer "Set a Stair Stone on the Cairn; three more knots
   will wake."), `group3` (`memories>=9`), `group4` (`memories>=12`); `set` on done: none (ch18 reads `memories>=12`).
   It never shows as the objective while `mq-ch14` or a stone quest is active (main-quest priority). S-212a later
   replaces `group2`-`group4` in place with `m456`, `m789`, `m101112` (same positions; the quest is never redefined).
6. **Unhide:** `REALMS.rootdeep.hidden = false`, `rootdeep_heart`, `hollow_rootdeep_1..3` and `remembered` likewise
   (R51: systems says S-190; S-191b unhides all four together so nothing is reachable half-built). Prints for `rootdeep`
   and `rootdeep_heart` are **frozen** from this PR on (a comment at the top of each fixture says so); later content
   is post-passes only. The Completion tracker shows the Rootdeep group from `realm:rootdeep.visited`.
7. **Plaza visitor (R26):** **Spud** at `home:spot-visit-rootdeep` (placeSpots row 6, bearing 270°, r 12-15, appended
   after the existing rows) from the morning after `stoneSites`, 08:00-20:00 daily (schedule rows `[[8,
   'home:spot-visit-rootdeep','stand'],[20,'rootdeep.market-2','indoors']]` with the realm switch handled by F27's
   off-screen placement). Lines (priority 10, by day index): "It IS blue! Mostly! Teasel was right, which is annoying." ·
   "Up here the ceiling is SO far away. Does anyone ever bump it?" · "Gran says I'm to be back by supper. She means
   moles' supper. That's late." Edit atlas-home's row (Gran Burrows → Spud) in this PR. World test rule 9 seeds 1-40.
8. **Villager memory lines for Act III start** are not here (S-213 era); Wenna's pedestal lines S7/S8 (story#wenna-lines)
   are S-260's.
9. **Chapter test `ch13`** (S-030 harness): `tests/fixtures/saves/ch13-start.json` (the `ch12` end state: `act2done`,
   `sneeze2`, `ch13`, `moteNamed`, the four beacons, items `lantern`, `glider`, `tide_bell`, `hook`, `sunglass`, swords 2,
   hearts 12, the four isles visited) and `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch13.json` (steps: talk Digby; kindle the windmill
   gate; partner walk with `G.debug.partner.skip()` allowed after the first marker; Gran scene; enter the Heart; solve
   with `G.debug.dungeon.autosolve('rootdeep_heart', { stopAt: 'r14' })`; win with `G.debug.actors.boss('rootdeep.lumbra',
   'win')`; three knots via `G.debug.memory.enter(n)` + skip; the roots scene; assert `lumbraCalm`, `mem:1-3`,
   `stoneSites`, `ability:dig`, `mq-ch14` active, `mq-remember` active, `travel['gate:duskwick']` etc. for the four
   isles and home, `REALMS.rootdeep.hidden === false`). CI matrix entry `chapter:ch13`; the chain check links ch12-end
   to ch13-start.

#### Assets
None new.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch13` passes from the fixture to the end state; the chain check passes.
- `ch13-roots` plays its five lines and staging, sets `stoneSites`, opens the five gates (home and the four visited
  isles) and starts both quests; skipping still applies everything.
- The veins pulse toward the three spur ends within 10 s of the scene's end (pacing rule 4).
- The Rootdeep group appears in Completion; `rootdeep` prints frozen (fixture header).
- Spud stands at his plaza spot at 12:00 the next day and is back in Burrowbottom at 21:00.
- `test:playthrough` unchanged; every earlier chapter test green. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch13`, plus the earlier chapters in order (`ch7` … `ch12`).
- `bun test` (quests, content, world rule 9 for the new spot, realm prints), `node tests/inpage.mjs chapter13`
  ("roots scene sets stoneSites", "veins pulse", "gates opened"), `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope
- Chapter 14 (S-203); the Glassway realm itself (M23); Wenna's later pedestal lines.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: unhiding exposes something half-finished in the Rootdeep; the milestone's steps up to S-190b are all stacked
before this one (dependency), and the realm suite runs all views. Rollback: revert the PR; the realms go back to hidden.

<a id="s-192"></a>
### S-192 · feat: side stories 31 Burrowbottom Lamplight and 32 Lumbra's Itch
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-191b, S-078 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [side-content#ss-31](../bible/side-content.md#ss-31), [side-content#ss-32](../bible/side-content.md#ss-32), [side-content#rules](../bible/side-content.md#rules), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes), [atlas-far#rootdeep-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-states)

#### Why
Two short Rootdeep stories with a mark you can see: a dim mole town relit lamp by lamp (using the Lantern, the Glider
and the Hook together), and a hundred-year itch scratched. Each pays gems and changes the place for good.

#### What
1. **Pass `placeSideMarks`** (`new RNG(40241 * 107 + 19)`, a post-pass on the now-frozen Rootdeep; writes
   `W.sideMarks`, restores `W.walk/W.reach`, decks and solids via `attachPost`). Entries in this order:
   - `rootdeep:lookout-root`: the **Lookout Root**, a root deck (`deck` record) 3 × 6 m, top y 11.5, at (−212, −27) on
     the upper terrace's edge, with a walkable root ramp from (−212, −40) y 9 (slope ≤ 0.35); headroom to the 13 m
     ceiling 1.5 m.
   - `rootdeep:chimney-1`, `-2`: two chimney stacks (`rootdeep_chimney`, code-built 1.2 m square stone stacks with a cap,
     solid) standing on the middle terrace at (−212, −19) and (−204, −17), tops y 8.0, each with a root lamp on top.
     Each top is ≥ 6 m from any walkable ground at y ≥ 7 and 3.5 m below the Lookout Root, so only a glide reaches it
     (solver proves: unreachable on foot, reachable with `glide`).
   - `rootdeep:arch-lamp-1`, `-2`: two hookbloom pads on the Great Burrow Lamp's root arch shoulders at (−170, −5) and
     (−160, −5), deck tops y 9.5, each with a root lamp and a hookbloom above (hooked from the middle terrace, y 6;
     under the 20 m dome of R13).
   - `rootdeep:itch-1..4`: four soft-earth mounds and `rootdeep:itch-ring` one pink itchy ring, laid along Lumbra's
     body when she lies in the **Pinewhistle spur tunnel** (R20: the Glowcap root-gate spur): the body spline runs 14 m
     from the gate room (−100, −242) back down the spur; mounds at 3, 6, 9 and 12 m from her tail, the ring at 13 m (near
     her head). Edit atlas-far's "6 itch spots" to 5 marks in this PR.
2. **ss-31 Burrowbottom Lamplight** (quest `ss-31`, n 131, owner `gran_burrows`, realm `rootdeep`, unlock
   `flag:stoneSites` (R43: side-content's `flag:ch13` meant "the Rootdeep done"), reward `gems:+35`, `set:ss31.done`,
   minutes 20):
   1. `ask` "Burrowbottom is dim" · talk to Gran (priority 40 entry `quest:ss-31.start`): `side.ss31.gran.1` "The roots
      dimmed and our lamps with them. Twelve lamps. Moles don't need much light. We do like a little."
   2. `kindle` "Light all three lamp rows (n/3)" · `[E] Kindle · Root lamp` (Lantern, reach 2 m) at the 8 street lamps
      `rootdeep:lamp-1..8` (S-178), the 2 chimney lamps and the 2 arch lamps. **Circuits** (side-content#ss-31, a
      1 × 4 lights-out per row): row A = `lamp-1..4`; row B = `chimney-1`, `lamp-5`, `lamp-6`, `chimney-2`; row C =
      `arch-lamp-1`, `lamp-7`, `lamp-8`, `arch-lamp-2`. Kindling a lamp toggles it and its row neighbours (state in
      flags `ss31.l<id>` 0/1, rewritable); a row with all four lit sets `ss31.row<A|B|C>` for good and stops toggling;
      `ss31.rows` counts them. From all dark each row is solved by its two end lamps, so the glide (row B) and the hook
      (row C) are still needed. Pure module `js/activities/lightsout.js` (`press(row, i) → row`, `solved(row)`), unit
      tests: the end-lamp solution, every one of the 16 states solvable within 4 presses. Hints `ht.ss31.kindle`
      "Each lamp tugs at its neighbours." / "On each row, kindle only the two end lamps. If a lamp is lit already,
      kindling it puts it out." Each lamp shows its lit state at once (`#ffcf7a`, a soft `sfx.lampOn`; going out, a
      soft `sfx.lampOff`). Teasel's line on the first kindle
      (`side.ss31.teasel.1`): "You light, I'll hold the ladder. There isn't a ladder. I'll hold the idea of one."
   3. `report` "Tell Gran Burrows" · talk; 15 s supper scene `ss31-supper` (replayable): the moles carry a long table into
      the Burrow Tunnel mouth (−104, 0); Gran `side.ss31.gran.2` "There. Burrowbottom's bright again. Digby will smell the
      supper from up top and pretend he came by chance."
   World mark (within 10 s): Burrowbottom's lamps burn warm amber; the Great Burrow Lamp gains a ring of 12 small lamps
   (instanced, `#ffcf7a`); a long supper table (`rootdeep_supper_table`, 8 m, code-built) at the tunnel mouth with
   Gran, Teasel, Loam and Spud at it 18:00-21:00 every evening (schedule rows appended for those four). Burrowers'
   idle line after done (priority 20): Teasel "They all light now. I tend them anyway. It's nice to be needed by a lamp."
   Journal open "A town of moles in the dark. They say they do not mind. They have lamps. They mind." / done "Twelve
   lamps. The moles had supper. Gran Burrows says I am 'a very loud owl'. Fair."
3. **ss-32 Lumbra's Itch** (quest `ss-32`, n 132, owner `lumbra`, unlock `boss:rootdeep_heart:lumbra`, reward
   `gems:+35`, `set:ss32.done`, minutes 15; pronoun **she**, R1):
   1. `ask` "Lumbra has an itch" · talk to Lumbra at the Pinewhistle spur gate room (`rootdeep.ferry-pinewhistle`,
      where she rests between ferries while this story is open): `side.ss32.lumbra.1` "Four stones. Stuck between my
      rings. I can't reach. I have no arms. This is the trouble with being a worm."
   2. `stones` "Dig out the four stones in Lumbra's rings (n/4)" · she lies along the spur tunnel (14 m, the resting-coil
      model laid straight on the spline); `[T] Dig · Soft earth` at `itch-1..4` (Paws, `digAt`); count `ss32.stones`.
   3. `scratch` "Scratch the last itch" · the ring near her head glows pink `#ff9fb0`; one Swing on it (friendly NPCs
      take no damage; the swing plays a soft "scritch" and a ripple down her body).
   Done line `side.ss32.lumbra.2` "Ohhhh. Oh, that's the spot. ...Thank you. A hundred years of root-keeping and nobody ever
   scratched." World mark: the four stones stacked into a tiny cairn beside the tunnel at (−104, −236) (`rootdeep_itch_cairn`);
   Lumbra returns to her resting coil (S-185) with a contented sigh every 30 s within 20 m. Journal open "Earthworms itch.
   This has not been studied. I shall study it. From a distance." / done "Lumbra is scratched. She sighed for an hour. I
   timed it." Edit side-content.md's ss-32 pronouns and the "(n/6)" of ss-11 per R19 is S-195's.
4. **Text** in `js/text/en/side-rootdeep.js`; dialogue entries in `js/content/dialogue/rootdeep.js`; flags `ss31.*`,
   `ss32.*` registered.
5. **Economy:** `js/content/economy.js` ledger rows ss-31 35 and ss-32 35 (side-content#ledger already counts them; the
   economy test must still sum to 1,855 for all side stories once all exist).

#### Assets
- `rootdeep_chimney` (code-built, 220 tris), `rootdeep_supper_table` (code-built 8 × 1 m table with benches, mugs,
  a lantern; 400 tris), `rootdeep_itch_cairn` (four stacked pebbles, 80 tris); ART BRIEFs in the PR (palette
  `#6b4a32`, `#8a5a3c`, `#ffcf7a`, `#c2a98a`; image prompts "a short crooked stone chimney with a little brass lamp on
  top, cosy underground mole town" / "a long wooden supper table in a cosy underground tunnel, mugs and a lantern,
  mole-sized benches" / "four smooth stones stacked into a tiny cairn by a glowing root tunnel"; 3D prompts in the
  preamble form). Lamp lit/dim states reuse `rootdeep_lamp_post`.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Post-pass test (atlas-far#far-passes): `generateRealm('rootdeep')` with and without `placeSideMarks` has equal prints;
  seeds 1-20 of the `cavern` recipe likewise; the chimney lamps are unreachable on foot and reachable with `glide`; the
  arch lamps reachable with `hook`.
- In-page: ss-31 from Gran's talk through the six end-lamp kindles (rows A, B, C) to the supper scene pays 35 gems; the town is lit and the table
  appears within 10 s; ss-32 from talk through 4 digs and the scratch pays 35 gems; the cairn appears.
- Nothing can be failed; leaving mid-story keeps the counts.
- Content lint (lengths, markers; Lumbra "she"). Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js` (post-pass group), `bun test tests/progress-graph.test.js`,
  `bun test tests/quests.test.js`, `bun test tests/content.test.js`, `bun test tests/economy.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (add "ss-31 relight", "ss-32 itch").

#### Out of scope
- ss-33 and For Spring's grove (S-193); Lumbra's Dream (S-295).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the Lookout Root's headroom under the 13 m ceiling is tight in TP; the TP arm probe already caps under `over`
ceilings (shipped). Rollback: revert; the post-pass is additive.

<a id="s-193"></a>
### S-193 · feat: side story 33 Gran's Pouch, Gran's burrow, and For Spring's golden grove
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-191b, S-114, S-036 · Size: M (about 430 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [side-content#ss-33](../bible/side-content.md#ss-33), [side-content#ss-01](../bible/side-content.md#ss-01), [atlas-home#grove](../bible/atlas-home.md#grove), [atlas-far#rootdeep-interiors](../bible/atlas-far.md#rootdeep-interiors), [cast#gran-burrows](../bible/cast.md#gran-burrows)

#### Why
The LOCKED "For Spring" keepsake was always Digby's. Gran Burrows stitched it; she stitches a new one ("and Summer"),
and Digby finally plants his grove in the Meadow, a spring he can smell from his own door. It ties the first secret of
the shipped game to its Act III family.

#### What
1. **Gran's burrow** interior `burrowbottom_homes` (R16; kind `interior`, dungeon recipe, realm grid 48 m, parent
   `rootdeep`, ceiling 3 m, clock runs). Map (tile 2 m; legend extension `h` hearth (solid), `T` table (solid), `S`
   Gran's sewing chair (her indoors spot), `b` the thread basket, `?` the framed picture (Look)):
   ```map burrowbottom_homes L1
   #################
   #t...h...t#t...t#
   #.........#.....#
   #...TT....D..S..#
   #...TT....#..b..#
   #.........#..?..#
   #t.......t#t...t#
   #....@....#######
   #####X###########
   ```
   Rooms: parlour [0, 0, 11, 8], sewing nook [10, 0, 7, 7]. Door portal `rootdeep:homes` at Gran's door (−183, −36):
   `[E] Enter · Gran's burrow` replaces S-178's Knock; exit `X` → Rootdeep spawn `gran_door` (−183, −34.5). Gran's
   `indoors` rows now place her at `S` (19:00-07:00). Dressing: the "For Spring" thread basket (`b`, green `#6f8f3a` and
   gold `#f2c94c` thread), and on the nook's north wall the framed root-bark picture of young Digby in a helmet too big
   for him (`?`, `[E] Look · Picture`: "Digby in his first helmet. It came down over his nose. He wore it to bed for a
   week.").
2. **ss-33 Gran's Pouch** (quest `ss-33`, n 133, owner `gran_burrows`, unlock `quest:ss-01>=3` and `flag:stoneSites`
   (R43), reward `gems:+30`, `set:ss33.done`, minutes 15):
   1. `show` "Show Gran Burrows the 'For Spring' pouch" · talk (the keepsake is in the Keepsake Hollow; talking is
      enough): `side.ss33.gran.1` "That's my stitching! Made it for Digby when he went up top. I told him: plant
      something you can smell."
   2. `seeds` "Gather three root-blossom seeds (n/3)" · three glowing seeds in Glowcap Forest, visible only inside the
      Lantern's light (alpha 0 outside 6 m of a raised Lantern; a soft honey hum `sfx.honeyHum` within 8 m is the cue),
      `[E] Pick up · Root-blossom seed`; count `ss33.seeds`. Gran's hint line `side.ss33.gran.2` "Root-blossom seeds.
      They glow, and they smell of honey. He'll know them by the honey. That's the point."
   3. `stitch` "Gran stitches a new pouch" · talk; item-get key item `pouch_spring2` (name "For Spring (and Summer)",
      desc "Gran's new pouch, green felt and gold thread. Three root-blossom seeds inside, smelling of honey."); line
      `side.ss33.gran.3` "'For Spring (and Summer).' I added summer. He'll want summer next. He always wants the next
      thing." Opens ss-01 step 4.
   Seeds via `placeSideMarks` (appended after S-192's entries): `rootdeep:seed-1` `near((62, −168), 2, 8)` (glade B),
   `seed-2` `near((2, −226), 2, 8)` (glade C), `seed-3` `near((−30, −200), 2, 8)` (by the heart-shaped moss).
   World mark: Gran's sewing chair outside her door (−181, −33) with a lit lamp (`rootdeep_sewing_chair`) where she sits
   07:00-12:00, and the framed picture inside. Journal open "Gran Burrows recognised the pouch at once. Mothers and
   grandmothers always do." / done "A new pouch. Summer added. Gran says Digby 'wants the next thing'. So do I. It is
   called curiosity."
3. **ss-01 For Spring, part 2** (S-114 shipped steps 1-3; this step adds 4-5 to the same quest, R53: atlas-home owns the
   grove's place and size):
   4. `pouch` "Bring Gran's new pouch to Digby" · talk to Digby with `item:pouch_spring2`: `side.ss01.digby.5` "That's
      Gran's stitching. 'For Spring (and Summer).' She thinks of everything. Even summer."
   5. `grove` "Plant the grove with Digby (n/9)" · `[E] Place · Golden seed` at the nine soft-earth rings on the grove
      circle (atlas-home#grove: G = `near((−84, 12), 0, 15)` with a clear r 7 m disc; nine spots on the r 5 m circle;
      `placeGrove` row 12, `new RNG(7151 * 109 + 53)`, the spots S-114 already placed; if S-114 placed only the part-1
      sprouts, append the ring records to the same pass's list after them). Any order; Digby stands at G while this step
      is active. As the ninth goes in, over 6 s the nine sprouts grow into **golden saplings** (2.2 m) and the **golden
      tree** (4.5 m) rises at G with a sign `[E] Read · Sign`: "For Spring. — D."
   Done: `gems:+20` (ss-01's second payment), `set:ss01.done`; lines `side.ss01.digby.6` "Can you smell that, Pip?
   That's spring. Right on time. ...Don't tell me what it looks like. I want to guess." and Ribbit after done
   (priority 20, once) `side.ss01.ribbit.1` "A grove! In my Meadow! And nobody asked for a permit. Croak. ...I suppose I
   shall allow it." Journal done "It was Digby! I was nearly right. He is small and tidy. The grove smells remarkable."
   Digby's grove row: `[5,'home.grove','work']` until 06:00 (R54: the protected-day rule keeps LOCKED villagers on their
   shipped spots 06:00-19:48, so the grove visit is at dawn; edit side-content's "06:00 to 09:00" to "05:00 to 06:00").
   Edit side-content's ss-01 step 5 to "(n/9)" and "nine saplings" in this PR.
4. **Tests:** home `placeGrove` rule 9 on seeds 1-40 if the pass list grows; the Rootdeep post-pass comparison for the
   seed marks.

#### Assets
- `golden_tree` (code-built per atlas-home's brief: `cyl` trunk `#a8723e`, `sphere` crowns `#ffe9a8` / `#f5c040`, cream
  blossom `#fff6e2`, leaves `glow` 0.15, sway 0.03 rad; tree ≤ 900 tris, sapling 300; gold petals drifting as a particle);
  ART BRIEF from atlas-home#grove in the PR.
- `rootdeep_sewing_chair` (code-built 0.8 m chair with a lamp, 200 tris), `root_blossom_seed` (0.15 m glowing seed
  `#ffcf7a` with a honey-coloured halo sprite, 40 tris), `pouch_spring2` icon (items atlas cell: "a small green felt
  pouch with gold stitching reading nothing, a tiny summer sun stitched beside a sprout, icon, transparent background";
  fallback canvas glyph).
- Interior kit pieces (hearth, table, basket, picture frame) code-built in `js/props-interior.js` with one shared ART
  BRIEF "Gran's burrow furnishings" (palette `#8a5a3c`, `#b8a6d0`, `#6f8f3a`, `#f2c94c`; image prompt "a cosy round
  mole burrow parlour with a little hearth, a lilac shawl on a chair, a sewing basket of green and gold thread, a framed
  bark picture of a small mole in a big helmet").

#### Acceptance criteria
- `burrowbottom_homes` compiles, Gran is inside 19:00-07:00 and at her chair 07:00-12:00; the realm suite views are
  ≤ 120 draw calls.
- ss-33 from talk to `pouch_spring2` pays 30 gems; seeds are invisible without the Lantern raised nearby.
- ss-01 steps 4-5: nine plantings grow the grove within 10 s of the last; the sign reads; 20 gems paid; Ribbit's line
  once; Digby at the grove 05:00-06:00 and on his shipped spot at 06:00.
- Home prints byte-identical; post-pass tests pass. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (placeGrove), `bun test tests/realms/rootdeep.test.js`, `bun test tests/quests.test.js`,
  `bun test tests/content.test.js`, `bun test tests/schedule.test.js` (Digby's dawn row vs the protected day).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` ("ss-33 pouch", "ss-01 grove grows"), `node tests/inpage.mjs npc`.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope
- Gran's later Act lines; the Planting (ss-53, S-291).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: S-114's grove pass differs from atlas-home's (6 trail sprouts instead of 9 circle spots); the step reads what
shipped and plants on the circle spots of `placeGrove`, adding them only by appending. Rollback: revert; post-passes are
additive.

<a id="s-194"></a>
### S-194 · feat: Grumble's Undercap: rooms 1-8 under Grumble's Crown
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-181b, S-057, S-053, S-074 · Size: M (about 380 lines with map data) · Save: none · Flag: none (reachable once the hole is dug) · Bible: [dungeons#o2](../bible/dungeons.md#o2), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [atlas-home#dig-spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#dig-spots), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table), [art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles)

#### Why
The first optional home dungeon: a 45-minute Lantern-and-Paws crawl under Grumble's Crown to a tiny shroomlet town
that has been dark since the Tree drooped. It gives home a new secret right where Act I began, and sets up ss-11.

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/undercap.js`: the map `undercap L1` (41 × 19), legend extension (`g` glowbud door, `l`
   wall-lantern / night-light, `d` soft earth, `V` thornvine door), room table r1-r8, room notes, ledger and hints
   copied **verbatim** from [dungeons#o2](../bible/dungeons.md#o2) (S-049's map test). Fields: `id: 'undercap'`, name
   "Grumble's Undercap", sub-line "under Grumble's Crown", `kind: 'dungeon'`, `tile: 2`, realm grid 98 m, palette
   (stone `#4b2f5c`, trim `#7b5a3a`, accent `#e8b84a`, glow `#b6f0a0`, dark tint `#1f1428`), `light: { ambient: 0.45,
   dark: ['r3', 'r4', 'r6'] }` (dark rooms at 0.22, R8), music `undercap` (art-audio#track-data `{ from: 'depths',
   transpose: -5, voices: { lead: 'bell' } }`, pizzicato feel, 90 bpm), `parent: 'home'`, `bundle: 'postgame'`
   (art-audio#bundles; the Snowglobe, Mabel, Dreams and Hundred Lamps share it), `needsOnEntry: ['lantern', 'mitts']`
   (and the Shiny Sword for r3's thornvine).
2. **Entities and waves** exactly as the room notes: r1 sign "UNDERCAP. Mind the little ones. And the gills. And your
   head. — G."; r2 three mounds (15,3)-(15,5) (dig one), map `&` (19,2); r3 (dark) Dimling ×2 at (23,2), (27,2), thornvine
   door `V` (30,4) (three sword hits, `sword2`); r4 (dark) Kindle the four lanterns (31,1), (39,1), (31,7), (39,7) →
   glowbud door (35,8) opens (`lit`); r5 four mounds, (33,11) sparkles twice as much and holds small key `r5:k1`, the
   others hold a glowcap each ("A glowcap. It wiggles."), locked door (30,12); r6 **Shroomlet Town** (dark, a town: no
   enemies, region max 0) with four mushroom houses (pillars `I`) and **eight** unlit night-lights `r6:l1-l8` at (21,9),
   (23,9), (27,9), (29,9), (21,17), (23,17), (27,17), (29,17) (R19), sign `?`(25,13) "SHROOMLET TOWN. Lights out at
   eight. (They went out at eight and stayed out.)", door (20,12) `always`; r7 Dozy Bat ×2 (`bat`) at (13,11), (17,11),
   gem chest `r7:c1` (19,15) walled by mounds (18,15), (19,14), glowbud door (10,12) `lanternOnly`; r8 Grumble's old throne
   (a toadstool on a dais), the Spore Rally board `?`(2,14) "SPORE RALLY. Bonk the spores back. Best: 0. Grumble says
   beat that.", gem chest `r8:c2` (1,15). Ledger: `r5:k1` → L (30,12); `r7:c1` + `r8:c2` = 80 gems.
3. **The night-lights in r6** are kindle targets (`[E] Kindle · Night-light`, reach 2 m); their lit state is saved in
   `S.dungeons.undercap.levers['l1'..'l8']`; when all eight are lit, set `undercapLit` (dungeons#o2's flag, registered)
   and the room's ambient rises to 0.6 with a `#b6f0a0` glow on the house windows. What that means for the nephews and
   the surface is S-195's.
4. **Portal:** S-181b's walk-in hole `home:undercap` now resolves: `to: { realm: 'undercap', spawn: 'entry' }`, needs
   `flag:gate:home:undercap` (R41). `X` tiles → home spawn `undercap` (1.5 m out from the hole). Register `undercap` in
   `REALMS` with `hidden: false` (optional dungeons are whole when their step lands). Name card on first entry.
5. **Solver** (full): r1 → r2 (dig, `&`) → r3 (cut thornvine) → r4 (Kindle ×4) → r5 (dig (33,11), `k1`) → unlock → r6
   (Kindle ×8, optional for progress) → r7 (dig, `c1`) → r8 (`c2`); both chests and the map reachable; no soft-lock.
6. **Ask the Tree hints** verbatim: "Bramble in a doorway: a job for a sword." / "Swing at the purple thornvine three
   times."; "One mound sparkles more than the others." / "Dig the mound in the Mossy Steps that sparkles the most. The
   key is in it."; "Little houses, little lights." / "Kindle all eight night-lights in Shroomlet Town with the Lantern."
7. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/undercap.json`; realm-suite entry (4 views: r1, r4 lit, r6 town, r8 throne).
8. **Completion:** the Undercap's 2 chests count in home's chests row (systems#completion: optional dungeons count toward
   their land).

#### Assets
- `tex_gills` (realm tag `postgame` bundle): "Seamless texture of the soft underside of a giant purple mushroom cap,
  gentle gill ridges in plum and lilac with tiny gold spores, tileable" with the tile kind suffix, `python3 -I
  tools/make_seamless.py <raw> <out> --blend 64`, hd 1024 / lo 512 webp (≤ 110 / 40 KB); fallback `#4b2f5c` stripes.
- `int_shroom_house` (code-built 1.6 m, cap `#c2563f` with cream spots `#f4e3c8`, stem `#f1e6d0`, round door
  `#7b5a3a`, a tiny window that glows when lit; 300 tris) and `int_night_light` (0.5 m acorn-cap lantern on a twig
  post, unlit grey, lit `#b6f0a0`; 60 tris), `int_toadstool_throne` (1.4 m toadstool on a 3-step dais, 260 tris).
  ART BRIEF `int_shroom_house` from dungeons#o2 verbatim, plus short briefs for the night-light and the throne.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes for `undercap`; the solver proves both chests, the map and the exit.
- In-page: dig the home hole → enter the Undercap; the thornvine needs the Shiny Sword; the four r4 lanterns open (35,8);
  the sparkly mound holds the key; kindling the 8 night-lights sets `undercapLit` and raises r6's light; leaving keeps
  every state; dizzy wakes Pip at `@`.
- Dark rooms: luminance ≥ 0.10 at the entrances with the Lantern off; room views ≤ 120 draw calls.
- `postgame` bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; core unchanged. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan

<a id="s-195"></a>
### S-195 · feat: side story 11 Grumble's Nephews: relight the shroomlet town
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-194, S-158, S-155 · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [side-content#ss-11](../bible/side-content.md#ss-11), [cast#catchers](../bible/cast.md#catchers), [atlas-home#placeReasons](../bible/atlas-home.md#placeReasons), [side-content#act-spore-rally](../bible/side-content.md#act-spore-rally)

#### Why
Grumble's kind reason in Act I was the little ones' fear of the dark: the three unlit night-lights by his Hollow sign
were always theirs. Relighting their town and those night-lights pays off the very first guardian, and opens the Spore
Rally's top tier "in front of the nephews".

#### What
1. **The nephews** (cast#catchers): `shroomlet_button`, `shroomlet_chanty`, `shroomlet_puff`, 0.5 m each, the shipped
   shroomlet look recoloured friendly (caps `#e85a5a` / `#f2b45a` / `#c9b6ec`, open `face()` eyes, no spore attack;
   they never use the enemy `mushroom` id). If an earlier step already added them (grep `shroomlet_button`), reuse
   those records. In the Undercap's r6 they peek from the houses until `undercapLit`, then stand in the town.
2. **Quest `ss-11`** (n 111, owner `grumble` (the `woods` boss NPC), unlock `flag:ch13` and `ability:dig` (R44:
   side-content's `ability:mitts` is `ability:dig`), reward `gems:+40`, `set:ss11.done`, minutes 20):
   1. `ask` "Grumble asks a favour" · talk to Grumble in his Hollow (after the bloom he naps; `[E] Talk` wakes him for
      talk only, `[E] Wake` stays the rematch): `side.ss11.grumble.1` "The little ones' night-lights. Under my Crown. They
      went out years back. Hmph. Don't make a fuss." then `side.ss11.grumble.2` "And don't tell them I asked. I have a
      reputation. It is GRUMPY." Grumble's map cross marks the Undercap hole.
   2. `enter` "Dig into Grumble's Undercap" · done `realm:undercap.visited`.
   3. `relight` "Relight the shroomlet town (n/8)" · count = lit `r6:l1-l8` (R19: eight, not six; edit side-content's
      ss-11 step 3 in this PR); on the first lit one, Button `side.ss11.shroomlet.1` "It's the hood! The hood came! Uncle
      Grumble said a hood might come!"
   4. `nightlights` "Light the three night-lights by the Hollow sign" · `[E] Kindle · Night-light` × 3 on the surface at
      `home:reason-grumble-1..3` (placeReasons row 1; unlit until now, `reason_nightlight` lit state `#ffd27a`, pulse
      0.9-1.0 at 0.5 Hz); count `ss11.lights`.
   Done line `side.ss11.grumble.3` "...They're asleep. All of them. First bedtime in a hundred years with nobody crying.
   Hmph. Good." and `side.ss11.grumble.4` "Spore Rally. Thirty. In front of the nephews. Now they'll be watching, I'll do
   it properly." Journal open "Grumble Shroom has asked for a favour. Grumble Shroom does not ask for favours. Note the
   date." / done "The nephews sleep. Grumble pretends not to care. He has said 'Hmph' eleven times today, all of them
   happy."
3. **World mark** (within 10 s of the last step): the three night-lights by the Hollow sign glow every night (18:00-06:00);
   at dusk (18:00-21:00) the three nephews sit in a row on the Hollow sign (perch spots on the sign's top board, schedule
   rows `[[18,'home.hollow_sign','sit'],[21,'home.undercap','indoors']]`); the Undercap's town glows warm through its
   soft-earth entrance (an additive `#b6f0a0` light card in the hole, visible from 20 m at night).
4. **Spore Rally tier 3** (S-158's activity): the 30-hit tier now needs `ss11.done` ("In front of the nephews"): the
   nephews stand at the Hollow's edge and cheer while Pip plays (a cheer sound every 10 hits). Reward unchanged (the
   plushie hat on every Nook plushie).
5. **Text and flags:** `js/text/en/side-home.js` keys above; flags `ss11.*` registered; dialogue entries for the nephews
   (priority 10 idle, after done): Button "I'm not scared of the dark. I'm scared of no lights. That's different." ·
   Chanty "Why is the dark dark? Why are you a hood? Why is Uncle Grumble so big?" · Puff "(Puff hides under his cap.
   He's waving from under there.)" — Puff's line is a stage direction; the content lint allows a fully parenthesised
   line as a non-speech line.

#### Assets
- Nephew looks are recolours (no new models). Portraits: none (name only). ART BRIEF note in the PR for a later
  `npc_shroomlet` GLB (0.5 m, ≤ 2,500 tris, three cap colourways).

#### Acceptance criteria
- In-page: ss-11 from Grumble's talk through the Undercap's eight lights and the three surface night-lights pays 40
  gems; the night-lights glow at 22:00; the nephews sit on the sign at 19:00; the hole glows at night.
- The Spore Rally's 30-hit tier is locked before `ss11.done` and open after.
- `wp9`'s Act I reason-prop counts are unchanged (the night-lights stay unlit before ss-11) and `test:playthrough` is
  unchanged.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/quests.test.js`, `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` ("ss-11 relight"), `node tests/inpage.mjs activities` ("spore rally tier 3 gated"),
  `node tests/inpage.mjs wp9`.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

<a id="s-196"></a>
### S-196 · perf: checkpoint 5: a mid-game soak across every realm and a full save
Milestone M22 · Depends on: S-191b (and S-192-S-195 stacked), S-151 · Size: M (about 350 lines: tests, fixtures, fixes) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [charter#budgets](../bible/charter.md#budgets), [charter#budgets-checkpoints](../bible/charter.md#budgets-checkpoints), [charter#budgets-over](../bible/charter.md#budgets-over), [charter#budgets-save](../bible/charter.md#budgets-save), [charter#close-out-perf](../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf), [systems#save-plan](../bible/systems.md#save-plan)

#### Why
Half the game's realms exist now: home, four isles, the Rootdeep, six dungeons, a variant, Hollows and interiors. Before
the sea and the sky are added, prove the engine still holds: no leaks over many swaps, every view in budget, and a save
far under 80 KB with everything set.

#### What
1. **Fixtures:**
   - `tests/fixtures/saves/act3-start.json`: the `ch13` end state (from the chapter test) with every side story and
     collectible available so far done (Act I-III-start flags, all four isles' and the Rootdeep's finds, bosses calmed,
     plushies bought, Hollows cleared).
   - `tests/fixtures/saves/full-100.json`: a **synthetic worst case** written by `tools/make-full-save.mjs` (new, pure):
     every key in the flag registry set (numbers at their max), every quest `'done'`, every `collect` map filled with
     every id the content registries and atlas totals define (35 + home acorns… all 12 bottles, 30 logs, 24 petals, 31
     vistas, 24 fish at 999, 24 Hollows, 10 Dash times, 10 Dreams), every chest, keepsake, boss, plushie and rematch key,
     every bench and gate in `travel`, every bestiary key at 9999, `dungeons` with every dungeon's maximal state.
2. **Save size test** (`tests/save-size.test.js`): `JSON.stringify(normalize(full-100)).length < 80 * 1024` and
   `act3-start` printed; a v6 → current migration of `v6-bloomed.json` still Continues (save tests unchanged).
3. **Soak suite** `js/debug/tests/soak.js` (`node tests/inpage.mjs soak`; local only, heavy; CI runs `soak:quick`, 3
   cycles, in the matrix): from `act3-start`, 20 cycles through home → duskwick → pinewhistle → archwater →
   threemills → rootdeep → rootdeep_heart → rootdeep → remembered (memory 1 entered and left) → rootdeep →
   burrowbottom_homes → rootdeep → (root-gate ferry) home → undercap → home → one Hollow per land → home. Each cycle:
   enter, wait for `EV.REALM`, warm 2 s, sample; at the end of each cycle record `renderer.info.memory` (geometries,
   textures), `renderer.info.programs.length`, `performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize` where available, and `game.modErrs`.
   Pass: geometries and textures back within ±2 % of cycle 1, programs not growing after cycle 2, heap growth < 10 %
   from cycle 2 to 20, `modErrs` empty, every swap under its budget (≤ 1.2 s generation; < 100 ms back to a resident
   parent).
4. **Views:** the realm suite in both cameras for every shipped and built realm (home, 4 isles, rootdeep, glassway not
   yet; the dungeons' room views; remembered; undercap) recording main and total draw calls and triangles; plus local GPU
   median and p90 for the main pass (`js/debug/gpu-timer.js`).
5. **Browser memory on the machine:** run the soak inside a systemd scope with a cgroup cap and read the cgroup's
   `memory.current` (never summed RSS): `systemd-run --user --scope -p MemoryMax=5G node tests/inpage.mjs soak`, record
   peak `memory.current` and confirm it stays under 4.5 GB with SwiftShader; record GPU-mode numbers when a GPU run is
   available.
6. **Fix what fails** in the charter's order (cull, instance or merge, LOD, fewer types, shadows, move the view's
   content) in this PR if under ~500 lines; otherwise file the over-budget view as the next bug step and name it.
7. **Records:** `docs/roadmap/progress.md` checkpoint-5 entry (template) and the DESIGN §14 performance table row for the
   new worst view.

#### Assets
None.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `full-100` normalises to < 80 KB (number recorded); `act3-start` size recorded.
- Soak 20 cycles passes the leak, program, heap, error and swap criteria (numbers recorded); `soak:quick` passes in CI.
- Every shipped realm view within budget in both cameras (main ≤ 200, total ≤ 300, ≤ 1.3 M tris), or a bug step filed
  for each that is not, named in the PR.
- Peak browser cgroup memory recorded and under 4.5 GB.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
`free -h` (≥ 6 GB), then one at a time: `bun test tests/save-size.test.js`; `bun test`;
`systemd-run --user --scope -p MemoryMax=5G node tests/inpage.mjs soak`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
`node tests/inpage.mjs soak:quick`; `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope
- New content; budget changes (only an owner-answered `agent:decision` issue may change a budget, charter decision 25).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the soak is too slow for CI; only `soak:quick` runs there. Rollback: revert the tests; fixes are reverted with
them only if they regress a view.
