<a id="s-198"></a>
### S-198 · feat: boat locomotion: steer the Dandelion on the water plane (behind a flag)
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-021 · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `boat` off by default (`?boat=1`, `G.debug.boat(true)`; `REALMS.<id>.sail`) · Bible: [systems#boat](../bible/systems.md#boat), [systems#brief-boat](../bible/systems.md#brief-boat), [cast#nell](../bible/cast.md#nell), [charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person)

#### Why
The Glassway is a sea made into a land: islets, sandbars and wrecks Pip reaches by steering Nell's *Dandelion* himself
(owner decision 4, free sailing). This is a third way of moving (after walking and gliding), built first in a sandbox so
the Glassway recipe can rely on it, and kept behind a flag so nothing shipped changes.

#### What
1. **`js/boat.js`** (pure, `bun test`-able): `stepBoat(b, input, dt, W, depthAt) → b` with state `{ x, z, heading,
   speed, bob, roll }` and the numbers of systems#boat:

   | Number | Value |
   |---|---|
   | Top speed | 9 m/s (11 with the Sail upgrade: `b.topK`, read from `item:dandelion_sail` when it exists) |
   | Acceleration / braking | 2.5 m/s² / 4 m/s² (Roll brakes) |
   | Reverse | 2 m/s |
   | Turn rate | 55°/s at full speed, 80°/s below 3 m/s (linear between) |
   | Bob / roll | 0.12 m at a 2.4 s period; roll ±3° (into the turn, scaled by speed) |
   | Hull | a 1.8 m circle against the collider hash (`moveCircle` with `r: 1.8` and the boat as the mover) |
   | Depth | water shallower than **0.7 m** is a wall (R29: atlas-far's 0.7 wins; fix systems.md's 0.6 in this PR); `depthAt(x, z) = waterY − W.H` |
   | Bump | a wall or shallows stops the boat (speed × −0.2, a soft thud `sfx.boatThud`, a foam puff); never damage |

   Input: move stick / WASD: forward = throttle up, back = brake then reverse, left/right = turn; Roll = brake; Hop = horn
   toot (`sfx.horn`, a two-note toot, just for fun); the view key toggles cameras as on foot; interact is S-199's Dock.
2. **Player state `boat`** (`js/player.js`): a new `case 'boat'` that skips `integrate`, copies `pos` from the boat (deck
   height = water level + 0.45 + bob), sets `facing = heading`, disables attack, dash, jump and lock-on; Pip cannot get
   dizzy aboard; enemies' target selection skips a Pip whose state is `boat` (nothing attacks the boat); scenes do not
   start while sailing (the scene runner's start guard), except the ch14 arrival (S-203 whitelists its id).
3. **`js/boat-mod.js`** (registered in `js/modules.js`): owns the boat object for the active realm, builds the model,
   runs `stepBoat` in the fixed step, wake foam above 2 m/s (`glassway_card_bow_wave` 6-frame strip when loaded, else a
   canvas foam sprite), sail bellies by speed (scale 1.0-1.15 on its normal axis), the `bowWave` emitter (lowpass noise
   700 Hz, volume 0.03-0.09 by speed; art-audio#sfx), and music: crossfade to the shipped `sailing` track after 2 s above
   3 m/s, back to the region's layer 4 s after stopping.
4. **Model** `model_dandelion`: reuse the code model S-068/S-081 built (grep `dandelion` in `js/props.js`); if missing,
   build it per [systems#brief-boat](../bible/systems.md#brief-boat) (scaled flattened lathe hull `#f3e3c3` with a leaf
   stripe `#7aa63a`, deck `#c9a06b`, 6 m mast, curved triangle sail `#f6d36b` with the seed-clock emblem `#fffbe8`,
   tiller, bow lantern hook, bench; 2,500 tris, LOD proxy 300). **Nell** sits at the tiller in TP (her `bird` rig,
   seated pose); in FP her hat brim and bill show at the bottom-right of the view (a viewmodel overlay of her `hat` and
   `bill` parts, cast#nell).
5. **Cameras** (`js/view/rig-fp.js`, `rig-tp.js`): a `boat` profile: FP eye 1.5 m above the deck at the tiller with the
   bow and the sail in view (look free ±110° yaw from the heading, pitch −60°..+50°); TP boom 6.5 m, pitch −14°, pivot
   1.2 m above the deck, follows the heading with 0.4 s lag. No motion-sickness roll on the camera: the camera stays
   level, only the boat rolls (charter comfort).
6. **Sandbox water realm** `sea_sandbox` (`js/realms/recipes/sea-sandbox.js`, `kind: 'sea'`, 192 m, seed 9001, debug only,
   `hidden: true`, never in the chart): deep water y −6, a shelf disc r 40 at (0, 0) y −2.4, three round islets (r 12 at (−50, −40),
   (60, 10), (−20, 60), top y 3) each with a pier stub, and a sandbar spline crest y −0.9 between two of them; water
   level 0. Used by the tests.
7. **Debug:** `G.debug.boat(on)`, `G.debug.boat.place(x, z, heading)`, `G.debug.boat.state()`.

#### Assets
- `model_dandelion` code-built (or reused); the ART BRIEF from systems#brief-boat verbatim in the PR (key
  `model_dandelion`, 2,500 tris, image and 3D prompts), slot commented out.
- No new images (the bow-wave strip is S-202's; the canvas foam is the fallback).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Unit (`tests/boat.test.js`): from rest, full throttle reaches 9 m/s in 3.6 s ± 0.1; braking from 9 m/s stops in
  2.25 s ± 0.1; turn rate 55°/s at 9 m/s and 80°/s at 2 m/s; a straight run at a 0.6 m-deep shelf edge stops at the
  edge; a run at an islet collider stops without passing through; reverse caps at 2 m/s.
- In-page (`sea_sandbox`, `?boat=1`): board via `G.debug.boat(true)`, sail a scripted loop round the three islets in
  both cameras with no clipping into islets; FP shows bow, sail and Nell's hat; no attack, dash or jump works aboard.
- With the flag off, nothing shipped changes: `test:playthrough`, `wp1`-`wp9` unchanged.
- Sandbox view budgets: ≤ 200 main calls (the boat adds ≤ 6 draw calls).
- Invariants: all lines except the precache line.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/boat.test.js` (new).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs boat` (new suite, CI matrix: "boat accel/turn in sandbox", "shallows are walls", "FP shows bow
  and Nell's hat", "no attack aboard").
- `bun run test:playthrough`, `node tests/inpage.mjs wp1`.

#### Out of scope
- Boarding and docking prompts, the save, Calling and the chart-stop fallback (S-199); the Glassway itself (S-200).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: free sailing proves too costly or unpleasant in FP; the bible's fallback (chart stops, S-199's switch) is the
plan B and nothing in the critical path needs free sailing. Rollback: revert; the flag is off.

<a id="s-199"></a>
### S-199 · feat: board, dock and remember the boat; save v10 keeps its place
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-198, S-071 · Size: M (about 430 lines) · Save: v9 -> v10 · Flag: `REALMS.<id>.sail = 'free' | 'chart'` · Bible: [systems#boat](../bible/systems.md#boat), [systems#save-v10](../bible/systems.md#save-v10), [systems#save-plan](../bible/systems.md#save-plan), [atlas-far#glassway-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-arrival)

#### Why
A boat you can leave somewhere has to be found where you left it, even after closing the game. This step adds the save
field (the one schema change of the Glassway), boarding and docking, the Starsong's *Calling* hook, and the bible's
fallback switch so the Glassway can ship either way.

#### What
1. **Save v10** (`js/save.js`): `SAVE_VERSION = 10`; `MIGRATIONS[9] = (s) => ({ ...s, boat: { realm: null, x: 0, z: 0,
   heading: 0 } })`; `normalize`: `boat.realm` must be the id of a realm in `REALMS` whose `kind` is `'sea'` (import `REALMS` from
   `js/realms/index.js`, as S-024 already does), else `null`; `js/save.js` stays pure and never reads `G`. Today that
   admits `'glassway'` and `'sea_sandbox'`; `sea_sandbox` stays unreachable in a release build because it is
   `hidden: true`. `x, z` finite and inside that realm's half-size, else
   `realm = null`; `heading` wrapped to [0, 2π). When `realm` is null the boat waits at the nearest pier of the realm Pip
   is in. The backup copy (`starseed-isle-save-v2-bak`) is written before migrating (S-003's rule). `freshState()` has
   `boat` with realm null.
2. **Tests** (`tests/save.test.js`): v2, v3, v4, v5, v6, v7, v8, v9 → v10 each keep every existing field and add `boat`;
   garbage `boat` values normalised (realm `'home'` → null; `x: NaN` → realm null; heading 7.0 → 0.717); the v6 fixture
   still Continues in `test:smoke`; a v10 fixture `tests/fixtures/saves/v10-aboard.json` (aboard in `sea_sandbox` under
   debug) Continues aboard.
3. **Docking spots** (realm data `W.docks = [{ id, x, z, facing, deckY, deep }]`; the Glassway's nine piers in S-200;
   the sandbox's three stubs here): aboard and within 6 m of a dock's end → `[E] Dock`; Pip steps onto the landing
   (0.6 s), the boat stays moored at the dock (bow along `facing`), autosave. On foot within 3 m of the moored boat or of a
   dock with the boat moored → `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` boards (0.6 s). At a **jetty** of a land without free sailing
   (home and the four isles) the shipped prompt keeps opening the chart (S-081), unchanged.
4. **Saving aboard:** autosave writes `S.boat = { realm, x, z, heading }` on docking, on every autosave tick while aboard,
   and on leaving the realm; Continue with `S.realm.id === S.boat.realm` and a flag `boatAboard` set puts Pip aboard at the
   boat's place (`RealmManager.enter` step 8: "restore the boat if `S.boat.realm === id`").
5. **Calling stub:** `boat.call(realm)` moves the moored or adrift boat to the dock nearest Pip over 4 s (it sails there
   visibly if within 120 m, else appears with a splash card); the Starsong wires it in S-208. Debug `G.debug.boat.call()`.
6. **The fallback switch** (`REALMS.glassway.sail = 'chart'`, default `'free'`; owner decision 4, read from
   the vision issue's Owner decisions (or, if the owner left it open, the council record that settled it; INDEX §7); no `agent:decision` issue is filed for it): with `'chart'`, no boat
   locomotion in that realm; `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` at any dock opens a **sub-chart** (`js/hud/subchart.js`: the
   realm's map art or its canvas map with the docks and buoys as buttons, a dotted line from the current dock); picking
   one plays a **3 s** card (R28; `load_sailing` with a caption "Nell steers between the islets") and arrives at that
   dock's landing. Content that needs the boat itself (fishing from the boat, the "while sailing" enemy row, which R34
   removes anyway) is skipped. Implemented and tested here on `sea_sandbox` with `sail: 'chart'`.
7. **Dizzy at sea** (systems#dizzy-rules): in a `sea` realm Pip wakes at the nearest dock aboard ("Pip woke up in the
   *Dandelion*.").
8. Record in the PR how to flip the switch (one line in `js/realms/index.js`) for the vision Log if the fallback is used.

#### Assets
None new (`load_sailing` from S-081).

#### Acceptance criteria
- All migrations v2..v9 → v10 pass and keep every field; `normalize` drops bad `boat` values as listed; v6 fixture
  Continues; `v10-aboard` Continues aboard at the saved position and heading (in-page, debug).
- In-page sandbox: Dock at a stub moors the boat and saves; walking away and reloading finds the boat at that dock;
  Sail boards; `G.debug.boat.call()` brings the boat to the nearest dock in 4 s ± 0.2.
- With `sail: 'chart'`: Sail at a dock opens the sub-chart; picking a dock plays a 3 s card and lands Pip on it; no
  boat physics run.
- Save size grows by < 100 bytes. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/save.test.js`, `bun test tests/boat.test.js`.
- `bun run test:smoke` (v6 fixture), `node tests/inpage.mjs boat` (add "dock, save, reload", "call", "chart fallback").

#### Out of scope
- The Glassway's docks and buoys (S-200, S-201a), the Starsong (S-208), boat upgrades (Kinako/Miso stock steps).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a corrupted `boat` field blocks Continue; `normalize` drops it to null instead (rule 4). Rollback: the migration
is additive; reverting leaves v10 saves loadable only if the revert keeps `SAVE_VERSION` 10 — so roll **forward** with a
fix rather than revert once a v10 save may exist (note in the PR).

<a id="s-200"></a>
### S-200 · feat: the Glassway shoals recipe: islets, sandbars and sail lanes (hidden)
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-117, S-198, S-048 · Size: M (about 490 lines) · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.glassway.hidden = true` · Bible: [atlas-far#glassway](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway) (Card, Shape and recipe, The tide, Regions, Arrival, Named anchors), [atlas-far#buoys](../bible/atlas-far.md#buoys), [atlas-far#chart](../bible/atlas-far.md#chart), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes), [systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry)

#### Why
The Glassway is the Wide Blue's shallow heart: glass-clear water over pale sand, nine islets, sandbars that exist only
at low tide, and the harbour for Kettle Isle and Saltglass Flats. This step builds its ground and both tide states so
the props, people and finds have a frozen World.

#### What
1. **Registry** (`js/realms/index.js`; R31 `arrive: 'north'`; R33 kind `sea`; R4 open `flag:stoneSites`):
   ```js
   glassway: { id: 'glassway', name: 'The Glassway', kind: 'sea', size: 640, seed: 40253, recipe: 'shoals',
     palette: 'glassway', music: 'glassway', ambience: 'glassway_sea', hidden: true, open: 'flag:stoneSites',
     arrive: 'north', tod: null, bundle: 'glassway', enemies: 'glassway', sail: 'free', tide: 'tide:glassway',
     benches: 5, rootgate: true, dash: true, views: [ /* below */ ] }
   ```
   Edit systems.md's registry row (`reef` → `north`, open → `flag:stoneSites`) in this PR.
2. **Recipe `js/realms/recipes/shoals.js`** (`class ShoalsWorld extends RealmWorld`), stage order `layout → seabed →
   shelf → islets → beaches → sandbars → walkway → reef → regions → scatter → paths → reach → splat → colliders →
   anchors`; stage streams as home's (7·3, 13·5, 17·3, 31·17).
   - **Seabed** y −6; **shelf** y −2.4 within 25 m of every islet shore and over **the Glass** (disc r 60 at (0, 0)).
   - **Islets** at fixed authored centres (R32: atlas-far decision 11 wins over INDEX's "Poisson discs"; Poisson is
     for reef heads, kelp and driftwood only). Shape: radial-noise blob (radius ±18 %, 9 lobes, on the `layout` stream),
     a 10 m beach ring sloping y 0.4 → −1.6, then the top rising to its height (noise amp 1.2 m, wavelength 30 m):

     | # | id | Centre | Size | Top y | Region |
     |---|---|---|---|---|---|
     | 1 | `post` Post Rock | (0, −190) | r 80 | 6 | `puffinpost` |
     | 2 | `tern` Tern Tuft | (−175, −110) | r 62 | 9 | `sandbars` |
     | 3 | `spit` Whelk's Spit | capsule (−250, −20) → (−215, 120), 40 m wide | - | 4 | `sandbars` |
     | 4 | `kelp` Kelp Knoll | (−120, 150) | r 50 | 5 | `sandbars` |
     | 5 | `gull` Gull Rock | (170, −150) | r 40 skirt; a 15 m-wide stack rising to y 22 (stack sides 75°, not walkable; the spiral path is S-201b's) | 22 | `wreckreach` |
     | 6 | `mabel` Mabel Rock | (225, 20) | r 66 | 7 | `wreckreach` |
     | 7 | `skerry` Spiral Skerry | (120, 150) | r 40 | 6 | `wreckreach` |
     | 8 | `bell` Bell Islet | (0, 200) | r 58 | 5 | `bellshallows` |
     | 9 | `whale` Hummock | (−125, 255), long axis 70°, 60 × 26 m | **water here**: Hummock is a prop with a deck top (S-201b) | (5.5) | `bellshallows` |

   - **Sandbars** (walkable at low tide only): splines with crest y −0.9 and 12-14 m width: B1 (−70, −175) → (−120, −140)
     12 m; B2 (−200, −60) → (−235, −30) 14 m; B3 (−210, 120) → (−165, 140) 12 m; B4 (−120, 200) → (−125, 235) 12 m.
   - **Walkway** to the spire: stone, 4 m wide, top y −0.6, from Bell Islet's south shore (0, 258) to the door (0, 278)
     (exposed only at low tide). The spire itself is S-201b's prop.
   - **Reef heads:** Poisson discs (min spacing 18 m) on shelves only, never within 12 m of a lane polyline; tops y −0.2 to
     1.0 (colliders: the boat bumps them).
   - **Scatter:** kelp clumps on shelves (two poses by tide, S-201a builds the model), driftwood on beaches; dunegrass on
     islet tops.
   - **Paths:** each islet gets a path from its pier to its centre (for the coverage rule's path cells).
3. **The tide** (R6: flag `tide:glassway`, **0 = low, 1 = high, absent = high**; atlas-far's `glassway.tide` with the
   opposite values is replaced in this PR): two sea levels, high y 0.0, low y −1.4. `W.walkHigh` and `W.walkLow` are
   both precomputed; colliders do not change; `W.walk` points at the current one. The water plane animates between levels
   over **4 s** (this realm's number). The Tide Bell (S-122) flips it within **3 m** of a tide stone with `[T] Ring · Tide
   stone` (R56: systems' 3 m and pill win over atlas-far's 4 m and "no pill"); toasts "✿ The tide goes out" / "✿ The tide
   comes in". If Pip is standing on a cell that floods, he is lifted to the nearest dry cell with a 0.4 s fade and the
   toast "✿ Nell fished Pip out. 'Mind the tide!'"; a boat that grounds as the tide drops is nudged to the nearest cell
   with ≥ 0.7 m of water.
4. **Anchors and spawns:** spawn `north` (0, −292) aboard, facing 180°; `post` (0, −268) Post Rock north pier; `buoy-
   kettle`, `buoy-saltglass` beside each buoy, aboard. **Docks** (`W.docks`, the nine piers, ends ≥ 1.0 m deep at low
   tide): Post Rock (0, −268) N · Tern Tuft (−175, −170) N · Whelk's Spit (−270, 40) W · Kelp Knoll (−75, 140) E · Gull
   Rock (210, −150) E · Mabel Rock (200, −45) N · Spiral Skerry (85, 130) W · Bell Islet (0, 140) N · Hummock (−150, 270)
   at his tail. **Buoys:** harbour `glassway:buoy-home` (0, −300), `buoy-kettle` (308, −150), `buoy-saltglass` (−250, 250);
   lane buoys every 40 m along the North, Wreck, Shell and Bell lanes (atlas-far#buoys waypoints); the Bar Cut has no buoys.
   Buoy prompts (from the boat within 6 m): home `[E] Sail · Starseed Isle` (opens the chart, default selection home);
   Kettle and Saltglass `[E] Sail · Kettle Isle` / `Saltglass Flats` **locked** with "Nell won't risk the fog yet." while
   those realms are hidden (portal `locked` rule). **Tide stones** (6) anchors: Post Rock (−30, −250), Tern Tuft (−150, −60),
   Whelk's Spit (−225, 90), Kelp Knoll (−80, 128), Mabel Rock (170, 20), Bell Islet (30, 240). Named anchors `w:pillarPost`
   (0, −178), `w:postCounter` (5, −182), `w:shellStack` (−232, 30), `w:mabel` (235, 45), `w:spire` (0, 285), `w:bellDoor`
   (0, 278), `w:hummock` (−125, 255), `w:glass` (0, 0), `w:buoyHome`, `w:buoyKettle`, `w:buoySaltglass`.
5. **Regions** (each land cell belongs to its islet's region; open water to the nearest islet's):

   | id | Card line 1 / line 2 | Landmark | max | Spawn table |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `puffinpost` | Puffin Post / Stamps required | the Pillar Post (0, −178) 12 m | 0 | none (town) |
   | `sandbars` | The Sandbars / Walkable at low tide | the Shell Stack (−232, 30) 12 m | 5 | `[]` until S-204 |
   | `wreckreach` | Wreck Reach / Three wrecks, no waiting | the Merry Mabel (235, 45) masts 20 m | 5 | `[]` until S-204 |
   | `bellshallows` | Bell Shallows / Listen | the Sunken Bell's spire (0, 285) 18 m | 5 | `[]` until S-204 |

   Music layer per region (`post`, `bars`, `wrecks`, `bell`, S-202); ambience `glassway_post` for `puffinpost`, else
   `glassway_sea`.
6. **Palette `glassway`** (art-audio#palettes row): swatch `#2f7d8c #f5e6c4 #ffb48a #bfeee6`; sky stops `#66b8e8`/`#e6faf6`
   (day), `#fde8c0` (dusk), `#6a80c4`/`#ffc4a8`, `#18264f`/`#36477e` (night); clouds `#ffffff` / `#ffd8c8` / `#7d89c4`;
   water deep `#2f7d8c`, shallow `#bfeee6`, foam `#ffffff` (shallow tint by depth: `#bfeee6` 0-1 m, `#7fd3d0` 2.4 m,
   `#2f7d8c` 6 m); fog per region (art-audio rows mapped by name to atlas ids, as M15's R11): `puffinpost [36, 262,
   #eef8f6, .10]`, `sandbars [36, 262, #f4f0e0, .12]`, `wreckreach [30, 240, #e4eeee, .20]`, `bellshallows [32, 250,
   #e8f6f4, .15]`; terrain layer keys `glassway_dunegrass, glassway_scrub, glassway_seapink, glassway_shellsand,
   glassway_wetsand, glassway_shingle, glassway_barnacle`, cliff `archwater_columns` (all fall back to home layers until
   S-202). Water plane `max(1000, 640 + 600)` = 1,240 m square.
7. **Reach and the boat graph:** BFS on foot per tide from every dock landing; the stack top of Gull Rock and the Hummock
   area are recorded as expected-unreachable until S-201b. The **boat graph** is the water cells with depth ≥ 0.7 m per
   tide: the progression solver proves every dock and the home buoy reachable by boat from `north` at **both** tides.
8. **Views:** densest `{x: −175, z: −150, yaw: 180, pitch: −4}` (Tern Tuft toward the Sandbars), landmark `{x: 0, z: 120,
   yaw: 180, pitch: 8}` (the Bell Lane toward the spire), town `{x: 0, z: −250, yaw: 180, pitch: 0}`, widest `{x: 120,
   z: −40, yaw: 250, pitch: 2}` (across the Glass from Wreck Reach).
9. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/glassway.json` with both tide states' walk counts, reachable m², land m² per
   tide (atlas: ≈ 0.087 km² high, ≈ 0.100 km² low) and the expected coverage land-cell count for the union (atlas: 64-80).

#### Assets
None (terrain falls back to home layers; S-202 paints).

#### Acceptance criteria
- `generateRealm('glassway')` deterministic and equal to the fixture; both tides' walk grids stored; land areas within
  ±10 % of the atlas numbers; every dock landing walkable at both tides; sandbar crests walkable only at low tide.
- The boat graph reaches all nine docks and the home buoy from `north` at both tides (solver).
- Tide flip in-page (`G.debug.flags.set('tide:glassway', 0)`): the water animates 4 s, B1-B4 become walkable; standing on
  B2 and flipping back lifts Pip to dry ground with the toast.
- `W.genMs` ≤ 1.2 s; realm suite 4 views within budget in both cameras; no leak.
- `REALMS.glassway.hidden === true`; the chart's reef band stays fogged. Invariants: all lines except the precache line.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/glassway.test.js` (new: determinism, prints, islets, sandbars per tide, docks, buoys, boat
  graph), `bun test tests/progress-graph.test.js`, `bun test` (home prints unchanged).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (picks up `glassway`), `node tests/inpage.mjs boat` (add "sail the Glassway from north to
  every dock at both tides" with the boat flag on).

#### Out of scope
- Props and landmarks (S-201a, S-201b), art and music (S-202), people and chapter 14 (S-203), enemies (S-204), finds,
  benches, the root-gate and coverage (S-205).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a 640 m mostly-water realm builds terrain chunks of sea floor that cost draw calls; chunks wholly deeper than −4 m
are drawn as one flat sea-floor plane (no mesh per chunk), measured by the realm suite. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-201a"></a>
### S-201a · feat: Glassway props: the stilt post office, the Pillar Post, the Shell Stack, piers, buoys and tide stones
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-200, S-082 · Size: M (about 450 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [atlas-far#glassway-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-kit), [atlas-far#buoys](../bible/atlas-far.md#buoys), [atlas-far#glassway-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-arrival), [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates)

Split from INDEX S-201 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): part a is the harbour kit and Post Rock; part b
([S-201b](#s-201b)) is the three wrecks, Gull Rock's path, the spire and Hummock.

#### Why
Everything the player sails toward needs a shape that reads from 200 m across glass-clear water: a coral-and-cream post
office on stilts with a post-box lookout tower, a leaning tower of shells, striped buoys with lamps, piers to moor at.

#### What
1. **`js/props-glassway.js`** (code-built toon models through `js/toon.js`, `GB` builder style; sizes, palettes and tris
   from atlas-far#glassway-kit):

   | Key | Kind | Build | Tris | Placement |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `glassway_post_office` | single | a two-storey timber house 14 × 10 m on 12 cylinder stilts 3 m tall over Post Rock's bight, a ramp from the beach, a round window, pigeonhole shutters; coral `#ff8f7a`, cream `#f5e6c4`, timber `#8a6a4a`, roof `#2f7d8c`; door at (0, −186) facing north | 1,200 | centred (2, −184), facing 0° |
   | `glassway_pillar_post` | single (part of the office mesh group) | the 12 m lookout tower painted like a post box: coral cylinder, cream band, a slot, a flag (flutters), a lamp on top (`nightGlow`) | 400 | (0, −178) |
   | `glassway_shell_stack` | single | a leaning tower of giant shells: a conch at the base, scallops, a spiral whelk on top, 12 m, lean 6°; cream `#f5e6c4`, pink `#ff9f8f`, sand `#e2c9a0`, pearl rim `#f1e6dc` | 900 | (−232, 30), lean toward 200° |
   | `glassway_pier` | instanced (9) | a 4 × 14 m wooden deck on posts with a rope rail and a mooring post; `#8a6a4a`, rope `#e2c9a0`; a `deck` record (walkable) and the dock landing at its end | 200 | the nine docks of S-200, deck top y 0.9 |
   | `glassway_buoy_harbour` | instanced (3) | 3.2 m coral and cream striped buoy with a lit lamp cage and a spiral on the cage; bobs ±0.2 m over 3 s | 220 | the three harbour buoys |
   | `glassway_buoy_lane` | instanced | 1.4 m white buoy with one coral band; `nightGlow` tip; bobs | 80 | every lane buoy (S-200 anchors) |
   | `glassway_tide_stone` | instanced (6) | a 1.6 m round standing stone with a carved wave spiral `#7fd3d0` and a bronze ring `#b8894a`; the carving glows 1 s when rung | 160 | the six tide stones; gate type `tide_stone` (S-122), `[T] Ring · Tide stone` within 3 m |
   | `glassway_kelp` | instanced | a clump of kelp ribbons, two poses (flat at low tide, standing at high), `#4f6b4a` / `#7fae4b`, double-sided | 60 | S-200 scatter; pose follows `tide:glassway` |
   | `glassway_reef_head` | instanced | round coral-pink rock heads 1-4 m `#e2c9a0` / `#ff9f8f` (colliders) | 80 | S-200 reef heads |
   | `glassway_tern_nest` | instanced | a grass-ringed nest with three speckled eggs, 0.6 m | 60 | 6 on Tern Tuft's dune top (recipe scatter stream) |
   | `glassway_driftwood` | instanced | a bleached log 3 m `#d8cfc0` | 60 | beaches (S-200 scatter) |
   | `glassway_shell_big` | instanced | a giant scallop half-buried in sand, 1.5 m | 120 | 1 per 600 m² of beach (scatter stream) |

   New material programs: wet sand, foam edge, barnacle, tin, bell bronze (5 of 12; tin and bronze are first used here
   by the ring and in S-201b). Instanced types used by the Glassway after this step: 9 own + bench, root-gate knot,
   Hollow door, vista post, lectern, soft earth, hookbloom, star door, thornglass, bottle (S-205 adds them) ≤ 24.
2. **Colliders and decks** go in the recipe's `colliders` stage (part of prints, refreshed): the office stilts (12 small
   cylinders) and the ramp deck; the Pillar Post (r 1.6); the Shell Stack (r 3); each pier's posts (the boat bumps them)
   and its deck; buoys (r 0.9, boat only); reef heads; tide stones (r 0.6).
3. **Buoy lights:** harbour buoys' lamps glow at night (`glassway_card_buoy_light` from S-202 as an additive sprite, canvas
   fallback); the lamps of the Kettle and Saltglass buoys stay dark while those realms are hidden.
4. **Landmark fog cap** 0.45 for the Pillar Post and the Shell Stack (visible from each region entry point listed in
   S-200's region table; in-page ray test from 1.6 m above the deck of the boat at each entry).
5. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/glassway.json`.

#### Assets
All code-built, `realm: 'glassway'`, GLB slots commented out. ART BRIEFs in the PR copied from atlas-far#glassway-kit:
`glassway_post_office` (with the Pillar Post; image prompt "a cosy timber post office on stilts over clear shallow sea,
a tall coral-and-cream lookout tower shaped like a post box with a slot and a flag, puffins on the railings"; 3D "toon
stilt post office 14 × 10 m with 12 m post-box lookout tower"), `glassway_shell_stack`, `glassway_buoy_harbour`,
`glassway_buoy_lane`, `glassway_pier`, `glassway_tide_stone`, `glassway_kelp`, and one sheet brief for the reef heads,
tern nest, driftwood and big shell ("beach props sheet: coral rock heads, a tern nest with speckled eggs, bleached
driftwood, a giant scallop shell, isolated on plain background").

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every key builds under `?noassets=all`; the town view (Post Rock) and the densest view stay within budget in both
  cameras (record numbers).
- The boat moors at each pier's end and Pip walks up each pier onto its islet at both tides.
- The tide stones ring with the Tide Bell within 3 m and flip the realm tide; kelp changes pose with the tide.
- Landmarks visible from their entry points through fog. Prints refreshed; home prints byte-identical.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/glassway.test.js` (colliders, decks, pier ends ≥ 1.0 m deep at low tide).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`, `node tests/inpage.mjs boat` ("moor at each pier", "tide stone flips tide").

#### Out of scope
- Wrecks, Gull Rock's path, the spire, Hummock (S-201b); terrain art (S-202).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: 9 piers + 40-odd lane buoys + reef heads add draw calls in the widest view; all are instanced types. Rollback:
revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-201b"></a>
### S-201b · feat: Glassway landmarks: three wrecks, Gull Rock's spiral path, the Sunken Bell's spire and the Snoozing Whale
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-201a · Size: M (about 470 lines) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [atlas-far#glassway-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-kit), [atlas-far#glassway-shape](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-shape), [atlas-far#glassway-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-states), [cast#hummock](../bible/cast.md#hummock)

#### Why
The Glassway's best views are its stories in waiting: a cargo ship on her side with a walrus figurehead, a tin barge
you can stand on at low tide, a sloop stuck halfway up a rock, a chapel spire rising from the sea and a whale asleep
with a meadow on her back. Each is a later chapter's door (the Bell, the Mabel, ss-37), built now so the land is whole.

#### What
1. **Models** (`js/props-glassway.js`, code-built, palettes and sizes from atlas-far#glassway-kit):

   | Key | Build | Tris | Placement and records |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `glassway_merry_mabel` | a plump three-masted cargo ship, 34 m, on her starboard side (tilted 70°) on Mabel Rock's south shore, masts reaching north-west over the islet (main mast tip 20 m up), torn furled sails `#f3e3c3`, hull `#8a5a3c` with a coral stripe `#ff8f7a`, barnacles `#c9c2b0` below the waterline, a walrus figurehead in a bonnet, a hull breach on the south side | 2,400 | centred (235, 45); the hull is a solid (box colliders along it); the breach `mabel_breach` (215, 55) above low water is **sealed** with boards: `[E] Look · Boarded breach` "Boarded up from inside. Something in there is snoring." (o3 opens it in S-214); rigging sways |
   | `glassway_biscuit_tin` | a squat 16 × 6 m barge with a corrugated tin cabin `#c9c2b0`, hull `#3b3540`, rust `#c2563f`, sunk upright | 500 | (160, 60); a deck record at y −1.0 (dry and walkable only at low tide; under 1.0 m of water at high tide, the boat passes over); stacked biscuit tins on deck |
   | `glassway_wobbly_gull` | a little 9 m teal sloop `#3fa79a` wedged on Gull Rock's east ledge, patched sail, round cabin door | 400 | ledge at (185, −135) y 8; its cabin interior is a 2 × 3 m deck room (chest c06 in S-205) |
   | Gull Rock's **spiral path** | a chain of ledge decks (`deck` records via `attachPost`-style recipe decks, part of prints since the realm is hidden) 2.5 m wide winding twice round the 15 m stack from the skirt at (190, −160) y 2 to the top y 22, rising ≤ 0.35 per m, with a rope rail; the *Wobbly Gull* ledge branches off at y 8; a niche at y ≈ 10 near (160, −160) for Lamp Log 23 (S-205) | 600 | the top (170, −150) becomes reachable on foot (S-200's expected-unreachable list shrinks) |
   | `glassway_spire` | a slim stone spire 18 m above low water (top y 16.6) at (0, 285): a carved spiral `#ffcf7a` (glows after `stone:bell`), an arched belfry window near the top with a bronze bell `#b8894a`, seaweed `#4f6b4a` and barnacles up to the high-water line, a door at its foot (sill y −0.5) | 900 | the door `bell_door` (0, 278) is **sealed** until S-209: `[E] Look · Chapel door` "A stone door under the spire, carved with a spiral. Locked. The keyhole is shaped like a bell."; at high tide the door is under water and shows nothing |
   | `glassway_hummock` | a huge sleeping whale whose back is an islet, 60 × 26 m, long axis 70°, back y 5.5: a squashed ellipsoid body `#5d7d8c` with a pale belly line `#e9e1d0`, a grassy deck top `#9bb87a` (a walkable deck record, 40 × 16 m, with a picnic bench and a palm), two barnacle patches `#c9c2b0`, a blowhole, a little smile; **upright state only** (the rolled state is S-216) | 2,000 | centred (−125, 255); breathing rise ±0.15 m over 12 s (the deck moves with it, Pip rides it); dock at the tail (−150, 270) |
   | `prop_hummock_eye` | the 2 m lidded half-sphere eye at the waterline, a blink every 12 s (cast.md) | 300 | on the head end facing the Bell Lane |
   | `fx_spout` | the blowhole spout: an additive mist sprite every **12 s** (R18: atlas-far's 60 × 26 m and 12 s win; edit cast.md's "40 m" and "40 s" in this PR) | sprite | `glassway_card_whale_spout` from S-202 when loaded, canvas mist fallback; ambience `ambWhale` (a 12 s breath cycle) within 30 m |

2. **Hummock's talk point** `glassway.hummock` at her head (−125, 255): `[E] Talk · Hummock` (lines are S-216's; until then
   one line, cast.md's tic: "(from below) Mmmrrr... itchy... mm."). Her pronoun is **she** (cast.md).
3. **Landmark fog cap** for the *Merry Mabel* and the spire; both visible from their region entry points (S-200 table).
4. **States read at runtime** (atlas-far#glassway-states, no prints change): `stone:bell` → the spire's spiral glows, the
   bell rings once at 19:00 each dusk (`sfx.bigBell` "ting" across the realm), a lamp burns in the belfry at night (the
   rules ship here; the flag is set in S-212). ss-36 done → the Mabel's mast bell gone (S-216). ss-37 → rolled state
   (S-216).
5. Refresh prints (both tides); the reach test now includes Gull Rock's top and Hummock's deck.

#### Assets
All code-built, `realm: 'glassway'`, slots commented out; ART BRIEFs in the PR copied from atlas-far#glassway-kit for
`glassway_merry_mabel`, `glassway_biscuit_tin`, `glassway_wobbly_gull`, `glassway_spire`, `glassway_hummock` (both poses
described), plus cast.md's `prop_hummock_eye` brief.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Gull Rock's top (170, −150) y 22 is reachable on foot by the spiral path; the *Wobbly Gull* ledge reachable.
- The *Biscuit Tin* deck is walkable at low tide only; the spire door and the Mabel's breach show their sealed Look
  lines; Hummock's deck is walkable and moves with her breathing without jitter (Pip stays grounded).
- Wreck Reach and Bell Shallows views within budget in both cameras (record numbers).
- Prints refreshed; home prints byte-identical. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/glassway.test.js` (spiral path slope ≤ 0.35, reach per tide, deck records).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`, `node tests/inpage.mjs boat` ("walk Gull Rock to the top", "ride Hummock's breathing").

#### Out of scope
- The Sunken Bell dungeon (S-209), the Merry Mabel dungeon (S-214), Hummock's story and rolled state (S-216).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a moving deck (Hummock) desyncs Pip's grounding; the deck moves through the shipped platform carry (or Pip is
carried by the deck's y each step) and the in-page test checks 60 s of standing. Rollback: revert; hidden realm.

<a id="s-202"></a>
### S-202 · art: Glassway look: terrain layers, water clarity, loading card, map art and music
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-200, S-029b · Size: M (about 280 lines of code and data, plus generated images) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible: [art-audio#glassway](../bible/art-audio.md#glassway), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes), [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data), [art-audio#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience), [art-audio#sfx](../bible/art-audio.md#sfx), [art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles)

#### Why
The Glassway's whole identity is its water: shallows that read as glass over sand. Painted terrain, a clarity setting
on the water, its cards, loading card, map and the glockenspiel track turn the recipe into a place, all in a lazy bundle.

#### What
1. **Terrain layers** (kind `tile`, 1024² seamless, `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <raw> <out> --blend 64`, `_hd`
   1024 and `_lo` 512 webp q82 under `assets/realms/glassway/terrain/`, ≤ 110 / 40 KB), subjects verbatim from
   art-audio#glassway: `glassway_dunegrass` (grass) "Tufts of pale marram grass on sand. Palette: #a8c88a #d8e8b0 #f5e6c4
   #2f7d8c." · `glassway_scrub` (forest) "Low silver-green coastal scrub with orange sea-buckthorn berries. Palette:
   #8aa88a #b8c8a8 #ffb48a #6a7a6a." · `glassway_seapink` (meadow) "Sea-pink and white sea-campion flowers on short turf.
   Palette: #f6a6c0 #ffffff #8aa88a #f5e6c4." · `glassway_shellsand` (sand) "Bright cream sand dotted with tiny pink and
   white shells. Palette: #f5e6c4 #fff4e0 #ffb48a #e2d2b0." · `glassway_wetsand` (snow slot) "Glistening wet sandbar sand
   with ripple marks and thin water sheen. Palette: #d8d0b8 #bfeee6 #e8e0c8 #a8c8c0." · `glassway_shingle` (dirt) "Smooth
   white and grey shingle pebbles. Palette: #e8e8e0 #b8b8b0 #d8d0c0 #8a9a98." · `glassway_barnacle` (rock) "Rounded sea
   rock with barnacles, limpets and green weed. Palette: #8a9a98 #a8b0a8 #6a8a6a #f5e6c4." Cliff: `archwater_columns`
   carried as a copy in this bundle (same file, second key). Splat rules: beaches → shellsand; sandbars and the tide band
   → wetsand; islet tops → dunegrass with seapink patches and scrub on north slopes; shingle on piers' landings; rock on
   slopes > 0.6.
2. **Cards** (alpha webp ≤ 150 KB): `glassway_card_ground` (1024 × 512 sheet of 4: marram, campion, starfish, seaweed;
   `tools/slice_sheet.py`), `glassway_card_bottle_glint` (128² 4-frame strip, `tools/process_strip.py`),
   `glassway_card_buoy_light` (128²), `glassway_card_bow_wave` (512 × 256 6-frame strip), `glassway_card_whale_spout`
   (256 × 512 4-frame strip); prompts verbatim from art-audio#glassway.
3. **Water clarity** (art-audio decision 27): a new `clarity` uniform in the water material (default 0 = today's water,
   home unchanged), `palette.water.clarity = 0.85` for the Glassway: the floor shows through to 3 m depth with a depth
   tint from shallow `#bfeee6` to deep `#2f7d8c`. Implement as a depth-fade on the existing water shader's alpha/colour
   (no new program; a uniform branch); `wp8` water checks unchanged on home.
4. **Loading** `load_glassway` (1600 × 900 ≤ 200 KB + lo 960 × 540): "A sea of glass-clear turquoise shallows over pale
   sand, sandbars and tiny islets with a few palms, three old shipwrecks resting in the shallows, a little post office on
   stilts with a red roof, and a stone bell spire rising from the water. Palette: #2f7d8c #f5e6c4 #ffb48a #bfeee6."
5. **Map** `map_glassway` (1024² ≤ 200 KB): "A wide shallow sea seen from above with nine little islets, sandbars, three
   wrecks, a stilt post office, a bell spire, and channels marked by buoys leading east-north-east and south-south-west.
   Palette: #2f7d8c #f5e6c4 #ffb48a #bfeee6." Registered as the This-land background aligned to the realm grid (canvas
   fallback draws the islets, bars at low tide dotted, buoys and docks); it is also the sub-chart background of S-199's
   fallback.
6. **Music** (`js/realms/music/glassway.js`, `G.audio.addTrack`): track `glassway` **exactly** as art-audio#track-data
   (E, 108 bpm, swing 0.18; chords `E C#m A B E G#m A B C#m A E B A B7 E E`; lead `pluck`, `lead2: 'glock'` at layer 1;
   bass, comp, perc as written; R40: art-audio's note data wins over atlas-far's "G, 96 bpm", edit atlas-far in this
   PR). Region layers: `post` (layer 1 + a squeezebox-like pad from the `pad` voice), `bars` (glock and wind only),
   `wrecks` (adds a low marimba voice and slow creaks), `bell` (perc replaced by three soft bell tones G3, C4, E4: the
   duel's pitches). The shipped `sailing` track plays while the boat moves (S-198 wiring). Ambience beds `glassway_sea`
   and `glassway_post` exactly as art-audio#ambience; one-shots `ambGull`, `ambBuoyBell`, `ambWhale`, `ambPage`.
7. **SFX** (art-audio#sfx): `bottleBob` (emitter within 15 m of a washed-up bottle: `bubble` at 0.4 every 1.8 s + a
   tink; visual twin the glint strip; used by S-203/S-205), `bowWave` (already in S-198, now with the real strip).
8. **Bundle:** every key `realm: 'glassway'` under `assets/realms/glassway/`; record the bundle size (budget 2.5 MB;
   art-audio's estimate ≈ 2,440 KB: if over, drop the lowest-value card first, then webp quality 76, never terrain `lo`).
9. Log every prompt and command in `docs/art/prompts.md`.

#### Assets
Seven terrain layers (+ the cliff copy), five cards, `load_glassway` (+ lo), `map_glassway`. Fallbacks: terrain → the
home layer in the same slot; cards → canvas sprites (white curl for the bow wave, soft circle for the buoy light,
four-point star for the glint, mist puff for the spout); loading → `F-home:loading_bg`; map → the canvas render.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every key loads from the `glassway` bundle with a working fallback (`?noassets=all` renders); `tests/assets.test.js`
  size limits pass; bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; core precache unchanged.
- Clarity: in-page, the sand floor at 2 m depth is visible on the Glass (pixel test: floor colour contributes ≥ 30 % at
  2 m, ≤ 5 % at 6 m); home's water renders as before (`wp8`).
- Music: entering a region switches the layer; Bell Shallows plays the three bell tones; sailing above 3 m/s for 2 s
  crossfades to `sailing`.
- Images original and in the house style (prompt log reviewed). Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (assets, pwa partition), `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`, `node tests/inpage.mjs wp8` (home water unchanged), `node tests/inpage.mjs glassway`
  (new suite, CI: "clarity floor visible", "music layers by region").
- `bun run test:smoke` with `?noassets=all&realm=glassway&debug=1`.

#### Out of scope
- Vista sketches (S-205), portraits (S-203), bestiary cards (S-204), the Bell and Mabel dungeon textures (S-209, S-214).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: clarity makes the shallows look washed out in FP at low sun; clarity is a palette number, tune within 0.7-0.9.
Rollback: revert; every key falls back.

<a id="s-203"></a>
### S-203 · feat: chapter 14 Past the Reef: Puffin Post, Postmistress Pell and Mr Whelk
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-199, S-201b, S-202, S-191b, S-030 · Size: M (about 470 lines incl. fixtures) · Save: none (v10 from S-199) · Flag: none (this step unhides the Glassway; `REALMS.glassway.sail` stays `'free'` unless the fallback is chosen) · Bible: [story#ch14](../bible/story.md#ch14), [cast#postmistress-pell](../bible/cast.md#postmistress-pell), [cast#mr-whelk](../bible/cast.md#mr-whelk), [cast#kinako](../bible/cast.md#kinako), [atlas-far#glassway-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-people), [atlas-far#sailing-geography](../bible/atlas-far.md#sailing-geography), [lore#bottle-4](../bible/lore.md#bottle-4), [atlas-home#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky)

#### Why
Wenna said Nell would have to sail past the reef, and that she's brave. Chapter 14 is that crossing: the first time the
player steers the *Dandelion* across glass-clear water to a post office on stilts, finds a hundred-year-old bottle on
the beach and learns where the far buoys lead. It is the doorway to the Stair Stone chapters.

#### What
1. **Quest `mq-ch14`** (`js/content/quests/glassway.js`; n 14, main, owner `nell`, title "Past the Reef", `start:
   'flag:stoneSites'` (R4); story#ch14 table, journal text and hints verbatim except step 1's answer, R57):

   | # | id | done (on) | Journal | Nudge / Answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | 0 | `nell` | `seen:nell.ch14.0` (TALK_END) | Ask Nell to sail past the reef. | Who's brave? Nell's brave. / Talk to Nell at the jetty. |
   | 1 | `sail` | `realm:glassway.visited` (REALM) | Sail into the Glassway. | The water past the reef is glass-clear. / Talk to Nell at the jetty and pick "Beyond the Reef" on the chart. |
   | 2 | `post` | `met:pell` (TALK_END) | Find the post office on stilts. | Puffins like tidy places. / Puffin Post stands on stilts in the north of the Glassway; moor at its slipway. |
   | 3 | `bottle` | `collect:bottle:glassway:4` (COLLECT) | Pick up the bottle on Puffin Post's beach. | Something's bobbing by the slipway. / The bottle glints on the sand by the slipway. Press {interact}. |
   | 4 | `bazaar` | `met:kinako` or `seen:kinako.glassway.1` (TALK_END) | Visit the floating bazaar. | You'll hear it before you see it. / Kinako's Floating Bazaar is moored beside Puffin Post this week. |
   | 5 | `buoys` | `seen:pell.ch14.5` (TALK_END) | Ask Pell about the far buoys. | Pell knows every route. / Talk to Postmistress Pell again. |

   `set: ['set:ch14']`. Edit story.md's step 1 answer ("or sail south-west from the jetty" cannot work: home's sea is not
   sailable) and the button name "Beyond the Reef" (atlas-far#chart) in this PR. Short labels: "Nell · the jetty",
   "Beyond the Reef", "Puffin Post", "The bottle", "The bazaar", "Pell".
2. **Lines** (`js/text/en/glassway.js`; dialogue `js/content/dialogue/glassway.js`):
   - Nell, step 0 (home jetty, priority 100): "Past the reef? Haven't sailed it since the storm. ...Did she say I was
     brave? Well. I knew that. Hold the Lantern high."
   - Pell, step 2: "Puffin Post! Letters in, letters out, no parcels over a puffin's weight. You'll be the hooded one.
     The Tree's been sending us her regards through the roots, very faintly. Took us ages to read."
   - Pell, when Pip carries the bottle to her (step 3 done, priority 90, once): "A bottle! Proper old glass. A mole's hand,
     big careful letters. Stamped! I'll keep a copy. Letters like this deserve delivering." (Return to Sender itself is
     S-215: it starts from this flag.)
   - Kinako, step 4: "A CUSTOMER!! Welcome to the Floating Bazaar!! Everything is limited edition!! Even the limited
     editions!!" (`!!` is Kinako's alone).
   - Pell, step 5: "Buoys, dear. Red to Kettle Isle, white to Saltglass. And the bell's out there in the shallows, if
     you've a mind to listen."
   - Arrival scene `ch14-reef` (8 s, the one scene allowed while sailing, whitelisted in S-198; replayable): the sailing
     card `card_sail_reef` (4-6 s) then the Glassway at `north` aboard; Nell at the tiller: "There. Past the reef. Look
     down, Pip: you can see the sand. ...I said I'd get you here. Eventually." Region card "Puffin Post / Stamps required".
     In the chart fallback the card lands Pip on the Post Rock dock instead.
3. **The chart:** the World tab's Glassway node ("Beyond the Reef" button at (512, 852) on atlas-far's layout; systems'
   (800, 900) node on its own chart image, whichever S-080 shipped) is listed from `flag:stoneSites` and selectable from
   `seen:nell.ch14.0`; its fog-bank line before that is "Past the reef? Not without a good light, Pip." From `ch14` the
   chart draws Kettle Isle and Saltglass Flats as fog banks with their gold marks ("Nell won't risk the fog yet." while
   their realms are hidden). On home's sky, horizon cards e (Kettle, 65°) and f (Saltglass, 200°) drop their fog haze
   (0.95 → the normal card haze) within 10 s of `ch14` (atlas-home#sky).
4. **People** (spots per atlas-far#glassway-people, R27; schedules per cast.md):

   | NPC | Look | Spot | Schedule |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `pell` Postmistress Pell | `bird` rig `beak: 'parrot'` (S-068 family), 0.8 m: back `#22242a`, face and belly `#f4f1ea`, beak `#f28a3a` with a blue-grey band `#7a8aa0` and yellow edge `#f2c94c`, feet `#f29b38`, postal cap and satchel `#2f7d8c` with a brass badge `#c99a4a`, a big rubber stamp `#c2563f` | `glassway.pell` (5, −182) | `[[6,'glassway.pell','work'],[20,'glassway.pell','indoors']]` (indoors → her door spot until the interior ships in S-215) |
   | `puffin_clerk_1`, `_2` (unnamed, label "Puffin clerk") | `bird` parrot recolours 0.7 m, grey caps | (−8, −186), (14, −190) | `[[7,…,'work'],[19,…,'indoors']]` |
   | `whelk` Mr Whelk | one-off code model, 0.6 m: a `lathe` blue-and-white porcelain teapot body `#f5f7fb` with cobalt flowers `#3a6fb0` (`colorFn` dots), `cyl` spout at the back, `sphere` lid worn as a hat; crab parts from the shipped `crab` enemy model scaled 0.6 (coral `#f28a6a`, claws `#e0603a`) with friendly `face()` eyes; a brass jeweller's loupe `#c99a4a` | `glassway.whelk` (−225, 40) | `[[8,'glassway.whelk','work'],[18,'glassway.whelk','indoors']]` (indoors = inside the teapot: the crab parts hide, a snore puff every 6 s) |
   | `kinako` (the bazaar) | Kinako and his bazaar boat as shipped by S-168 (grep `bazaar`) | `glassway.kinako` (34, −196) at Post Rock's east dock | cast#kinako rule: on the first day a land is unlocked the bazaar docks there; it stays while `mq-ch14` needs him (step 4), then rotates in chart order |

   Voice markers reserved in the content lint: Pell "Stamped!", Whelk "Rare. Very rare." Idle lines (priority 10, by day
   index): Pell "Stamped! ...Sorry. I stamp when I'm happy, and I'm always happy at a counter." · Pell "Letters for the
   Tree go in the round slot. We don't know how they get to her either. They just do." · clerk "Sorting. Always sorting."
   · clerk "That one's for a lighthouse. That one's for a cloud. Don't ask me." · Whelk "A visitor! Rare. Very rare. Mind
   the shelves; the shells are older than my teapot." · Whelk "This pebble? Rare. Very rare. ...No, it's a pebble. But
   it's a very good one." Sound cues: Pell's stamp thunk every 15 s with an ink-dust puff; Whelk's teapot lid rattles
   when he talks. The post office door and Whelk's shop show `[E] Knock`: "Counter's out front, sender! The back's all
   sorting." / "Shop's open when the teapot's warm. Come by in the day."
5. **Bottle 4** (R10: atlas-far's `bottle-1` moves from Tern Tuft to Post Rock's north beach): a new Glassway post-pass
   `placeFinds` (`new RNG(40253 * 71 + 3)`) whose **first** entry is `glassway:bottle-4` at (14, −258) on the tideline
   (S-205 appends every other find after it, so this one never moves). Bob and glint (`bottleBob` emitter + glint strip);
   `[E] Pick up · Bottle`; the letter card shows Loam's letter in his hand (lore#bottle-4 text verbatim, ink `#3a3a3a`,
   1.3 × size, ±3° per word; paper `#eee4c8`); `collect.bottle['glassway:4']`; the lighthouse shelf shows it (S-075).
6. **Unhide:** `REALMS.glassway.hidden = false`; prints frozen (fixture header). Completion shows the Glassway group from
   `realm:glassway.visited`. Free sailing on (`sail: 'free'`, owner decision 4 default). If the fallback is chosen
   (S-198/S-199 prove free sailing impossible), set `sail: 'chart'` here and record it in the vision Log.
7. **Chapter test `ch14`**: `tests/fixtures/saves/ch14-start.json` (= ch13 end) and `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch14.json`
   (talk Nell; chart → "Beyond the Reef"; arrival scene skip; boat to the Post Rock dock with `G.debug.boat.place` +
   Dock; talk Pell; pick up bottle 4; talk Kinako; talk Pell; assert `ch14`, `collect.bottle['glassway:4']`,
   `S.boat.realm === 'glassway'`, `REALMS.glassway.hidden === false`); a second run with `sail: 'chart'` forced by URL
   `?sail=chart` proves the fallback path. CI entries `chapter:ch14`, `chapter:ch14-chart`; the chain links ch13 → ch14.

#### Assets
- `portrait_pell`, `portrait_whelk` (realm `glassway`, cast.md prompts verbatim, processed as S-178): Pell "A neat,
  brisk little puffin postmistress with a black back and white face, a striped orange, yellow and blue-grey beak, a teal
  postal cap with a brass badge and a teal satchel, holding up a big rubber stamp." · Whelk "A small coral-red hermit
  crab gentleman living in a blue-and-white porcelain teapot with the lid worn like a hat, a brass jeweller's loupe in
  one eye, holding up a tiny spiral shell with great reverence."
- `card_sail_reef` (sailing card, 1600 × 900 ≤ 200 KB + lo, core like `load_sailing`): kind `loading` + "A little sailing
  boat with a dandelion-yellow sail and a pelican at the tiller crossing a ring of white foam over a reef into glass-clear
  turquoise water, a small hooded child at the bow pointing ahead. Palette: #2f7d8c #bfeee6 #f6d36b #f5e6c4."; fallback
  `load_sailing`.
- ART BRIEFs `npc_pell` (≤ 4,000 tris) and `npc_whelk` (≤ 3,000 tris) from cast.md in the PR; slots commented out.

- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch14`, `node tests/chapter.mjs ch14-chart`, then the earlier chapters in order.
- `bun test` (quests, content, save, realm prints), `node tests/inpage.mjs glassway` ("pell schedule", "whelk teapot
  indoors", "bottle 4 bob and pickup", "horizon e/f clear at ch14"), `node tests/inpage.mjs boat`.

<a id="s-203b"></a>

### S-203b · feat: Fog on the Water: sail buoy to buoy by bell sound to reach Puffin Post

Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-203, S-201a, S-091b · Size: M (about 400 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces), [../bible/story.md#ch14](../bible/story.md#ch14),
[../bible/atlas-far.md#buoys](../bible/atlas-far.md#buoys), [../bible/systems.md#boat](../bible/systems.md#boat)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed **after** S-203 (the Glassway is open), so it adds a
`before` sub-objective (S-091b's field) to ch14's step `post` and never shifts a saved step index.

#### Why

Part of **The Glassway I: sailing and Puffin Post**. The Glassway's main-path set piece (15-25 minutes, no fail): the
first time Pip sails past the reef, a sea fog sits over the northern shallows, and the Glassway's puffins ring bell
buoys to bring boats in. Pip steers by ear from bell to bell through the hidden rocks, scooping up two lost mailbags
on the way, until the fog thins on Puffin Post. It is the first real use of free sailing.

#### What

1. **Data** (`mq-ch14`, step `post`): `before: [{ id: 'fog', until: 'flag:gw:fogCrossed', minutes: 18, text:
   'qt.ch14.fog', short: 'qt.ch14.fog.short', target: 'w:glassway.bell-next', hint: ['ht.ch14.fog.n',
   'ht.ch14.fog.a'] }]`. Journal "Follow the bell buoys through the fog ({n}/6)." · compass "Follow the bells" ·
   nudge "In fog, puffins ring bells. Only one rings at a time." · answer "Sail toward the bell you can hear; when you
   reach it, the next one starts ringing. Pick up the floating mailbags on the way."
2. **The fog** (from the first arrival until `gw:fogCrossed`): over the Glassway north of z = −120, the realm fog
   tightens to near 8 m / far 40 m (palette colour `#e6eef0`); the boat's top speed there is capped at 4 m/s. South of
   z = −120 the realm is clear, so nothing else of the Glassway is blocked. Nell's line on arrival (from the jetty
   card): "Fog on the shallows. Listen for the puffins' bells, and don't steer for anything you can't hear."
3. **Six bell buoys** (Glassway post-pass `placeSetPiece` on `new RNG(40253 * 109 + 23)`, the atlas-far#far-passes row
   for set pieces): `glassway:bell-1..6` at the nearest water cell ≥ 1.8 m deep within 8 m of
   (−60, −300), (−120, −262), (−104, −214), (−58, −176), (−8, −152), (40, −168); the finish is Post Rock's slipway
   dock (the dock record nearest (10, −165)). Model: the shipped lane buoy (S-201a) with a 0.4 m brass bell under its
   cap. Only the next bell rings: two strokes every 4 s (`sfx.buoyBell`, G4, positional, audible to 120 m), each stroke
   with a ring-pulse halo sprite above the buoy that reads through the fog (charter first-person rule 5). Within 8 m of
   the ringing buoy, the puffin perched on it flaps once and the next bell starts. Sets `gw:bell<k>` and `gw:bells`.
4. **The Teeth** (same pass): 14 fog-hidden rocks (`glassway_tooth`, reuse S-201b's wreck-rock mesh scaled 0.5, boat
   collider only) on both sides of the bell channel, 10-16 m from the straight line between consecutive bells,
   present only while `!flag:gw:fogCrossed` (they sink 2 m after, so they never block later sailing). Bumping one:
   the boat stops with a wooden thunk and a 1 m push back, no damage; a puffin squawks from the nearest bell: "Wrong
   way, love! Listen for the bell!" (once per 20 s). **No fail.**
5. **Two mailbags** (between bells 2-3 and 4-5): a floating canvas bag (`prop_mailbag`, 0.5 m, bobbing) 6 m off the
   channel line; from the boat within 3 m `[E] Pick up · Mailbag` (`give:pell_mailbag*1`, stack 2). At Puffin Post,
   meeting Pell (`met:pell`) with bags adds her line "You found our post! In THAT fog! Stamp of approval, both bags."
   and `gems:+20` per bag (`take:pell_mailbag*n`). Bags missed stay floating (nothing missable) until picked up.
6. **Arrival** (within 6 m of the slipway dock after bell 6): the fog lifts over 4 s (fog lerps to the realm
   palette), the Glassway's music `glassway` comes in, toast "✿ Puffin Post", `set:gw:fogCrossed`. Dock as usual
   (S-199).
7. **Chart-stop fallback** (`REALMS.glassway.sail === 'chart'`, S-199's switch): the set piece plays as six sub-chart
   hops, bell to bell, each a 3 s card with the bell sound and its caption "Nell steers toward the bell ({k}/6)";
   the mailbags are picked up automatically on hops 3 and 5. The flag and payoffs are the same.
8. **Saves**: on load with `quest:mq-ch14>=3`, set `gw:fogCrossed` (no fog for a player already past it).
9. **Chapter test** `ch14` (S-203's spec): after `sail glassway`, `G.debug.gw.followBells()` (steers the boat bell
   to bell with the boat bot); expect `gw:fogCrossed`, `gw:bells = 6`; and the `ch14-chart` variant with the fallback.
   **Debug:** `G.debug.gw.followBells()`, `G.debug.gw.fog(on)`.

#### Assets

- `prop_mailbag` code-built (≤ 120 tris; canvas `#d9c7a0`, strap `#7a5a3e`, a stamped puffin decal drawn in canvas).
  **ART BRIEF**: a soft canvas post sack tied with a strap, 0.5 m, a little puffin stamp; image prompt "[style
  preamble] a small canvas mail sack with a leather strap and a round puffin postage stamp, floating on clear
  turquoise water, soft painterly storybook style, transparent background, 1024 × 1024"; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a
  small canvas mail sack with a strap, 0.5 m, hand-painted toon storybook prop, under 400 triangles".
- Bell: a `lathe` bell (≤ 80 tris, `#c99a4a`) added under the shipped lane buoy's cap. `glassway_tooth` reuses a mesh.

#### Acceptance criteria

- On a ch14 save at step `post` without the flag, the northern Glassway is fogged, only the next bell rings, and six
  bells lead to Puffin Post; the Teeth bump but never damage; the fog lifts on arrival and never returns.
- Both mailbags can be picked up and pay 20 gems each on delivery; a skipped bag stays.
- The fallback (`sail: 'chart'`) completes the same flags.
- Fog view budgets: ≤ 200 main-pass calls, ≤ 1.3 M tris with the Teeth and bells.
- Invariants: *Frozen realms* (post-pass rows only; atlas-far#far-passes row added), *Tone* (no fail), *Budgets per
  view*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js tests/realm-glassway.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs boat
node tests/chapter.mjs ch14 && node tests/chapter.mjs ch14-chart
```

#### Out of scope

The Glassway's other finds (S-205) and fishing (S-206). Kinako's bazaar stop (S-203).

#### Risk & rollback

A post-pass, a fog override and a quest field entry. Rollback: revert; step `post` shows its own text and the north
stays clear.

<a id="s-204"></a>
### S-204 · feat: Glassway enemies: Bubble Puffer and Gullgrab
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-042, S-201b, S-179 · Size: M (about 360 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [creatures#enemy-bubble-puffer](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-bubble-puffer), [creatures#enemy-gullgrab](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-gullgrab), [creatures#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables), [atlas-far#glassway-enemies](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-enemies), [systems#boat](../bible/systems.md#boat)

#### Why
The Glassway's islets and sandbars need their own cheeky wildlife, tier 2 like the Rootdeep's: a pufferfish that keeps
its distance and blows bubbles, and a gull that snatches gems (and always gives them back).

#### What
1. **Species** (`js/realms/glassway/enemies.js`, numbers exactly):

   | id | name | archetype | hp | r | flying / alt | emerge | gems | poof | params |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `bubble_puffer` | Bubble Puffer | kiter | 5 | 0.45 | floats 1.2 m over water or sand | water | 2 | `0xbfeee6` | `{ band: [6, 10], retreat: 3.0, throwWind: 0.60, shotSpeed: 8, volley: 2, cornerT: 0.5, dizzy: 1.6, tier: 2 }` |
   | `gullgrab` | Gullgrab | diver | 4 | 0.40 | flying 2.5 m | sky | 2 | `0xf7f7f2` | `{ aim: 0.70, diveSpeed: 9, diveT: 0.45, reach: 5.0, alt: 2.5, tier: 2, snatch: 3, bonus: 2 }` |

   - Bubble Puffer: keeps 6-10 m away, retreats at 3.0 m/s; puffs its cheeks for 0.60 s (`sfx.puff`, rising) then blows
     **two** bubbles 0.4 s apart in straight lines at 8 m/s (glint shots, a sword hit pops one); retreat stays over shelf
     water or sand within 10 m of a shore (never deep water, never onto a pier); blocked by a rock, wreck, pier post or the
     shore for 0.5 s → puffs up round and dizzy 1.6 s (gold).
   - Gullgrab: flaps up and caws for 0.70 s (`sfx.caw`), dives 9 m/s; after a miss it re-aims once (a new line drawn
     0.6 s). A hit costs half a heart **and snatches 3 gems** (R35: creatures.md's 3, not INDEX's "a gem"; never more
     than Pip has; 0 gems → it squawks and flies off empty, no snatch) shown as gems in its beak. Bonked while carrying:
     it drops them **plus 2** (a gem burst). If it despawns still carrying, the gems come back at once with the toast "The
     Gullgrab dropped your gems. Rude!". Gems are never lost: a unit test sums `coins` over a scripted snatch/despawn.
2. **Rigs** (`enemyDef`, ≤ 1,200 tris): `bubble_puffer` a cream oval fish `#f5e6c4` with teal spots `#2f7d8c` (`colorFn`),
   little fins `#bfeee6`, rosy cheeks `#ff9f8f`, puffed state a sphere with soft cone spines; `gullgrab` a plump white
   gull `#f7f7f2` with grey wingtips `#5a6a7a`, yellow beak `#f2b43d` and a teal neckerchief `#2f7d8c`, glide / flap-up /
   dive poses, a 3-gem cluster in the beak when carrying.
3. **Spawn tables** (creatures.md is the definition; **R34: no "while sailing" row**: systems#boat's "nothing attacks the
   boat" wins; edit creatures.md and atlas-far's enemy tables in this PR): `puffinpost` `[]` max 0; `sandbars`
   `[['gullgrab', 3], ['crab', 2]]` max 5, **low tide only** (spawn points on sandbar or beach cells valid only while
   `tide:glassway` is 0; at high tide the region spawns only on islet tops: gullgrab only); `wreckreach`
   `[['bubble_puffer', 3], ['gullgrab', 2]]` max 5; `bellshallows` same. Filters: no spawn within 12 m of Post Rock's shore
   (town edge rule) or on Hummock's deck; Bubble Puffers spawn over shelf water within 10 m of a shore; no spawn on a pier.
   Enemy targeting skips Pip in state `boat`; an enemy whose target boards the boat gives up and wanders.
4. **Bestiary:** Bubble Puffer — Hoot "The Bubble Puffer is a fish that wanted to see the sky. It is taking it slowly." /
   Clover "Chase it into a rock. It gets dizzy!"; Gullgrab — "Gullgrabs collect shiny things to decorate their nests.
   Their taste is excellent." / "Bonk it and it drops your gems." Land chip "The Glassway".

#### Assets
- `card_bubble_puffer`, `card_gullgrab` (384² ≤ 45 KB, realm `glassway`), bestiary prompt pattern + creatures.md's
  descriptions ("a cream pufferfish with teal spots floating in the air above glass-clear shallows, cheeks puffed to blow a
  big round bubble"; "a plump cheeky white seagull in a teal neckerchief, holding a shiny gem in its beak, flying over a
  sandbar"). Fallback: canvas blob card.
- ART BRIEFs from creatures.md in the PR (poly 800/2,500 and 900/3,000; animation lists; 3D prompt pattern).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Per species in-page (`G.debug.actors.spawn`): telegraph ≥ 0.55 s before the first damage frame; every hit 1 HP;
  poof at 0 HP; bestiary counts.
- Gullgrab: a hit with 10 gems leaves 7 and shows 3 in the beak; a bonk returns 5 (3 + 2); a despawn returns 3 with the
  toast; with 0 gems nothing is taken. Sum test over 100 scripted snatches: gems never decrease net.
- Puffer: cornered against a reef head 0.5 s → dizzy 1.6 s gold; its retreat never leaves the 10 m shore band.
- No enemy spawns in Puffin Post or within 12 m of its shore, on Hummock, on piers, or (at high tide) on sandbar cells;
  no enemy targets Pip aboard.
- Unit: every move wind ≥ 0.55 s, damage 1. Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/enemies.test.js`, `bun test tests/gullgrab.test.js` (new: gem conservation).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs species` ("bubble_puffer", "gullgrab"), `node tests/inpage.mjs glassway` ("no spawns in town
  or on Hummock", "sandbar spawns only at low tide", "no target aboard").

#### Out of scope
- The Sunken Bell's and the Merry Mabel's waves (S-209-S-211, S-214), Moonmere silver variants.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: snatching feels mean; it is three gems, always returned, and the toast is playful. Rollback: revert; the tables go
back to empty.

<a id="s-205"></a>
### S-205 · feat: Glassway finds: chests, keepsakes, acorns, vistas, bottles, Lamp Logs, Hollow doors, benches and the root-gate
Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-201b, S-075, S-203, S-074 · Size: M (about 480 lines, mostly data) · Save: none · Flag: none (post-pass on the unhidden realm) · Bible: [atlas-far#glassway-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-pois), [atlas-far#glassway-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-keepsakes), [atlas-far#glassway-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-curios), [atlas-far#glassway-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-arrival), [atlas-far#glassway-coverage](../bible/atlas-far.md#glassway-coverage), [lore#bottle-table](../bible/lore.md#bottle-table), [lore#log-22](../bible/lore.md#log-22), [lore#log-23](../bible/lore.md#log-23), [lore#log-24](../bible/lore.md#log-24), [systems#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids)

#### Why
The Glassway is explored islet by islet and tide by tide. Its finds (low-tide chests on sandbars, a crow's nest, a
sloop's cabin, three more century-old bottles) give the boat a reason to go everywhere, and the coverage test proves
every land cell has something.

#### What
1. **Append to `placeFinds`** (`new RNG(40253 * 71 + 3)`, after S-203's `glassway:bottle-4`; a post-pass on the frozen
   realm: own list `W.finds`, nothing else touched, `W.walk/W.reach` restored for both tides, decks/solids via
   `attachPost`). Anchors from atlas-far, nudged ≤ 8 m to valid ground at the tide the find needs. Ids per systems#ids
   (R38):

   | Kind | Ids · anchors · gates |
   |---|---|
   | Chests (30 gems) | `glassway:c1` (6, −160) under the post office stilts, **low tide** · `c2` (−180, −125) Tern Tuft dune top among the nests · `c3` (−212, 122) Whelk's Spit tip behind driftwood · `c4` (−90, 165) Kelp Knoll tide pool, **low tide** · `c5` (−215, −48) half-buried crate mid-sandbar B2, **low tide** · `c6` (185, −135) y 8 the *Wobbly Gull*'s cabin · `c7` (160, 60) the *Biscuit Tin*'s deck, **low tide** · `c8` (120, 150) Spiral Skerry behind a **star door** (inert until S-208: by day "The stars on it are asleep."; at night "The stars on it twinkle. They seem to be waiting for a tune.") · `c9` (20, 190) **buried** by the chapel steps (Paws, `soft_earth`) · `c10` (−125, 240) the Barnacle Garden on Hummock's flank (**inert**: hidden until ss-37 rolls her, S-216) · `c11` (240, 40) y 17 the *Merry Mabel*'s crow's nest, a **hookbloom** grows from the mast top · `c12` (155, −172) y 12 a ledge across a 10 m gap from Gull Rock's spiral path (**Leaf Glider**) |
   | Keepsakes (25 gems) | `glassway:k1` stamp (0, −172) · `k2` ribbon (−240, 36) · `k3` plate (240, 30) · `k4` tassel (0, 292) on the spire's back ledge, **low tide**; names, labels and two lines each verbatim from atlas-far#glassway-keepsakes; the fifth line when all four are found |
   | Sleeping Acorns | `glassway:a1..a8`: (−20, −215), (−160, −95), (−225, 70), (−130, 130), (175, −160) Gull Rock ledge, (205, −15), (110, 160), (−20, 210) |
   | Vistas | `glassway:v1` "All of the Wide Blue" Gull Rock top (170, −150) y 22 · `v2` "The Sandbars at Low Tide" Tern Tuft dune top (−175, −120) · `v3` "The Bell in the Water" Bell Islet south shore (0, 255) |
   | Bottles (R10) | `glassway:7` (Sandy Pelliwick) Whelk's Spit tip (−205, 128) · `glassway:8` (Quill Thimble) Gull Rock's SE foot (200, −182) · `glassway:10` (Croakley) against Hummock's flank (−95, 255) (on the deck edge, reachable at both tides); letters verbatim from lore#bottle-7, #bottle-8, #bottle-10 in each writer's hand; bob, glint and `bottleBob` |
   | Lamp Logs (R11) | `glassway:22` Post Rock garden (−25, −205) · `glassway:23` Gull Rock spiral-path niche y ≈ 10 near (160, −160) · `glassway:24` Bell Islet's top chapel step (0, 175); texts verbatim from lore#log-22, #log-23, #log-24 |
   | Hollow doors | `glassway:hollow1` (−140, 178) Kelp Knoll south cave mouth (**low tide**) → `hollow_glassway_1` · `glassway:hollow2` (130, 165) Spiral Skerry ruin ring → `hollow_glassway_2`; props only, Look "A spiral door, shut tight. Something inside hums." (portals in S-220) |
   | Benches (R5) | `bench:glassway:b1` (−10, −200) "Puffin Post · Pillar Post" · `b2` (−165, −100) "The Sandbars · Tern Tuft" · `b3` (−220, 40) "The Sandbars · Whelk's Spit" · `b4` (205, 0) "Wreck Reach · Mabel Rock" · `b5` (10, 180) "Bell Shallows · Chapel Steps" (edit systems.md's bench table to 5 here) |
   | Root-gate (R37) | `glassway:gate` Spiral Skerry (110, 140), a root rising through the ruin ring, sealed `root_gate` (S-182): `[T] Dig · Root-gate` → `travel['gate:glassway']`; links to the Rootdeep's `gate-glassway` (0, 250); spawn `rootgate` 2 m south (edit systems.md's "Puffin Post islet" in this PR) |
   | Curios | `glassway:curio-1..12` verbatim from atlas-far#glassway-curios, plus `curio-13` (R11) at the Tern Tuft cairn (−170, −110) `[E] Look · Lamplighter cairn`: "Flat stones piled into a cairn, a spiral scratched on the top one. Someone counted the tides here, then went home for tea." |
   | Gate finds | Mabel mast hookbloom (c11), Gull Rock glide ledge (c12), Skerry star door (c8), Bell Islet soft earth (c9) |

2. **Gate and tide rules for the solver:** every low-tide find needs `tide:glassway == 0` reachable (the Tide Bell, an
   Act II tool, is owned; the bell's tide stones are reachable at both tides); c11 needs `hook`, c12 `glide`, c9 `dig`,
   c8 `starsong` + night (inert now: the solver reports "waits for S-208"), c10 `flag:ss37.done` ("waits for S-216").
   Pacing rule 5 report: the Paws, the Hook and the Glider each re-open ≥ 1 find here.
3. **Coverage** (`tests/coverage.test.js`, atlas-far#poi-kinds rule; R6 tide ids): cells of ≥ 200 m² walkable reachable
   land; (a) and (b) run once per tide state, (c) on the union; open-water cells not tested; expected land-cell count
   recorded in the prints fixture (atlas: 64-80). Cells most at risk: sandbar middles (c5, path cells) and Whelk's Spit's
   middle (the shop, the Stack, curio 4, bench b3).
4. **POI count** ≥ 80 (atlas budget 99 with fish spots and thornglass added later): this step asserts ≥ 85 and prints the
   total.
5. **Completion** (`tools/realm-stats.mjs` → `js/realms/stats.js`): Glassway group chests 12, keepsakes 4, acorns 8,
   vistas 3, bottles 4, Lamp Logs 3, Hollows 2, benches 5.
6. **Vista sketches** `sketch_glassway_1..3` (512 × 288 webp ≤ 45 KB, realm `glassway`, kind `sketch`): "the whole Wide
   Blue from a gull rock: a great tree on an island to the north, a steaming cone to the east, a bell tower's tip to the
   south-west" · "sandbars at low tide between little islets, gulls on the bars" · "a stone bell spire standing in clear
   shallow water, steps leading down into the sea"; palette `#2f7d8c #f5e6c4 #ffb48a #bfeee6`; fallback the canvas pencil
   frame.

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c06` Sea-glass Wind Chime, `c11` Starlight Blue trim, `c08` "Spiral Skerry" sticker. Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

#### Assets
Three vista sketches (above). Everything else uses existing kit models (bench, lectern, Hollow door, vista post, star
door, hookbloom, soft earth, bottle, root-gate knot, keepsake glint).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Post-pass test: `generateRealm('glassway')` with and without the appended `placeFinds` entries has equal prints at
  both tides (bottle 4 unchanged at (14, −258)); seeds 1-20 of the `shoals` recipe likewise.
- Every find within 8 m of its anchor; low-tide finds reachable only at low tide; c11, c12, c9 unreachable without their
  tool and reachable with it; c8 and c10 reported waiting (not failures).
- Coverage passes at both tides with no failing cell; counts recorded; POI ≥ 85.
- Texts (keepsakes, curios, bottles, logs) equal the bible by id (content lint).
- The root-gate opens with the Paws and appears in the root-gate menu and on the chart's Under tab.
- Invariants: all lines.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test tests/realms/glassway.test.js` (post-pass group, both tides), `bun test tests/coverage.test.js`,
  `bun test tests/progress-graph.test.js`, `bun test tests/stats.test.js`, `bun test tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs glassway` ("low-tide chest c5", "crow's nest by hook", "glassway root-gate opens"),
  `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa` (sketches in the bundle; bundle ≤ 2.5 MB).

#### Out of scope
- Fish spots (S-207), the star door and Calling (S-208), the Bell door (S-209), the Mabel breach (S-214), Hummock's roll
  (S-216), Hollow interiors and the Glassway Dash (S-220), thornglass (S-233), the milestone close-out (S-205b).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a low-tide find is also reachable at high tide by a hop from a pier (a free find); the solver tags it and the step
moves it within its 8 m radius. Rollback: revert; post-passes are additive and bottle 4 stays.

Milestone M23 · Depends on: S-198 … S-205 (every M23 step, splits included), S-001, S-030 · Size: S (docs and records) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible: [charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone), [charter#progress-template](../bible/charter.md#progress-template)

5. **Bench table** ([systems#benches](../bible/systems.md#benches), R5): set Starseed Isle 5, Threemills 6, The Rootdeep
   5, The Glassway 5 (on islets), add a row "The Crown | 1 (post-game)", and set the total to **56**; set the Benches
   column of [systems#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles)' land table to the same numbers (home 4 → 5,
   Threemills 5 → 6, Rootdeep 4 → 5, Glassway 6 → 5) and its total to 55 (that table has no Crown row), and the
   "Home gets 4 benches; per-land counts total 53" line to "Home gets 5 benches; per-land counts total 56".

None.

- systems.md's bench table reads 56 in total, matching the atlases (R5).

## Index changes

Step ids and order are INDEX's; these are the splits and additions this file makes (the plan job files each part
as its own issue, in this order):

| Change | Parts | Why |
|---|---|---|
| S-181 split | **S-181a** Digging Paws, the `soft_earth` gate, buried chests on the Rootdeep and the four isles · **S-181b** home's dig spots, Digby's rumours, Shellby's old hat, the Undercap hole | tool + five realms + a home pass exceeds ~500 lines |
| S-190 split | **S-190a** knots, the memory runner, memory 1 · **S-190b** memories 2-3, Remembered acorns, journal replays | runner plus three scripted memories exceeds ~500 lines |
| S-191 split | **S-191a** mq-ch13, Digby's walk, Gran, `ch13_heart` · **S-191b** `ch13-roots`, veins, root-gates on visited isles, unhide, Spud, `mq-remember`, chapter test | a chapter with a partner walk, two scenes, an unhide and a chapter test |
| S-201 split | **S-201a** post office, Pillar Post, Shell Stack, piers, buoys, tide stones, small props · **S-201b** wrecks, Gull Rock's path, the spire, Hummock | 16 models in one PR exceeds ~500 lines |
| S-190c added | **S-190c** `feat: the vignette runner, the Ladder's path lamps and vignette 1 The Lamp Path in memory 3` (deps S-190b; filed after S-190b, before S-191a). S-190b's title becomes "feat: memories 2 and 3, free roam and echoes in every memory, the Remembered acorns and journal replays" and its size S → M | memories priced at 1.5 h modelled at about 25 min watched; free roam, echoes and one vignette per group make them played (lore#memory-play, #memory-echoes, #memory-vignettes) |
| S-205b added | **S-205b** `chore: close M23` | INDEX has no M23 close-out; the charter requires one per milestone |
| S-203b added (review) | **S-203b** Fog on the Water, the Glassway's set piece: a `before` sub-objective of ch14's step `post` (depends S-203, S-201a, S-091b); S-205b depends on it | story.md#set-pieces: one main-path set piece per land |
| Dependencies added | S-178 also needs S-174 (schedules); S-180 before S-181a (c9 follows `placeFinds`); S-183 needs S-181a and S-160; S-186 also needs S-183 and S-181a; S-190a needs S-189 and S-185; S-191a needs S-178; S-191b also needs S-190c (its chapter test plays memory 3's vignette through `scene.skip`); S-203 needs S-202; S-205 needs S-203 (bottle 4 is `placeFinds`' first entry); S-199 also needs S-071 (every save bump depends on the previous bump; INDEX §3) | ordering the content each step reads |
| Unhide moved | `remembered` is unhidden with the Rootdeep in **S-191b** (systems.md said S-190) (R51) | nothing reachable half-built |
| New quest id | `mq-remember` (n 23) ships in S-191b | story decision 19 |

INDEX scope lines that the bible overrides here (no renumbering; recorded so the index can be regenerated with
`python3 step-index-src.py body`): S-200 "9 islets by Poisson discs" → fixed authored islets (R32); S-204 "Gullgrab
snatches a gem" → 3 gems (R35); S-186's title gains "and the Rootdeep Dash" (R24).

**Added by the 2026-11-22 depth review** (CANON §11.1 A2/A3; dungeons.md R14 and R15). **S-186b** (added after S-186; S-187 depends on it): the Rootdeep's Hollows get a second stage; the **Dark Hollow (H15)** becomes the Deep Hollow (dungeons.md §7; the review named H15). **S-184b** (added after S-184; S-185 and S-187 depend on it): the Rootdeep Heart grows two annex rooms (r15-r16; the Sap Valve shortens the roots) and the Warden's latch, as dungeons.md §5A.8 draws them, with two-wave combat rooms. **Scope changed (creatures review):** S-185 gives Lumbra 128 HP, a second escalation at 33 % with the `holes` arena change and the 120-180 s window; if it passes ~500 lines, the P3 change, balance test and rematch ship as **S-185b** (INDEX §1.6).
